[HPT_SRC_TARGET] PointTheoremBaseObject and HidaPropsAlt
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/ModularityArgument/ThresholdArguments/DenseThresholdCases/PointTheoremBaseObject.lean#L26-L39
def PointTheoremBaseObject : Prop :=
  ∀ (x : B.Pt) (h : s.Wt n) (ρ : G L Ω →* GL (Fin n) (Qbar l)), 2 ≤ n → n ≤ 4 → x.Arith h →
    x.IsRep ρ → Continuous ρ → _root_.UpperTheory.V.Z.Win.IsAutCsdA ι ρ → (∀ g ∈ Ine (F := L) (s.A s.v0), ρ g = 1) →
    (∀ v : Vl l L, HasFlag (ρ.comp (Dec (F := L) (s.A v)).subtype) (x.psi v) ∧
      ∀ i j, x.psi v i ≠ x.psi v j * (cycBar L Ω l).comp (Dec (F := L) (s.A v)).subtype) →
    J ≤ RingHom.ker x
structure HidaPropsAlt (Λ : LamObj r) : Prop where
  ring : B.RingProps Λ J
  dense : B.Dense J
  pt : B.PointTheoremBaseObject ι J
  end1 : B.arithmeticPointPurityDense ι J
  end2 : B.arithmeticPointClassicalityDense ι J
  twist : B.TwistOk J
structure ModularAlt (Λ : LamObj r) : Prop extends toHidaProps : B.HidaPropsAlt ι J Λ where

    J ≤ RingHom.ker x :=

end

private theorem norm_one {Γ K : Type} [Group Γ] [TopologicalSpace Γ] [CompactSpace Γ] [NormedField K]
    (ψ : Γ →* Kˣ) (hψ : Continuous fun s => (ψ s : K)) (s : Γ) : ‖(ψ s : K)‖ = 1 := by
  have key : ∀ t, ‖(ψ t : K)‖ ≤ 1 := fun t => by
    obtain ⟨C, hC⟩ := (isCompact_range hψ.norm).bddAbove
    refine not_lt.1 fun hlt => ?_
    obtain ⟨m, hm⟩ := pow_unbounded_of_one_lt C hlt
    have h1 := hC ⟨t ^ m, rfl⟩
    simp only [map_pow, Units.val_pow_eq_pow_val, norm_pow] at h1
    exact absurd hm (not_lt.2 h1)
  refine le_antisymm (key s) ?_
  have h2 := key s⁻¹
  rw [map_inv, Units.val_inv_eq_inv_val, norm_inv] at h2
  exact (inv_le_one₀ (norm_pos_iff.2 (ψ s).ne_zero)).1 h2

variable {L Ω : Type} [Field L] [NumberField L] [Field Ω] [Algebra L Ω] [IsAlgClosure L Ω] {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime]
  {w : Plc L} {A : ValuationSubring Ω} {h : IsPlaceOver A w} {a : Weil h →* (w.adicCompletion L)ˣ}
private theorem algPow_add (d e : (L →+* Qbar l) → ℤ) (x : (w.adicCompletion L)ˣ) :
    algPow w (d + e) (x : w.adicCompletion L) = algPow w d (x : w.adicCompletion L) * algPow w e (x : w.adicCompletion L) := by
  haveI := GlobalTheory.HeckeGal.finite_contEmb (l := l) w
  unfold algPow
  rw [← finprod_mul_distrib (Set.toFinite _) (Set.toFinite _)]
  exact finprod_congr fun j => by rw [Pi.add_apply, zpow_add₀ ((map_ne_zero j.1).2 x.ne_zero)]
private theorem sum_zero [Fintype (W.Emb l w)] (ha : IsLocalArtin h a) (d : (L →+* Qbar l) → ℤ)
    (χ : Dec (F := L) A →* (Qbar l)ˣ) (hχ : Continuous χ)
    (hv : ∀ s : Weil h, (χ (Subgroup.inclusion (Weil_le_Dec h) s) : Qbar l) = algPow w d (a s : w.adicCompletion L)) :
    ∑ j : W.Emb l w, d (j.1.comp (algebraMap L _)) = 0 := by
  haveI : CompactSpace (Dec (F := L) A) := isCompact_iff_compactSpace.1 (isClosed_Dec A).isCompact
  haveI : CharZero (w.adicCompletion L) := algebraRat.charZero _
  have hl : (l : Qbar l) ≠ 0 := Nat.cast_ne_zero.2 (Fact.out : l.Prime).ne_zero
  obtain ⟨s, hs⟩ := ha.surj (Units.mk0 (l : w.adicCompletion L) (Nat.cast_ne_zero.2 (Fact.out : l.Prime).ne_zero))
  have h1 := norm_one χ (Units.continuous_val.comp hχ) (Subgroup.inclusion (Weil_le_Dec h) s)
  rw [hv, hs, algPow, finprod_eq_prod_of_fintype] at h1
  simp only [Units.val_mk0, map_natCast] at h1
  have hp : ∀ (T : Finset (W.Emb l w)) (c : W.Emb l w → ℤ), ∏ j ∈ T, (l : Qbar l) ^ c j = (l : Qbar l) ^ ∑ j ∈ T, c j :=
    fun T c => T.cons_induction (by simp) fun j T hj ih => by rw [Finset.prod_cons, Finset.sum_cons, ih, zpow_add₀ hl]
  rw [hp, norm_zpow] at h1
  exact (zpow_eq_one_iff_right₀ (norm_nonneg _) (GlobalTheory.PlaceBridge.norm_l_lt_one (l := l)).ne).1 h1
private theorem not_linked (H : GlobalTheory.Published.GlobalArtinReciprocity) (ι : Qbar l ≃+* ℂ) {n : ℕ} (hw : ((l : ℕ) : 𝓞 L) ∈ w.asIdeal)
    (ha : IsLocalArtin h a) {hh : Fin n → (L →+* Qbar l) → ℤ} (hm : ∀ τ, StrictMono fun j => hh j τ)
    {ω : Fin n → (w.adicCompletion L)ˣ →* ℂˣ} {ψ : Fin n → Dec (F := L) A →* (Qbar l)ˣ}
    (hO : ∀ j, OrdChar ι a (ω j) (hh j) (ψ j))
    (hne : ∀ i j, ψ i ≠ ψ j * (cycBar L Ω l).comp (Dec (F := L) A).subtype) (i j : Fin n) (hij : i ≠ j) :
    ω i ≠ ω j ∧ ω i ≠ ω j * absPow (w.adicCompletion L) 1 := by
  haveI := GlobalTheory.HeckeGal.finite_contEmb (l := l) w
  haveI := Fintype.ofFinite (W.Emb l w)
  let inc := Subgroup.inclusion (Weil_le_Dec h)
  let ε := (cycBar L Ω l).comp (Dec (F := L) A).subtype
  have hεc : Continuous ε := ((Continuous.units_map ((algebraMap ℚ_[l] (Qbar l)).comp PadicInt.Coe.ringHom).toMonoidHom
    ((continuous_algebraMap ℚ_[l] (Qbar l)).comp continuous_subtype_val)).comp (continuous_cycZ L Ω l)).comp continuous_subtype_val
  have hε : ∀ s : Weil h, (ε (inc s) : Qbar l) = ((normV w : ℕ) : Qbar l) ^ deg s * algPow w 1 (a s : w.adicCompletion L) :=
    UpperTheory.V.Loc.epsArt H h hw a ha
  have hv : ∀ k (s : Weil h), (ψ k (inc s) : Qbar l) = ι.symm (ω k (a s)) * algPow w (-hh k) (a s : w.adicCompletion L) :=
    fun k s => (hO k).val s _ rfl (Set.mem_univ _)
  have hN : Nonempty (W.Emb l w) := by
    by_contra hE
    haveI : IsEmpty (W.Emb l w) := not_nonempty_iff.1 hE
    haveI : CompactSpace (Dec (F := L) A) := isCompact_iff_compactSpace.1 (isClosed_Dec A).isCompact
    have h1 := norm_one ε (Units.continuous_val.comp hεc) (inc (frob h))
    rw [hε, deg_frob, zpow_one, algPow, finprod_of_isEmpty, mul_one, GlobalTheory.Pst.normV_eq_pow (l := l) hw, Nat.cast_pow, norm_pow] at h1
    have h2 := (pow_eq_one_iff_of_nonneg (norm_nonneg _) (fun h0 => by
      have := one_lt_normV w
      rw [GlobalTheory.Pst.normV_eq_pow (l := l) hw, h0] at this
      exact lt_irrefl _ this)).1 h1
    exact (GlobalTheory.PlaceBridge.norm_l_lt_one (l := l)).ne h2
  have hneg : ∀ d : W.Emb l w → ℤ, (∀ t, d t < 0) → ∑ t, d t ≠ 0 := fun d hd =>
    (Finset.sum_neg (fun t _ => hd t) Finset.univ_nonempty).ne
  constructor
  · intro e
    have h1 := sum_zero ha (hh j - hh i) (ψ i * (ψ j)⁻¹) ((hO i).cont.mul (hO j).cont.inv) fun s => by
      rw [MonoidHom.mul_apply, MonoidHom.inv_apply, Units.val_mul, Units.mul_inv_eq_iff_eq_mul, hv, hv, e, mul_left_comm,
        ← algPow_add]
      congr 2
      ring
    rcases lt_or_gt_of_ne hij with hlt | hlt
    · exact (Finset.sum_pos (fun t _ => sub_pos.2 (hm _ hlt)) Finset.univ_nonempty).ne' h1
    · exact hneg _ (fun t => sub_neg.2 (hm _ hlt)) h1
  · intro e
    have hc : Continuous (ψ i * (ψ j * ε)⁻¹) := (hO i).cont.mul ((hO j).cont.mul hεc).inv
    have hab : ∀ s : Weil h, ι.symm (absPow (w.adicCompletion L) 1 (a s)) = ((normV w : ℕ) : Qbar l) ^ deg s := fun s => by
      have := DFunLike.congr_fun (ha.absPow_comp 1) s
      rw [Complex.ofReal_one] at this
      rw [← MonoidHom.comp_apply, this, GlobalTheory.WeilDeligne.unrChar_apply, Units.val_zpow_eq_zpow_val, map_zpow₀]
      simp
    have hx : ∀ s : Weil h, (((ψ i * (ψ j * ε)⁻¹) (inc s) : (Qbar l)ˣ) : Qbar l) =
        algPow w (hh j - hh i - 1) (a s : w.adicCompletion L) := fun s => by
      rw [MonoidHom.mul_apply, MonoidHom.inv_apply, Units.val_mul, Units.mul_inv_eq_iff_eq_mul, MonoidHom.mul_apply,
        Units.val_mul, hv, hv, hε, e, MonoidHom.mul_apply, Units.val_mul, map_mul, hab,
        show -hh i = (hh j - hh i - 1) + (-hh j + 1) by ring, algPow_add, algPow_add]
      ring
    have h1 := sum_zero ha _ _ hc hx
    rcases lt_or_gt_of_ne hij with hlt | hlt
    · have h2 := (Finset.sum_eq_zero_iff_of_nonneg fun t _ => by
        have := hm (t.1.comp (algebraMap L _)) hlt
        simp only [Pi.sub_apply, Pi.one_apply] at this ⊢
        omega).1 h1
      refine hne i j (mul_inv_eq_one.1 (MonoidHom.ext fun g => Units.ext ?_))
      refine congrFun ((UpperTheory.weil_group_dense_in_galois_group L Ω w A h).equalizer (Units.continuous_val.comp hc) continuous_const
        (funext fun s => ?_)) g
      refine (hx s).trans (finprod_eq_one_of_forall_eq_one fun t => ?_)
      rw [h2 t (Finset.mem_univ _), zpow_zero]
    · refine hneg _ (fun t => ?_) h1
      have := hm (t.1.comp (algebraMap L _)) hlt
      simp only [Pi.sub_apply, Pi.one_apply] at this ⊢
      omega
end
private theorem dual_of_weights {n : ℕ} (μ ν : DomWeight n)
    (h : (Finset.univ.val.map fun j : Fin n => μ.w j.rev + (j : ℕ)).map (fun k : ℤ => (k : ℂ)) =
      (infOfWeight ν).map fun z => ((n : ℂ) - 1) / 2 - z) : ν = μ.dual := by
  have h1 : (Finset.univ.val.map fun j : Fin n => μ.w j.rev + (j : ℕ)) =
      Finset.univ.val.map fun j : Fin n => -ν.w j + (j : ℕ) := by
    refine Multiset.map_injective (Int.cast_injective (α := ℂ)) (h.trans ?_)
    rw [infOfWeight, Multiset.map_map, Multiset.map_map]
    exact Multiset.map_congr rfl fun j _ => by simp only [Function.comp_apply]; push_cast; ring
  have m1 : StrictMono fun j : Fin n => μ.w j.rev + (j : ℕ) := fun i j hij =>
    add_lt_add_of_le_of_lt (μ.anti (Fin.rev_le_rev.2 hij.le)) (by exact_mod_cast hij)
  have m2 : StrictMono fun j : Fin n => -ν.w j + (j : ℕ) := fun i j hij =>
    add_lt_add_of_le_of_lt (neg_le_neg (ν.anti hij.le)) (by exact_mod_cast hij)
  have h2 := (m1.range_inj m2).1 (Set.ext fun z => by simpa using (congrArg (z ∈ ·) h1).to_iff)
  obtain ⟨u, hu⟩ := ν
  have h3 : u = μ.dual.w := funext fun j => by
    have := congrFun h2 j
    simp only [DomWeight.dual] at this ⊢
    omega
  subst h3
  rfl
section
variable {L Ω : Type} [Field L] [NumberField L] [Field Ω] [Algebra L Ω] [IsAlgClosure L Ω] {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime]
  {w : Plc L} {A : ValuationSubring Ω} {ι : Qbar l ≃+* ℂ} {n : ℕ}
private theorem emb_place (j : W.Emb l w) (x : 𝓞 L) : ‖j.1.comp (algebraMap L _) (x : L)‖ < 1 ↔ x ∈ w.asIdeal := by
  have h1 : w.asIdeal ≤ (GlobalTheory.EvExtend.placeOf (j.1.comp (algebraMap L _))).asIdeal := fun y hy => by
    rw [GlobalTheory.EvExtend.mem_placeOf, ← tendsto_pow_atTop_nhds_zero_iff_norm_lt_one]
    have h2 : Valued.v (algebraMap L (w.adicCompletion L) (y : L)) < 1 := by
      rw [show Valued.v (algebraMap L (w.adicCompletion L) (y : L)) = w.valuation L (algebraMap (𝓞 L) L y) from
        HeightOneSpectrum.valuedAdicCompletion_eq_valuation' w _]
      exact (HeightOneSpectrum.valuation_lt_one_iff_mem w _).2 hy
    have h3 := (j.2.tendsto 0).comp (Valued.tendsto_zero_pow_of_v_lt_one h2)
    simpa [Function.comp_def] using h3
  rw [w.isMaximal.eq_of_le (GlobalTheory.EvExtend.placeOf _).isPrime.ne_top h1, GlobalTheory.EvExtend.mem_placeOf]
private theorem jumps_eq (h : IsPlaceOver A w) (hw : ((l : ℕ) : 𝓞 L) ∈ w.asIdeal) {r : Dec (F := L) A →* GL (Fin n) (Qbar l)}
    {J J' : (L →+* Qbar l) → Multiset ℤ} {P P' : Weil h → ℂ[X]} (H : DRat ι h r J P) (H' : DRat ι h r J' P')
    (j : W.Emb l w) : J (j.1.comp (algebraMap L _)) = J' (j.1.comp (algebraMap L _)) := by
  obtain ⟨O, _, _, _, Bw, hj⟩ := Af.exists_bridge (l := l) h hw
  obtain ⟨τ', hτ'⟩ := GlobalTheory.CharAboveRead.exists_isInduced_of _ w hw (emb_place j) A h O Bw hj
  obtain ⟨LL, r', hM, -, τ₁, hτ₁, hc⟩ := (H O Bw hj).2.1 _ _ _ hτ'
  ext k
  exact_mod_cast (hc k).trans (GlobalTheory.Pst.IsJumpsAt.count_eq _ hM hτ₁ ((H' O Bw hj).2.1 _ _ _ hτ') k).symm
private theorem flag_cont {Γ : Type} [Group Γ] [TopologicalSpace Γ] [IsTopologicalGroup Γ] {r : Γ →* GL (Fin n) (Qbar l)}
    (hr : Continuous r) {ψ : Fin n → Γ →* (Qbar l)ˣ} (hf : W.HasFlag r ψ) (i : Fin n) : Continuous (ψ i) := by
  obtain ⟨g, hg⟩ := hf
  have h1 : Continuous fun s => (ψ i s : Qbar l) :=
    ((Units.continuous_val.comp ((continuous_const.mul hr).mul continuous_const)).matrix_elem i i).congr fun s => (hg s).2 i
  exact Units.continuous_iff.2 ⟨h1, (h1.comp continuous_inv).congr fun s => by simp⟩
private theorem above [IsCMField L] (P : _root_.UpperTheory.V.Af.Hypotheses') (hPs : _root_.UpperTheory.V.Z.Desc.W.PseudoLocalDescent) (hl : l ≠ 2) {ρ : G L Ω →* GL (Fin n) (Qbar l)}
    {HT : (L →+* Qbar l) → Multiset ℤ} (hρ : Continuous ρ) (HG : HasHTAt ρ HT w) (h : IsPlaceOver A w)
    (hw : ((l : ℕ) : 𝓞 L) ∈ w.asIdeal) {τ : SmIrr (GLw n w)} (H : LocGlobT ι ρ w τ) {rc : IrrGL (w.adicCompletion L) → WD h}
    (hrc : IsRecT h rc) (hp : GlobalTheory.RoadU.PlainTor (IrrGL.of τ.ρ τ.smooth τ.irred)) (hg : Gd (rc (algNorm τ)))
    (lam : Wt L l n) {ψ : Fin n → Dec (F := L) A →* (Qbar l)ˣ}
    (hψ : ∀ i, W.IsLocAlg h (ψ i) fun t => htW lam i (t.1.comp (algebraMap L _)))
    (hf : W.HasFlag (ρ.comp (Dec (F := L) A).subtype) ψ)
    (hne : ∀ i j, ψ i ≠ ψ j * (cycBar L Ω l).comp (Dec (F := L) A).subtype) :
    (∃ ω : Fin n → (w.adicCompletion L)ˣ →* ℂˣ, Nonempty (τ.ρ.Equiv (PS ω)) ∧
      ∀ i, OrdChar ι (artOf h) (ω i) (htW lam i) (ψ i)) ∧
    ∀ j : W.Emb l w, HT (j.1.comp (algebraMap L _)) = Finset.univ.val.map fun i => htW lam i (j.1.comp (algebraMap L _)) := by
  have ha : IsLocalArtin h (artOf h) := by
    obtain ⟨a, ha, -⟩ := hrc
    exact Classical.epsilon_spec ⟨a, ha⟩
  have hc : Continuous (ρ.comp (Dec (F := L) A).subtype) := hρ.comp continuous_subtype_val
  choose ω hω using fun i => (Loc.ordCharIff P.litN.aut.art h ι _ ha (htW lam i) (ψ i)).1.2 ⟨flag_cont hc hf i, hψ i⟩
  have hD : DRat ι h (ρ.comp (Dec (F := L) A).subtype) HT fun s => (rc (algNorm τ)).charPol s := fun O _ _ _ Bw hj =>
    have h1 := HG A h O Bw hj
    ⟨h1.1, h1.2, fun s => by rw [((H A h rc hrc).2 O Bw hj hg).2]⟩
  have hD' := _root_.UpperTheory.V.Loc.flag_weil_deligne_of_literal_automorphy_n P.litN L Ω l hl ι n w hw A h _ ha _ hc _ ω ψ hω hf _ _ hD
  refine ⟨⟨ω, ?_, hω⟩, jumps_eq h hw hD hD'⟩
  have hs : IsSubquotient τ.ρ (PS ω) := by
    obtain ⟨O, _, _, _, Bw, hj⟩ := Af.exists_bridge (l := l) h hw
    exact hPs P.litN L Ω n w A h _ ha rc hrc τ hp ω (fun i => (hω i).smooth) fun s =>
      ((hD O Bw hj).2.2 s).symm.trans ((hD' O Bw hj).2.2 s)
  refine (_root_.UpperTheory.V.Hi.prin_series_irreducible_and_unique_subquotient P.litN _ n ω (fun i => (hω i).smooth) (not_linked P.litN.aut.art ι hw ha (fun t i j hij => ?_) hω hne)).2
    _ τ.ρ τ.irred hs
  exact add_lt_add_of_le_of_lt ((lam t).anti (Fin.rev_le_rev.2 hij.le)) (by exact_mod_cast hij)
private theorem unram_of_fixed [IsCMField L] {σ : Submodule ℂ (UA L n → ℂ)} {τ : SmIrr (GLw n w)}
    (hτ : IsCompAlong σ (locU n w) ((cLoc w).comp (locU n w).subtype) τ.ρ)
    (hs : Function.Surjective ((cLoc w).comp (locU n w).subtype)) (hf : Af.HasFixed τ.ρ (GlobalTheory.LFun.KGL w n)) : IsUnramU σ w := by
  obtain ⟨hi, f, f0, fm, fe⟩ := hτ
  obtain ⟨z, hz, hf⟩ := hf
  refine ⟨f z, fm z, fun h0 => ?_, fun x hx hk => ?_⟩
  · let K : Subrepresentation τ.ρ := ⟨LinearMap.ker f, fun g v hv => by
      obtain ⟨x, rfl⟩ := hs g
      rw [LinearMap.mem_ker] at hv ⊢
      rw [fe, hv, map_zero]⟩
    rcases hi.eq_bot_or_eq_top K with hK | hK
    · have h1 : z ∈ K.toSubmodule := h0
      rw [hK] at h1
      exact hz h1
    · refine f0 (LinearMap.ext fun v => ?_)
      have h1 : v ∈ K.toSubmodule := by rw [hK]; trivial
      exact h1
  · have := fe ⟨x, hx⟩ z
    rw [← this]
    exact congrArg f (hf _ hk)
end
private theorem chi_comap {n : ℕ} {A Γ H : Type} [CommRing A] [Group Γ] [Group H] (D : W.GDet n A Γ) (φ : H →* Γ) (g : H) :
    (D.comap φ).chi (MonoidAlgebra.single g 1) = D.chi (MonoidAlgebra.single (φ g) 1) := by
  change D.D A[X] (MonoidAlgebra.mapDomainRingHom A[X] φ _) = D.D A[X] _
  rw [map_sub]
  simp
private theorem ker_le {n : ℕ} {k Γ H : Type} [Field k] [CharZero k] [Group Γ] [Group H] {D : W.GDet n k Γ} {φ : H →* Γ}
    {ρ : H →* GL (Fin n) k} (hn : 1 ≤ n) (e : D.comap φ = W.GDet.ofRep (W.mat ρ)) (hs : Formalism.MainNotions.N.IsSSRep ρ) {g : H}
    (hg : φ g = 1) : ρ g = 1 := by
  haveI : Nonempty (Fin n) := ⟨⟨0, hn⟩⟩
  refine UpperTheory.eq_one_of_trace_translation_invariant_semisimple H k n ρ hs g fun y => ?_
  have h1 : ∀ z, (ρ z : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) k).charpoly = D.chi (MonoidAlgebra.single (φ z) 1) := fun z =>
    ((AlgebraTools.Det.repApi (W.mat ρ)).2.1 z).symm.trans (by rw [← e, chi_comap])
  rw [Matrix.trace_eq_neg_charpoly_coeff, Matrix.trace_eq_neg_charpoly_coeff, h1, h1, map_mul, hg, mul_one]
theorem UpperTheory.V.Z.Desc.W.isRecT_of_isRecT (_P : _root_.UpperTheory.V.Af.Hypotheses') {L Ω : Type} [Field L] [NumberField L] [Field Ω] [Algebra L Ω] [IsAlgClosure L Ω]
    {w : Plc L} {A : ValuationSubring Ω} {h : IsPlaceOver A w} {rc : IrrGL (w.adicCompletion L) → WD h} (k : IsRecT h rc) : IsRecT h rc := k
theorem UpperTheory.V.Z.Desc.W.jPT_of_alt :
    RecTExists → CompatT → PlainRec → Same → _root_.UpperTheory.V.Z.Desc.W.SphTAlt → _root_.UpperTheory.V.Z.Desc.W.PseudoLocalDescent → UnlOf → _root_.UpperTheory.V.Af.HypothesesH' → Z.Desc.DescWeak → ∀ (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (L Ω : Type) [Field L] [NumberField L] [IsCMField L] [Field Ω] [Algebra L Ω] [IsAlgClosure L Ω] (s : HidaSetting l L Ω) (n : ℕ), 1 ≤ n →
    ∀ (V : s.Lev) (Q : QData V) (c : Coef l), ∀ (r : Resid V.frame c.O n) (ι : Qbar l ≃+* ℂ), ∀ (B : UniversalOrdinaryDeterminantRing r) (Λ : LamObj r) (R : _root_.UpperTheory.V.Hi.CaseU.UnitaryHeckeData Q ι Λ) (K : (hset Q R.τ₀).Lev), V.S' = s.S → ∀ J : Ideal B.B, R.IsJ B K J →
    ∀ (x : B.Pt) (h : s.Wt n) (ρ : G L Ω →* GL (Fin n) (Qbar l)), 2 ≤ n → n ≤ 4 → x.Arith h →
    x.IsRep ρ → Continuous ρ → (AutCsd0 ι ρ ∧ (Continuous ρ → WitA ι ρ)) → (∀ g ∈ Ine (F := L) (s.A s.v0), ρ g = 1) →
    (∀ v : Vl l L, W.HasFlag (ρ.comp (Dec (F := L) (s.A v)).subtype) (x.psi v) ∧
      ∀ i j, x.psi v i ≠ x.psi v j * (cycBar L Ω l).comp (Dec (F := L) (s.A v)).subtype) →
    J ≤ RingHom.ker x := by
  intro hEx hCo hPl hSame hSph hPs hUn PH hD l _ L Ω _ _ _ _ _ _ s n hn V Q c r ι B Λ R K hS J hJ x h ρ hn2 hn4 hx hρ hc ⟨⟨π, hreg, hd, hatt⟩, hW⟩ h0 hfl
  have P := PH.toClassicalTheorems
  have hE := UpperTheory.isUnramifiedAt_totallyReal_of_inertia_le_restriction_range L Ω s.unr
  obtain ⟨lam, hlam⟩ := (_root_.UpperTheory.V.Hi.arith_good_iff_hset_of_theta_incr_weight P.litN l L Ω s n hn V Q c R.m R.τ₀ r B Λ R.θ R.theta h hx.1).1
  have hpol : ∃ χ, GlobalTheory.ContraPair.PolarizedPair π χ := by
    refine ⟨quadChar L ^ n, fun v => ?_, ?_⟩
    · have e : (quadChar L ^ n).locInf v (-1) = (quadChar L).locInf v (-1) ^ n := rfl
      rw [e, quadChar_locInf]
    · rw [map_pow, compNorm_quadChar, one_pow]
      exact (GlobalTheory.ContraPair.dualIsConjBy_one π).2 hd
  obtain ⟨HT, hwt, hLG⟩ := hCo L Ω l ι n hn ρ π hc hreg hpol hatt
  have heq : ∀ (w : Plc L), ((l : ℕ) : 𝓞 L) ∉ w.asIdeal → ∀ (A : ValuationSubring Ω) (hA : IsPlaceOver A w) rc, IsRecT hA rc →
      ∀ τ, π.IsLocComp w τ → IsWDOf ι ρ (rc (algNorm τ)) := fun w hw A hA rc hrc τ hτ =>
    (((hLG w τ hτ).1 A hA rc hrc).1 hw).isWDOf (guard_of hPl hreg hatt (hW hc) (Or.inl hw) hrc hτ).2
  have hsph : ∀ (w : Plc L), ((l : ℕ) : 𝓞 L) ∉ w.asIdeal → ∀ (A : ValuationSubring Ω), IsPlaceOver A w →
      (∀ g ∈ Ine (F := L) A, ρ g = 1) → ∀ τ, π.IsLocComp w τ → Af.HasFixed τ.ρ (GlobalTheory.LFun.KGL w n) := fun w hw A hA hI τ hτ => by
    obtain ⟨rc, hrc⟩ := hEx L Ω A w hA
    exact hSph P.litN L Ω l ι n ρ hc w hw A hA hI rc hrc τ (guard_of hPl hreg hatt (hW hc) (Or.inl hw) hrc hτ).1
      (heq w hw A hA rc hrc τ hτ)
  have hund : ∀ {A : ValuationSubring Ω} {w : Plc L}, IsPlaceOver A w → IsPlaceOver A (w.under (𝓞 (Lp L))) :=
    fun hA y => hA (algebraMap (𝓞 (Lp L)) (𝓞 L) y)
  have hwv : ∀ v, (s.w v).under (𝓞 (Lp L)) = v := fun v =>
    HeightOneSpectrum.ext (Ideal.ext fun y => (hund (s.hw v) y).symm.trans (s.hA v y))
  have hl : ∀ w : Plc L, ((l : ℕ) : 𝓞 L) ∈ w.asIdeal ↔ ((l : ℕ) : 𝓞 (Lp L)) ∈ (w.under (𝓞 (Lp L))).asIdeal := fun w => by
    rw [← map_natCast (algebraMap (𝓞 (Lp L)) (𝓞 L))]
    rfl
  have hsp : ∀ w : Plc L, w.under (𝓞 (Lp L)) ∈ V.S' → IsSplitAt w := fun w hw =>
    UpperTheory.isSplitAt_of_decomposition_le_restriction_range L Ω _ _ (s.hA _) (V.split _ hw) w rfl
  have hin : ∀ w : Plc L, ((l : ℕ) : 𝓞 L) ∈ w.asIdeal → w.under (𝓞 (Lp L)) ∈ V.S' := fun w hw =>
    V.sub (Or.inl ((hl w).1 hw))
  have hI : ∀ w : Plc L, w.under (𝓞 (Lp L)) ∉ V.S' → ∀ A, IsPlaceOver A w → ∀ g ∈ Ine (F := L) A, ρ g = 1 := fun w hw A hA g hg => by
    refine ker_le hn hρ hatt.1 (Subtype.ext ((QuotientGroup.eq_one_iff _).2 ?_))
    refine Subgroup.le_topologicalClosure _ (Subgroup.subset_normalClosure ?_)
    simp only [Set.mem_iUnion]
    exact ⟨_, hw, A, hund hA, hg⟩
  have hlv : ∀ v : Vl l L, ((l : ℕ) : 𝓞 L) ∈ (s.w v).asIdeal := fun v => by
    rw [hl, hwv]
    exact v.2
  have hψ : ∀ (v : Vl l L) i, W.IsLocAlg (s.hw v) (x.psi v i) fun t => htW lam i (t.1.comp (algebraMap L _)) :=
    fun v i => (funext (hlam v i)).symm ▸ hx.2 v i
  obtain ⟨σ, hσ, hunr, hsat⟩ := hD L hE l Fact.out V.S' V.fin hsp hin n hn2 hn4 π hreg hd
    fun w hw => by
      obtain ⟨A, hA⟩ : ∃ A : ValuationSubring Ω, IsPlaceOver A w := exists_isPlaceOver Ω w
      obtain ⟨⟨τ, hτ⟩, hi⟩ := _root_.UpperTheory.V.Af.local_comp_complex_of_printed_results P L n π w
      obtain ⟨b, hb⟩ := (_root_.UpperTheory.V.Af.satake_local_of_printed_results P L n w τ).1 (hsph w (mt (hin w) hw) A hA (hI w hw A hA) τ hτ)
      exact ⟨b, ((_root_.UpperTheory.V.Af.satake_local_of_printed_results P L n w τ).2 _ π.finPart (hi τ hτ) b).2 hb⟩
  obtain ⟨hloc, hinf⟩ := hSame L Ω hE l ι n hn π ρ hc hatt σ hσ hsat
  choose τ₀ hτ₀ using fun w => (_root_.UpperTheory.V.Af.local_comp_complex_of_printed_results P L n π w).1
  choose rcl hrcl using fun v : Vl l L => hEx L Ω _ _ (s.hw v)
  have hg := fun v : Vl l L => guard_of hPl hreg hatt (hW hc)
    (Or.inr (Or.inl (hUn L Ω l n ρ _ _ (s.hw v) _ (hfl v).1 (hfl v).2))) (hrcl v) (hτ₀ (s.w v))
  have hab := fun v : Vl l L => above P hPs s.odd hc ((hLG _ _ (hτ₀ _)).2 (hg v).1) (s.hw v) (hlv v) (hLG _ _ (hτ₀ _)).1 (hrcl v)
    (hg v).1 (hg v).2 lam (hψ v) (hfl v).1 (hfl v).2
  have hcl : ∀ w, IsSplitAt w → ∀ τ : SmIrr (GLw n w), IsCompAlong σ (locU n w) ((cLoc w).comp (locU n w).subtype) τ.ρ →
      algNorm τ = algNorm (τ₀ w) := fun w hw τ hτ => by
    unfold algNorm
    rw [IrrGL.of_eq_of.2 (UpperTheory.building_component_uniqueness L n σ hσ w hw _ _ _ _ hτ (hloc w hw _ (hτ₀ w)))]
  refine _root_.UpperTheory.V.Z.Desc.W.upU_w_alt PH l L Ω s n hn V Q c r ι B Λ R K hS J hJ x h lam hx hlam σ ρ hσ hc hatt.1 hρ (fun w hs hw τ b hτ hb => ?_)
    (fun y => ?_) (fun w hw => ?_) ?_ (fun v => ?_) fun u τ hu hτ rc hrc => ?_
  · refine _root_.UpperTheory.V.Z.Desc.W.satFrobT_alt P L Ω l ι n ρ hc w hw τ b hb fun A hA rc hrc => ?_
    rw [hcl w hs τ hτ]
    exact ((hLG w _ (hτ₀ w)).1 A hA rc hrc).1 hw
  · obtain ⟨ν, h1, h2⟩ := hinf (ι.toRingHom.comp y.res)
    have h3 := hwt y.res
    rw [CG.HasInfChar.infChar_eq (π := CG.mk π) h2, show HT y.res = _ from (hab y.1).2 y.2] at h3
    rwa [← dual_of_weights (lam y.res) ν h3]
  · by_cases hs : IsSplitAt w
    · obtain ⟨A, hA⟩ : ∃ A : ValuationSubring Ω, IsPlaceOver A w := exists_isPlaceOver Ω w
      refine unram_of_fixed (hloc w hs _ (hτ₀ w)) ?_ (hsph w (mt (hin w) hw) A hA (hI w hw A hA) _ (hτ₀ w))
      obtain ⟨e, he⟩ := GlobalTheory.locU_equiv_GL L n w hs
      exact fun g => ⟨e.symm g, (he _).symm.trans (e.apply_symm_apply g)⟩
    · exact hunr w hs
  · have hp : ((l : ℕ) : 𝓞 L) ∉ (s.w s.v0).asIdeal := fun hm => by
      have h1 := (hl _).1 hm
      rw [hwv] at h1
      have h2 := GlobalTheory.Pst.normV_eq_pow (l := l) h1
      rw [s.norm_v0_eq_one] at h2
      exact s.p_ne ((Nat.Prime.eq_one_or_self_of_dvd s.p_prime l (h2 ▸ dvd_pow_self l fun h3 => s.p_prime.ne_one
        (by rw [h2, h3, pow_zero]))).resolve_left (Fact.out : l.Prime).ne_one).symm
    have hs := hsp (s.w s.v0) (by rw [hwv]; exact V.sub (Or.inr (Or.inr rfl)))
    obtain ⟨z, hz, hf⟩ := hsph _ hp _ (s.hw s.v0) h0 _ (hτ₀ _)
    refine ⟨τ₀ _, hloc _ hs _ (hτ₀ _), (Submodule.ne_bot_iff _).2 ⟨z, fun u hu => hf u ?_, hz⟩⟩
    rw [← GlobalTheory.HeckeEigConj.KO_eq_KGL]
    obtain ⟨u, -, rfl⟩ := hu
    exact ⟨u, rfl⟩
  · obtain ⟨ω, h1, h2⟩ := (hab v).1
    exact ⟨τ₀ _, ω, hloc _ (hsp _ (hin _ (hlv v))) _ (hτ₀ _), h1, h2⟩
  · rw [hcl _ (hsp _ (by rw [hwv]; exact V.sub (Or.inr (Or.inl u.2)))) τ hτ]
    exact heq _ hu _ (s.hw u) rc (_root_.UpperTheory.V.Z.Desc.W.isRecT_of_isRecT P hrc) _ (hτ₀ _)
end


[HPT_SRC_DESC] Exact retained low-rank weak descent contract
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/ModularityArgument/CuspidalDescent/DescWeak.lean#L10-L21
def DescWeak : Prop :=
  ∀ (E : Type) [Field E] [NumberField E] [IsCMField E],
    (∀ w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E), Algebra.IsUnramifiedAt (𝓞 (maximalRealSubfield E)) w.asIdeal) →
    ∀ (p : ℕ), p.Prime → ∀ S : Set (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 (maximalRealSubfield E))), S.Finite →
      (∀ w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E), w.under (𝓞 (maximalRealSubfield E)) ∈ S → IsSplitAt w) →
      (∀ w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E), (p : 𝓞 E) ∈ w.asIdeal → w.under (𝓞 (maximalRealSubfield E)) ∈ S) →
    ∀ n : ℕ, 2 ≤ n → n ≤ 4 → ∀ π : CuspSub E n, (CG.mk π).IsRegAlg → GlobalTheory.ContraPair.DualIsConj π →
      (∀ w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E), w.under (𝓞 (maximalRealSubfield E)) ∉ S → ∃ B, (CG.mk π).HasSat w B) →
      ∃ σ : Submodule ℂ (UA E n → ℂ), IsAutRepU σ ∧ (∀ w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E), ¬ IsSplitAt w → IsUnramU σ w) ∧
        ∀ᶠ w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E) in Filter.cofinite, IsSplitAt w →
          ∃ (τ : SmIrr (GL (Fin n) (w.adicCompletion E))) (B : Multiset ℂˣ),
            IsCompAlong σ (locU n w) ((cLoc w).comp (locU n w).subtype) τ.ρ ∧ GlobalTheory.LFun.HasSatGL w τ.ρ B ∧ (CG.mk π).HasSat w B


[HPT_SRC_UNIVERSAL] Residual Ip, Pair, Maps and universal property
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/WeilGroupCharacters/UniversalOrdinaryDeterminantRing.lean#L125-L163
def Dbar : GDet n (ResidueField O) F.G₀ := GDet.ofRep (diagRep r.chib)

def Ip (u : F.St) : Subgroup F.G₀ := F.Iu u ⊓ ⨅ i, (r.chib i).ker

variable {R : Type} [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] [IsCO O R]

structure IsPsi (v : F.Vl) (i : Fin n) (ψ : F.Gv v →* Rˣ) : Prop where
  cont : AdicCont (maximalIdeal R) fun g => ((ψ g : Rˣ) : R)
  red : ∀ g, res O R (ψ g) = r.psib v i g
  mu : ∀ g : F.mu v, ((ψ g : Rˣ) : R) = algebraMap O R (r.th v i g)

end Resid

structure Pair (R : Type) [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] [IsCO O R] : Type 1 where
  D : GDet n R F.G₀
  ψ : ∀ v, Fin n → F.Gv v →* Rˣ
  cont : D.IsCont (maximalIdeal R)
  red : D.map (res O R : R →+* ResidueField O) = r.Dbar
  psi : ∀ v i, r.IsPsi v i (ψ v i)
  P : ∀ i g, D.lam i (F.star O R n (MonoidAlgebra.single g 1)) = D.lam i (MonoidAlgebra.single g 1)
  Ord : ∀ v (g : Fin n → F.Gv v) (h : F.G₀), D.tr (MonoidAlgebra.single h 1 * F.oEl v (ψ v) g) = 0
  I : ∀ u, ∀ g ∈ r.Ip u, ∀ h : F.G₀, D.tr (MonoidAlgebra.single h 1 * (MonoidAlgebra.single g 1 - 1)) = 0

variable {r} in

def Pair.Maps {R A : Type} [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] [IsCO O R] [CommRing A] (p : Pair r R) (f : R →+* A)
    (D : GDet n A F.G₀) (ψ : ∀ v, Fin n → F.Gv v →* Aˣ) : Prop :=
  p.D.map f = D ∧ ∀ v i, pushCh f (p.ψ v i) = ψ v i

structure UniversalOrdinaryDeterminantRing : Type 1 where
  B : Type
  [ring : CommRing B]
  [alg : Algebra O B]
  [co : IsCO O B]
  u : Pair r B
  univ : ∀ (R : Type) [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] [IsCO O R] (p : Pair r R),
    ∃! f : B →ₐ[O] R, u.Maps (f : B →+* R) p.D p.ψ

attribute [instance] UniversalOrdinaryDeterminantRing.ring UniversalOrdinaryDeterminantRing.alg UniversalOrdinaryDeterminantRing.co


[HPT_SRC_ISCO] Exact coefficient category IsCO
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/CommutativeAlgebra/ResMapBijective.lean#L1-L19
import Mathlib

noncomputable section

open IsLocalRing MvPowerSeries

namespace UpperTheory.V.Alg

class IsCO (O R : Type) [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] : Prop
    extends IsLocalRing R, IsNoetherianRing R where
  complete : IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal R) R
  isLocalHom : IsLocalHom (algebraMap O R)
  res_surj : ∀ x : R, ∃ a : O, x - algebraMap O R a ∈ maximalIdeal R

attribute [instance] IsCO.complete IsCO.isLocalHom

instance IsCO.self (O : Type) [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] [IsNoetherianRing O]
    [IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal O) O] : IsCO O O where
  complete := inferInstance


[HPT_SRC_TYPEPOINT] diag_pt full original trace/type producer
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/HeckeAlgebras/UpOperatorEigenvalues/WdCharpoly.lean#L119-L158
theorem diag_pt (hn : 1 ≤ n) (hs : Formalism.MainNotions.N.IsSSRep ρ) (t : Fin n → F.G₀ →* c.Oˣ) (ht : r.IsTeich t) (u : F.St) (I : Subgroup Γ)
    (hI : ∀ g ∈ I, p g ∈ F.Iu u) : ∃ P : GL (Fin n) (Qbar l), ∀ g : I,
      ((P * ρ g * P⁻¹ : GL (Fin n) (Qbar l)) : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) (Qbar l)) =
        Matrix.diagonal fun i => (W.pushCh (algebraMap c.O (Qbar l)) ((t i).comp (p.comp I.subtype)) g : Qbar l) := by
  obtain ⟨-, hfin, -⟩ := UpperTheory.V.abelian₂.dvr_finite l c.O
  refine diag_of_cong (N := Nat.card (ResidueField c.O) - 1) ?_ _ (fun i g => ?_) (ρ.comp I.subtype) (fun g hg => ?_) fun g k => ?_
  · have h1 : IsUnit ((Nat.card (ResidueField c.O) - 1 : ℕ) : c.O) := by
      rw [← residue_ne_zero_iff_isUnit, map_natCast, Nat.cast_pred Nat.card_pos]
      letI := Fintype.ofFinite (ResidueField c.O)
      simp [Nat.card_eq_fintype_card]
    have := pt_unit x (h1.map (algebraMap c.O B.B)).unit
    rwa [IsUnit.unit_spec, map_natCast, map_natCast] at this
  · rw [MonoidHom.comp_apply, ← map_pow]
    exact (congrArg _ (ht i _).2).trans (map_one _)
  · refine UpperTheory.eq_one_of_trace_translation_invariant_semisimple Γ (Qbar l) n ρ hs g fun h => ?_
    have h1 : p g ∈ r.Ip u := ⟨hI g g.2, Subgroup.mem_iInf.2 fun i => Units.ext (by
      have h2 : (t i (p g) : c.O) = 1 := Subtype.ext (congrArg Units.val (hg i))
      rw [← (ht i _).1, h2, map_one, Units.val_one])⟩
    have h2 := congrArg x (B.u.I u _ h1 (p h))
    have h3 := (AlgebraTools.Det.repApi (W.mat ρ)).2.2.1 hn (MonoidAlgebra.single h 1 * (MonoidAlgebra.single (g : Γ) 1 - 1))
    rw [← hx, W.GDet.tr, W.GDet.lam, chi_comap] at h3
    have h4 := (AlgebraTools.Det.mapApi B.u.D (x : B.B →+* Qbar l)).2.1 1 (MonoidAlgebra.single (p h) 1 * (MonoidAlgebra.single (p g) 1 - 1))
    rw [W.GDet.lam] at h4
    simp only [map_mul, map_sub, map_one, MonoidAlgebra.mapRingHom_single] at h4
    simp only [map_mul, map_sub, map_one] at h3
    simp only [MonoidAlgebra.mapDomainRingHom_apply, MonoidAlgebra.mapDomain_single] at h3
    have h5 := h3.symm.trans (h4.trans (h2.trans (map_zero x)))
    simp only [W.GDet.repAlg, MonoidAlgebra.lift_single, one_smul] at h5
    rw [mul_sub, mul_one, Matrix.trace_sub, sub_eq_zero, ← map_mul] at h5
    simpa [W.mat] using h5
  · have := chi_cong x p ρ hx g (∏ i, (X - C (t i (p g) : c.O))) (by
      rw [Resid.Dbar, (AlgebraTools.Det.repApi _).2.1, Polynomial.map_prod]
      simp only [Polynomial.map_sub, map_X, map_C, (ht _ _).1]
      exact Matrix.charpoly_diagonal _) k
    simp only [Polynomial.map_prod, Polynomial.map_sub, map_X, map_C] at this
    exact this

end Pt

[HPT_SRC_ORDCHAR] OrdChar equivalence and inverse finite-part identity
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/PadicHodgeTheory/OrdCharIff.lean#L1-L139
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.HeckeAlgebras.Distinct
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.WeilGroupCharacters.ContinuousResP
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.PadicHodgeTheory.IotaSpec

open NumberField IsDedekindDomain Formalism.ConjugateSelfDual ReciprocityTheory.Gal Formalism.BasicNotions Formalism.BasicNotions.Loc GlobalTheory.WeilDeligne GlobalTheory.GalOfCusp GlobalTheory.HeckeGal GlobalTheory.LocLanglands GlobalTheory.Published UpperTheory.V.W UpperTheory.V.Hi
attribute [local instance] GlobalTheory.LocalData.complRel GlobalTheory.LocalData.isLocalField

private theorem algPow_ne {F : Type} [Field F] [NumberField F] {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] (w : Plc F)
    (hh : (F →+* Qbar l) → ℤ) (x : (w.adicCompletion F)ˣ) : algPow w hh (x : w.adicCompletion F) ≠ 0 := by
  unfold algPow
  apply finprod_induction (fun z : Qbar l => z ≠ 0) one_ne_zero
  · intro a b ha hb
    exact mul_ne_zero ha hb
  · intro j
    exact zpow_ne_zero _ ((map_ne_zero _).mpr x.ne_zero)

private theorem algPow_mul {F : Type} [Field F] [NumberField F] {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] (w : Plc F)
    (hh : (F →+* Qbar l) → ℤ) (x y : w.adicCompletion F) : algPow w hh (x * y) = algPow w hh x * algPow w hh y := by
  haveI := finite_contEmb (l := l) w
  unfold algPow
  rw [← finprod_mul_distrib (Set.toFinite _) (Set.toFinite _)]
  exact finprod_congr fun j => by rw [map_mul, mul_zpow]

private noncomputable def algHom {F : Type} [Field F] [NumberField F] {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] (w : Plc F)
    (hh : (F →+* Qbar l) → ℤ) : (w.adicCompletion F)ˣ →* (Qbar l)ˣ :=
  MonoidHom.mk' (fun x => Units.mk0 _ (algPow_ne w hh x)) fun x y => Units.ext (algPow_mul w hh x y)

private theorem artin_unique (H : GlobalArtinReciprocity) {F Ω : Type} [Field F] [NumberField F] [Field Ω]
    [Algebra F Ω] [IsAlgClosure F Ω] {A : ValuationSubring Ω} {w : Plc F} {h : IsPlaceOver A w}
    {a a' : Weil h →* (w.adicCompletion F)ˣ} (ha : IsLocalArtin h a) (ha' : IsLocalArtin h a') : a' = a := by
  obtain ⟨art, hart⟩ := H F Ω
  have hinj := GlobalTheory.ArtinLocalInj.artin_localIdele_injective GlobalTheory.Published.chevalleyUnits' F Ω art hart w
  exact MonoidHom.ext fun σ => hinj ((ha'.glob art hart _).trans (ha.glob art hart _).symm)

private theorem part_iff (H : GlobalArtinReciprocity) {F Ω : Type} [Field F] [NumberField F] [Field Ω] [Algebra F Ω]
    [IsAlgClosure F Ω] {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {A : ValuationSubring Ω} {w : Plc F} (h : IsPlaceOver A w)
    (ι : Qbar l ≃+* ℂ) (a : Weil h →* (w.adicCompletion F)ˣ) (ha : IsLocalArtin h a) (hh : (F →+* Qbar l) → ℤ)
    (ψ : ↥(Dec (F := F) A) →* (Qbar l)ˣ) (ω : (w.adicCompletion F)ˣ →* ℂˣ) (ho : OrdChar ι a ω hh ψ)
    (ph : ↥(Ine (F := F) A) →* (Qbar l)ˣ) :
    IsWtPart h ψ (fun τ => hh (τ.1.comp (algebraMap F (w.adicCompletion F)))) ph ↔
      ∀ σ : ↥(Ine (F := F) A), ι (ph σ : Qbar l) = ((ω (a (ineIncl h σ)))⁻¹ : ℂˣ) := by
  have key : ∀ σ : Ine (F := F) A,
      ((ψ (Subgroup.inclusion (Weil_le_Dec h) (ineIncl h σ)) : Qbar l)) =
        ι.symm (ω (a (ineIncl h σ)) : ℂ) * algPow w (-hh) (a (ineIncl h σ) : w.adicCompletion F) :=
    fun σ => ho.val _ _ rfl (Set.mem_univ _)
  constructor
  · rintro ⟨-, hw⟩ σ
    have e := hw a ha σ
    rw [Units.val_mul, key σ] at e
    change _ = algPow w (-hh) _ at e
    have hP : algPow w (-hh) (a (ineIncl h σ) : w.adicCompletion F) ≠ 0 := algPow_ne w (-hh) _
    have e2 : ι.symm (ω (a (ineIncl h σ)) : ℂ) * (ph σ : Qbar l) = 1 := by
      apply mul_right_cancel₀ hP
      rw [one_mul]
      convert e using 1
      ring
    apply_fun ι at e2
    rw [map_mul, map_one, RingEquiv.apply_symm_apply] at e2
    rw [Units.val_inv_eq_inv_val]
    exact eq_inv_of_mul_eq_one_right e2
  · intro hR
    refine ⟨?_, fun a' ha' σ => ?_⟩
    · have hker : (ph.ker : Set (Ine (F := F) A)) = (fun σ => a (ineIncl h σ)) ⁻¹' (ω.ker : Set _) := by
        ext σ
        simp only [SetLike.mem_coe, MonoidHom.mem_ker, Set.mem_preimage]
        constructor
        · intro h1
          have e := hR σ
          rw [h1, Units.val_one, map_one] at e
          exact inv_eq_one.mp (Units.val_eq_one.mp e.symm)
        · intro h1
          have e := hR σ
          rw [h1, inv_one, Units.val_one] at e
          exact Units.val_eq_one.mp (ι.injective (e.trans (map_one ι).symm))
      rw [hker]
      exact IsOpen.preimage ha.cont ho.smooth
    · rw [artin_unique H ha ha', Units.val_mul, key σ]
      change _ = algPow w (-hh) _
      have e := hR σ
      have hph : (ph σ : Qbar l) = ι.symm (((ω (a (ineIncl h σ)))⁻¹ : ℂˣ) : ℂ) := by
        rw [← e, RingEquiv.symm_apply_apply]
      rw [hph, Units.val_inv_eq_inv_val, map_inv₀]
      have hne : ι.symm (ω (a (ineIncl h σ)) : ℂ) ≠ 0 := by simp
      field_simp

theorem UpperTheory.V.Loc.ordCharIff :
    GlobalArtinReciprocity → ∀ {F Ω : Type} [Field F] [NumberField F] [Field Ω] [Algebra F Ω] [IsAlgClosure F Ω] {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {A : ValuationSubring Ω} {w : Plc F} (h : IsPlaceOver A w) (ι : Qbar l ≃+* ℂ) (a : Weil h →* (w.adicCompletion F)ˣ), IsLocalArtin h a → ∀ (hh : (F →+* Qbar l) → ℤ) (ψ : ↥(Dec (F := F) A) →* (Qbar l)ˣ), ((∃ ω, OrdChar ι a ω hh ψ) ↔ Continuous ψ ∧ IsLocAlg h ψ fun τ => hh (τ.1.comp (algebraMap F (w.adicCompletion F)))) ∧ ∀ ω, OrdChar ι a ω hh ψ → ∀ ph : ↥(Ine (F := F) A) →* (Qbar l)ˣ, (IsWtPart h ψ (fun τ => hh (τ.1.comp (algebraMap F (w.adicCompletion F)))) ph ↔ ∀ σ : ↥(Ine (F := F) A), ι (ph σ : Qbar l) = ((ω (a (ineIncl h σ)))⁻¹ : ℂˣ)) := by
  intro H F Ω _ _ _ _ _ l _ A w h ι a ha hh ψ
  refine ⟨⟨fun ⟨ω, ho⟩ => ⟨ho.cont, ?_⟩, fun ⟨hc, ph, hph⟩ => ?_⟩,
    fun ω ho ph => part_iff H h ι a ha hh ψ ω ho ph⟩
  · let ph : Ine (F := F) A →* (Qbar l)ˣ :=
      (Units.map (ι.symm : ℂ →+* Qbar l).toMonoidHom).comp ((ω.comp (a.comp (ineIncl h)))⁻¹)
    refine ⟨ph, (part_iff H h ι a ha hh ψ ω ho ph).mpr fun σ => ?_⟩
    simp [ph]
  · let incl : Weil h →* Dec (F := F) A := Subgroup.inclusion (Weil_le_Dec h)
    let ξ : Weil h →* ℂˣ :=
      (Units.map (ι : Qbar l →+* ℂ).toMonoidHom).comp (ψ.comp incl * ((algHom w (-hh)).comp a)⁻¹)
    have hξ : ∀ s, ((ξ s : ℂˣ) : ℂ) =
        ι ((ψ (incl s) : Qbar l) / algPow w (-hh) (a s : w.adicCompletion F)) := by
      intro s
      simp [ξ, algHom, div_eq_mul_inv]
    have hsm : IsSmooth ξ := by
      have hset : {σ | ξ (ineIncl h σ) = 1} = (ph.ker : Set (Ine (F := F) A)) := by
        ext σ
        simp only [Set.mem_setOf_eq, SetLike.mem_coe, MonoidHom.mem_ker]
        have e := hph.2 a ha σ
        change ((ψ (incl (ineIncl h σ)) * ph σ : (Qbar l)ˣ) : Qbar l) =
          algPow w (-hh) (a (ineIncl h σ) : w.adicCompletion F) at e
        have hP := algPow_ne w (-hh) (a (ineIncl h σ))
        constructor
        · intro h1
          have h2 : (ψ (incl (ineIncl h σ)) : Qbar l) / algPow w (-hh) (a (ineIncl h σ) : w.adicCompletion F) = 1 := by
            apply ι.injective
            rw [← hξ, h1, Units.val_one, map_one]
          rw [div_eq_one_iff_eq hP] at h2
          rw [Units.val_mul, h2] at e
          apply Units.ext
          rw [Units.val_one]
          exact mul_left_cancel₀ hP (e.trans (mul_one _).symm)
        · intro h1
          rw [Units.val_mul, h1, Units.val_one, mul_one] at e
          apply Units.ext
          rw [Units.val_one, hξ, e, div_self hP, map_one]
      show IsOpen {σ | ξ (ineIncl h σ) = 1}
      rw [hset]
      exact hph.1
    obtain ⟨χ, hχs, hχ⟩ := ha.factor ξ hsm
    refine ⟨χ, ⟨hχs, hc, ?_⟩⟩
    intro s g hg _
    have hgs : g = incl s := Subtype.ext (by exact hg)
    subst hgs
    show (ψ (incl s) : Qbar l) = ι.symm (χ (a s) : ℂ) * algPow w (-hh) (a s : w.adicCompletion F)
    have hχa : χ (a s) = ξ s := by
      rw [hχ]
      rfl
    rw [hχa, hξ, RingEquiv.symm_apply_apply, div_mul_cancel₀ _ (algPow_ne w (-hh) (a s))]

[HPT_SRC_PS] Support-polynomial reverse principal-series comparison
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/ModularityArgument/ThresholdArguments/DenseThresholdCases/PseudoLocalLanglandsDescent.lean#L1-L136
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.ModularityArgument.ThresholdArguments.DenseThresholdCases.DiscreteSeriesCoefficientsCuspidal
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.ModularityArgument.CuspidalDescent.SphericalTwists.UnramifiedTwists.IsSubquotientOfHasSupport

section
open NumberField IsDedekindDomain Formalism.ConjugateSelfDual ReciprocityTheory.Gal Formalism.BasicNotions Polynomial ValuativeRel GlobalTheory.CuspRep GlobalTheory.WeilDeligne GlobalTheory.LocLanglands GlobalTheory.LocGL GlobalTheory.GalOfCusp GlobalTheory.SmGL GlobalTheory.SplitWeil UpperTheory.V.Hi
attribute [local instance] GlobalTheory.LocalData.complRel GlobalTheory.LocalData.isLocalField
private theorem multiset_eq_of_sum {Γ : Type} [Monoid Γ] (s t : Multiset (Γ →* ℂ))
    (h : ∀ g, (s.map fun f => f g).sum = (t.map fun f => f g).sum) : s = t := by
  classical
  have key : ∀ (u : Multiset (Γ →* ℂ)) (g : Γ),
      Finsupp.linearCombination ℂ (fun f : Γ →* ℂ => (f : Γ → ℂ))
        (u.map fun f => Finsupp.single f (1 : ℂ)).sum g = (u.map fun f => f g).sum := fun u g => by
    induction u using Multiset.induction_on with
    | empty => simp
    | cons a u ih => simp [ih]
  have cnt : ∀ (u : Multiset (Γ →* ℂ)) (f : Γ →* ℂ),
      (u.map fun f => Finsupp.single f (1 : ℂ)).sum f = (u.count f : ℂ) := fun u f => by
    induction u using Multiset.induction_on with
    | empty => simp
    | cons a u ih =>
      rw [Multiset.map_cons, Multiset.sum_cons, Finsupp.add_apply, ih, Multiset.count_cons, Finsupp.single_apply]
      by_cases e : a = f
      · simp [e, add_comm]
      · simp [e, Ne.symm e]
  have h1 := linearIndependent_monoidHom Γ ℂ (funext fun g => (key s g).trans ((h g).trans (key t g).symm))
  ext f
  have h2 := (cnt s f).symm.trans ((congrArg (fun u => u f) h1).trans (cnt t f))
  exact_mod_cast h2
section
variable {E Ω : Type} [Field E] [NumberField E] [Field Ω] [Algebra E Ω] [IsAlgClosure E Ω]
  {A : ValuationSubring Ω} {w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E)} {h : IsPlaceOver A w}
  {a : Weil h →* (w.adicCompletion E)ˣ} {ha : IsLocalArtin h a} {rc : IrrGL (w.adicCompletion E) → WD h}
private theorem charPol_supp (hZ : GlobalTheory.Published.ZelevinskyPolynomialRing) (H : UpperTheory.Zero.LLC.LawsT a ha rc)
    (x : IrrGL (w.adicCompletion E)) (s : Multiset (IrrGL (w.adicCompletion E))) (hs : HasSupport x.rep s) (g : Weil h) :
    (rc x).charPol g = (s.map fun y => (rc y).charPol g).prod := by
  haveI : CharZero (w.adicCompletion E) := charZero_of_injective_algebraMap (algebraMap E _).injective
  obtain ⟨𝔪, hm⟩ := GlobalTheory.langlandsExhaustionGL_holds (w.adicCompletion E) x
  have hs' : HasSupport x.rep (msupp 𝔪) := GlobalTheory.LocGL.hasSupport_msupp_of_isL hZ _ 𝔪 x hm
  have he : s = msupp 𝔪 :=
    GlobalTheory.LocGL.SupportUnique.hasSupport_unique GlobalTheory.RecT.geometricLemma_holds GlobalTheory.bernsteinZelevinskyFiniteness_holds
      hZ _ x _ _ hs hs'
  rw [he, H.langlands 𝔪.toList x (by rw [Multiset.coe_toList]; exact hm), segSum, CuspExt.charPol_sumList, List.map_map,
    msupp, Multiset.map_bind, Multiset.prod_bind]
  have hl : ∀ (l : List (Segment (w.adicCompletion E))) (F : Segment (w.adicCompletion E) → ℂ[X]),
      (l.map F).prod = ((l : Multiset _).map F).prod := fun l F => by rw [Multiset.map_coe, Multiset.prod_coe]
  rw [hl, Multiset.coe_toList]
  exact congrArg Multiset.prod (Multiset.map_congr rfl fun Δ _ => GlobalTheory.RoadU.CharSupport.charPol_seg H Δ g)
private theorem charPol_ofChar (H : UpperTheory.Zero.LLC.LawsT a ha rc) (χ : (w.adicCompletion E)ˣ →* ℂˣ) (hχ : IsSmoothChar χ)
    (g : Weil h) : (rc (IrrGL.ofChar χ hχ)).charPol g = X - C (χ (a g) : ℂ) := by
  rw [H.one, WD.lpOfChar, WD.charPol_mk, WDRep.ofChars_ρ, charpoly_diagEnd]
  simp
private theorem hasSupport_of_charPol (hZ : GlobalTheory.Published.ZelevinskyPolynomialRing) (H : UpperTheory.Zero.LLC.LawsT a ha rc)
    (x : IrrGL (w.adicCompletion E)) (s : Multiset (IrrGL (w.adicCompletion E))) (hs : HasSupport x.rep s)
    (h1 : ∀ y ∈ s, y.1 = 1) {n : ℕ} (ω : Fin n → (w.adicCompletion E)ˣ →* ℂˣ) (hω : ∀ i, IsSmoothChar (ω i))
    (hp : ∀ g, (rc x).charPol g = ∏ j, (X - C (ω j (a g) : ℂ))) :
    HasSupport x.rep (Finset.univ.val.map fun i => IrrGL.ofChar (ω i) (hω i)) := by
  let S := {χ : (w.adicCompletion E)ˣ →* ℂˣ // IsSmoothChar χ}
  let F : S → IrrGL (w.adicCompletion E) := fun χ => IrrGL.ofChar χ.1 χ.2
  obtain ⟨t, rfl⟩ : ∃ t : Multiset S, t.map F = s :=
    haveI : CanLift (IrrGL (w.adicCompletion E)) S F fun y => ∃ χ, F χ = y := ⟨fun _ hy => hy⟩
    (Multiset.canLift F (fun y => ∃ χ, F χ = y)).prf s fun y hy => by
      obtain ⟨χ, hχ, e⟩ := GlobalTheory.RoadU.PlainChars.exists_eq_ofChar y (h1 y hy)
      exact ⟨⟨χ, hχ⟩, e.symm⟩
  let c : S → (w.adicCompletion E)ˣ →* ℂ := fun χ => (Units.coeHom ℂ).comp χ.1
  have hc : Function.Injective c := fun χ ψ e =>
    Subtype.ext (MonoidHom.ext fun y => Units.ext (DFunLike.congr_fun e y))
  let t' : Multiset S := Finset.univ.val.map fun i => ⟨ω i, hω i⟩
  have htt : t = t' := by
    refine Multiset.map_injective hc (multiset_eq_of_sum _ _ fun y => ?_)
    obtain ⟨g, rfl⟩ := ha.surj y
    have h1 : (((t.map c).map fun f => f (a g)).map fun r => X - C r).prod =
        (((t'.map c).map fun f => f (a g)).map fun r => X - C r).prod := by
      have h2 := (charPol_supp hZ H x _ hs g).symm.trans (hp g)
      simp only [Multiset.map_map, Function.comp_def, t'] at h2 ⊢
      rw [Finset.prod_eq_multiset_prod] at h2
      refine Eq.trans (congrArg Multiset.prod (Multiset.map_congr rfl fun χ _ => ?_)) h2
      exact (charPol_ofChar H χ.1 χ.2 g).symm
    have h3 := congrArg Polynomial.roots h1
    rw [roots_multiset_prod_X_sub_C, roots_multiset_prod_X_sub_C] at h3
    rw [h3]
  rw [htt, Multiset.map_map] at hs
  exact hs
end
private theorem isSubquotient_of_hasSupport (hZ : GlobalTheory.Published.ZelevinskyPolynomialRing) {k : Type} [Field k] [ValuativeRel k]
    [TopologicalSpace k] [IsNonarchimedeanLocalField k] {n : ℕ} (τ : IrrRep (GL (Fin n) k)) (μ : Fin n → kˣ →* ℂˣ)
    (hμ : ∀ i, IsSmoothChar (μ i)) (hs : HasSupport τ.ρ (Finset.univ.val.map fun i => IrrGL.ofChar (μ i) (hμ i))) :
    IsSubquotient τ.ρ (prinSeries μ) := by
  have hd : ∑ i, (IrrGL.ofChar (μ i) (hμ i)).1 = n := by simp [IrrGL.ofChar]
  refine IsSubquotient.trans ?_ (GlobalTheory.SphSeg.isSubquotient_prodIrr_prinSeries _ hd μ hμ fun _ => rfl)
  obtain ⟨Z, Ψ, -, hΨ, -⟩ := hZ k
  have h1 := SupportRing.pos_pc_sub hΨ hs τ.smooth τ.irr
  obtain ⟨hp, ep⟩ := SupportRing.prodIrr_cls hΨ _ hd
  refine SupportRing.isSubquotient_of_clsGL_ne hp τ.smooth τ.irr ?_
  have h2 := h1 (IrrGL.of τ.ρ τ.smooth τ.irr)
  rw [SupportRing.pc_map, ← ep] at h2
  have e1 : ∀ (A : GrothGL k) (y : IrrGL k), Ψ.symm (Ψ A - SupportRing.bas Ψ y) y = A y - 1 := by
    intro A y
    rw [SupportRing.bas, ← map_sub, Ψ.symm_apply_apply, Finsupp.sub_apply, Finsupp.single_eq_same]
  rw [e1] at h2
  intro h3
  rw [h3] at h2
  exact absurd h2 (by norm_num)
theorem UpperTheory.V.Z.Desc.W.pseudo_local_langlands_descent :
    _root_.UpperTheory.V.Z.Desc.W.PseudoLocalDescent := by
  intro P L Ω _ _ _ _ _ n w A h a ha rc hrc σ hT ω hω hp
  obtain ⟨a', ha', H⟩ := hrc
  obtain rfl : a' = a := by
    obtain ⟨art, hart⟩ := P.aut.art L Ω
    have hinj := GlobalTheory.ArtinLocalInj.artin_localIdele_injective P.loc.chev L Ω art hart w
    exact MonoidHom.ext fun σ => hinj ((ha'.glob art hart _).trans (ha.glob art hart _).symm)
  haveI : CharZero (w.adicCompletion L) := algebraRat.charZero _
  have hμ : ∀ i, IsSmoothChar (ω i * absPow (w.adicCompletion L) (((n : ℂ) - 1) / 2)) :=
    fun i => (hω i).mul (isSmoothChar_absPow _)
  have e0 : ((n : ℂ) - 1) / 2 + (((1 - (n : ℝ)) / 2 : ℝ) : ℂ) = 0 := by push_cast; ring
  have e1 : ∀ i, (IrrGL.ofChar (ω i) (hω i)).nu (((n : ℂ) - 1) / 2) = IrrGL.ofChar _ (hμ i) := fun i =>
    IrrGL.tw_ofChar _ _ _ _
  let τ : IrrRep (GL (Fin n) (w.adicCompletion L)) := ⟨_, σ.ρ, σ.smooth, σ.irred⟩
  obtain ⟨s, hs0, hs1, -⟩ := hT
  have hs := hasSupport_of_charPol P.loc.zel H (algNorm σ) _
    (SupportRing.hasSupport_nu hs0 (((1 - (n : ℝ)) / 2 : ℝ) : ℂ)) (fun y hy => by
      obtain ⟨z, hz, rfl⟩ := Multiset.mem_map.1 hy
      exact hs1 z hz) ω hω hp
  have h2 := SupportRing.hasSupport_nu hs (((n : ℂ) - 1) / 2)
  have e2 : (algNorm σ).nu (((n : ℂ) - 1) / 2) = IrrGL.of σ.ρ σ.smooth σ.irred := by
    rw [algNorm, IrrGL.nu_nu, add_comm, e0, IrrGL.nu_zero]
  have key : ∀ (x y : IrrGL (w.adicCompletion L)) s, x = y → HasSupport x.rep s → HasSupport y.rep s := by
    rintro _ _ _ rfl h; exact h
  have h3 := (key _ _ _ e2 h2).of_equiv (IrrGL.equivOf σ.ρ σ.smooth σ.irred).symm
  simp only [Multiset.map_map, Function.comp_def, e1] at h3
  exact isSubquotient_of_hasSupport P.loc.zel τ _ hμ h3
end

variable {k : Type} [Field k] [ValuativeRel k] [TopologicalSpace k] [IsNonarchimedeanLocalField k]
private theorem eq_of_twist {χ ψ η : kˣ →* ℂˣ}
    (φ : (detRep (Fin 1) χ).Equiv (twist (detRep (Fin 1) ψ) (detChar (Fin 1) η))) : χ = ψ * η := by
  refine MonoidHom.ext fun x => Units.ext ?_
  let g : GL (Fin 1) k := Matrix.GeneralLinearGroup.scalar _ x
  have hg : Matrix.GeneralLinearGroup.det g = x := by
    ext; simp [g, Matrix.GeneralLinearGroup.scalar]
  obtain ⟨y, hy⟩ := φ.surjective 1
  have h1 := LinearMap.congr_fun (φ.isIntertwining' g) y
  have hy' : φ.toLinearMap y = 1 := hy
  have h2 : detRep (Fin 1) χ g y = (χ x : ℂ) • y := by simp [detRep, hg]
  simp only [LinearMap.comp_apply, h2, map_smul, hy'] at h1
  simpa [twist, detRep, ofChar, hg, mul_comm] using h1
private theorem irr_zero (ψ : Fin 0 → kˣ →* ℂˣ) : (prinSeries ψ).IsIrreducible := by
  have hg : ∀ g : GL (Fin 0) k, g = 1 := fun g => Units.ext (Subsingleton.elim _ _)
  let f : ℂ →ₗ[ℂ] IndSp (points (Fin 0)) ((ofChar (torusChar ψ)).comp (leviTorus k (Fin 0)).symm.toMonoidHom) :=
    { toFun := fun c => ⟨⟨fun _ => c, fun p g => by
          obtain rfl : p = 1 := Subtype.ext (hg _)
          simp⟩, mem_smoothPart_of_nhds (Filter.Eventually.of_forall fun g => rfl)⟩
      map_add' := fun a b => rfl
      map_smul' := fun a b => rfl }
  have E : (detRep (Fin 0) (1 : kˣ →* ℂˣ)).Equiv (prinSeries ψ) :=
    Representation.Equiv.mk' ⟨f, fun g => by rw [hg g, map_one, map_one]; rfl⟩ (fun F => F.1.1 1)
      (fun c => rfl) (fun F => Subtype.ext (Subtype.ext (funext fun g => by rw [hg g])))
  exact GlobalTheory.Parabolic.isIrreducible_of_equiv E (isIrreducible_ofChar _)
set_option synthInstance.maxHeartbeats 400000 in
private theorem irr_prin (H : GlobalTheory.Published.TotallyDisjointProductIrreducible) (n : ℕ) :
    ∀ ψ : Fin n → kˣ →* ℂˣ, (∀ i, IsSmoothChar (ψ i)) →
      (∀ i j, i ≠ j → ψ i ≠ ψ j ∧ ψ i ≠ ψ j * absPow k 1) → (prinSeries ψ).IsIrreducible := by
  induction n with
  | zero => exact fun ψ _ _ => irr_zero ψ
  | succ n ih =>
    intro ψ hψ hne
    let ψ₁ : Fin n → kˣ →* ℂˣ := fun i => ψ (Fin.castAdd 1 i)
    let ψ₂ : Fin 1 → kˣ →* ℂˣ := fun _ => ψ (Fin.natAdd n 0)
    have happ : Fin.append ψ₁ ψ₂ = ψ := by
      have : ψ₂ = fun i => ψ (Fin.natAdd n i) := funext fun i => by rw [Subsingleton.elim i 0]
      rw [this]
      exact Fin.append_castAdd_natAdd
    have h₁ : (prinSeries ψ₁).IsIrreducible :=
      ih ψ₁ (fun i => hψ _) fun i j hij => hne _ _ fun h => hij (Fin.castAdd_injective _ _ h)
    have h₂ : (prinSeries ψ₂).IsIrreducible :=
      GlobalTheory.Parabolic.isIrreducible_of_equiv (GlobalTheory.PrinSat.oneEquiv _ (hψ _)) (isIrreducible_ofChar _)
    let σ₁ : Irr k n := ⟨_, prinSeries ψ₁, isSmooth_parInd _ _, h₁⟩
    let σ₂ : Irr k 1 := ⟨_, prinSeries ψ₂, isSmooth_parInd _ _, h₂⟩
    have hirr : (prod₂ σ₁.π σ₂.π).IsIrreducible := by
      refine H k n 1 n 1 σ₁ σ₂ _ _ _ _
        (GlobalTheory.TameIwahori.isCuspSupport_of_prinSeries σ₁ (ψ := ψ₁) (fun i => hψ _) (.refl _))
        (GlobalTheory.TameIwahori.isCuspSupport_of_prinSeries σ₂ (ψ := ψ₂) (fun i => hψ _) (.refl _)) ?_
      rintro a b h j hj ⟨φ⟩
      have e : ψ (Fin.castAdd 1 a) = ψ (Fin.natAdd n 0) * absPow k (j : ℂ) := eq_of_twist φ
      have hab : Fin.castAdd 1 a ≠ Fin.natAdd n 0 := fun h => by
        have := congrArg Fin.val h
        have := a.2
        simp at *
        omega
      obtain rfl | rfl | rfl : j = -1 ∨ j = 0 ∨ j = 1 := by
        have := abs_le.1 hj
        omega
      · refine (hne _ _ hab.symm).2 ?_
        rw [e, mul_assoc, ← absPow_add]
        simp
      · exact (hne _ _ hab).1 (by simpa using e)
      · exact (hne _ _ hab).2 (by simpa using e)
    obtain ⟨E1⟩ := PrinProd.stage_one (k := k) ψ₁ ψ₂
    obtain ⟨E2⟩ := PrinProd.stage_two (k := k) ψ₁ ψ₂
    rw [← happ]
    exact GlobalTheory.Parabolic.isIrreducible_of_equiv (E1.trans E2) hirr
end UpperTheory.V.Hi.PsIrr
theorem UpperTheory.V.Hi.prin_series_irreducible_and_unique_subquotient :
    _root_.GlobalTheory.Mem.LiteralAutomorphy.LiteratureRankNHypothesis → ∀ (k : Type) [Field k] [ValuativeRel k] [TopologicalSpace k] [IsNonarchimedeanLocalField k] (n : ℕ) (ω : Fin n → kˣ →* ℂˣ), (∀ i, IsSmoothChar (ω i)) → (∀ i j, i ≠ j → ω i ≠ ω j ∧ ω i ≠ ω j * absPow k 1) → (PS ω).IsIrreducible ∧ ∀ (V : Type) [AddCommGroup V] [Module ℂ V] (π : Representation ℂ (GL (Fin n) k) V), π.IsIrreducible → IsSubquotient π (PS ω) → Nonempty (π.Equiv (PS ω)) := by
  intro H k _ _ _ _ n ω hω hne
  have hi : (PS ω).IsIrreducible := by
    refine PsIrr.irr_prin H.loc.disj n _
      (fun i => (hω i).mul (isSmoothChar_of_isUnram (isUnram_absPow k _))) fun i j hij => ⟨fun h => ?_, fun h => ?_⟩
    · exact (hne i j hij).1 (mul_right_cancel h)
    · rw [mul_right_comm] at h
      exact (hne i j hij).2 (mul_right_cancel h)
  exact ⟨hi, fun V _ _ π hπ hs => (hs.nonempty_equiv hπ hi).map fun e => e.symm⟩
end

variable {k : Type} [Field k] [ValuativeRel k] [TopologicalSpace k] [IsNonarchimedeanLocalField k]

private theorem isL_chars {n : ℕ} {V : Type} [AddCommGroup V] [Module ℂ V] (ρ : Representation ℂ (GL (Fin n) k) V)
    (hρ : ρ.IsIrreducible) (ξ : Fin n → kˣ →* ℂˣ) (hξ : ∀ i, IsSmoothChar (ξ i))
    (hu : GlobalTheory.RoadU.Unlinked (GlobalTheory.RoadU.chars ξ hξ)) (e : ρ.Equiv (prinSeries ξ)) :
    IsL ((List.ofFn fun i => Segment.single (IrrGL.ofChar (ξ i) (hξ i)) (IrrGL.isCusp_ofChar _ _) : List (Segment k)) :
      Multiset (Segment k)) ρ := by
  let y : Fin n → IrrGL k := fun i => IrrGL.ofChar (ξ i) (hξ i)
  have hy : ∀ i, (y i).IsCusp := fun i => IrrGL.isCusp_ofChar _ _
  have hd : ∑ i, (y i).1 = n := by simp [y, IrrGL.ofChar]
  have hmem : ∀ i, y i ∈ GlobalTheory.RoadU.chars ξ hξ := fun i => Multiset.mem_map_of_mem _ (Finset.mem_univ i)
  have hnl : ∀ i j, ¬ (Segment.single (y i) (hy i)).Linked (Segment.single (y j) (hy j)) := fun i j =>
    GlobalTheory.CompanionTheory.BZPair.not_linked_single (hy i) (hy j) (hu _ (hmem j) _ (hmem i)) (hu _ (hmem i) _ (hmem j))
  have hP : (prodIrr y hd).IsIrreducible :=
    GlobalTheory.RgGu.Holds.lUnlinkedIrr_holds k n n (fun i => Segment.single (y i) (hy i)) y hd hnl
      (fun i => (single_spec (hy i)).2.1)
  obtain ⟨p⟩ := (Placing.parInd_jac_placing k _ _ _ (Equiv.sigmaUnique (Fin n) fun _ => Fin 1)
    (stdBlocks (fun i => (y i).1) hd) _).1 (piTensor fun i => detRep (Fin 1) (ξ i))
  let q := multiProdEquiv (stdBlocks (fun i => (y i).1) hd) fun i => equivRep (IrrRep.det (Fin 1) (ξ i) (hξ i))
  have hM : (multiProd (Equiv.sigmaUnique (Fin n) fun _ => Fin 1) fun i => detRep (Fin 1) (ξ i)).IsIrreducible :=
    GlobalTheory.Parabolic.isIrreducible_of_equiv (p.trans q).symm hP
  haveI := nontrivial_of_irreducible hM
  haveI := GlobalTheory.TypeTwo.PowIrrProof.nontrivial_of_isSubquotient
    (GlobalTheory.PermPrin.isSubquotient_multiProd_prinSeries (Equiv.sigmaUnique (Fin n) fun _ => Fin 1) ξ)
  obtain ⟨e1⟩ := GlobalTheory.TypeTwo.PowIrrProof.equiv_of_isSubquotient (GlobalTheory.PrinProd.isSubquotient_prinSeries_multiProd k n ξ) hM
  have φ : ρ.Equiv (prodIrr y hd) := (e.trans e1.symm).trans (p.trans q)
  exact ⟨n, y, hd, ⟨fun i => Segment.single (y i) (hy i), ⟨Fin.univ_val_map _, fun i j _ hp => hnl i j hp.1⟩,
    fun i => (single_spec (hy i)).2.1⟩, (isCosocle_self hρ).of_equiv (.refl _) φ⟩

end Loc

section WD

variable {E Ω : Type} [Field E] [NumberField E] [Field Ω] [Algebra E Ω] [IsAlgClosure E Ω]
  {A : ValuationSubring Ω} {w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E)} {h : IsPlaceOver A w}

private theorem monodromy_free_sum_list (l : List (WD h)) (hl : ∀ z ∈ l, z.mono0) : (sumList l).mono0 := by
  induction l with
  | nil =>
    show (WD.lpZero h).mono0
    rw [WD.lpZero, WD.monodromyZero_ofWeilDeligneRepClass_iff]
    rfl
  | cons b l ih =>
    show (WD.lpSum b (sumList l)).mono0
    exact (WD.monomial_sum_iff _ _).2 ⟨hl b (by simp), ih fun z hz => hl z (by simp [hz])⟩

private theorem monodromy_free_of_character (c : Weil h →* ℂˣ) (hc : GlobalTheory.WeilDeligne.IsSmooth c) : (WD.lpOfChar h c hc).mono0 := by
  rw [WD.lpOfChar, WD.monodromyZero_ofWeilDeligneRepClass_iff]
  rfl

private theorem comm_one {G : Type} [Group G] {H : Type} [CommGroup H] (f : G →* H) (s t : G) : f ⁅s, t⁆ = 1 := by
  rw [map_commutatorElement]
  exact (Commute.all _ _).commutator_eq

private theorem tor_sum {n : ℕ} (c : Fin n → Weil h →* ℂˣ) (hc : ∀ i, GlobalTheory.WeilDeligne.IsSmooth (c i)) (r : ℝ)
    (hd : ((sumList (List.ofFn fun i => WD.lpOfChar h (c i) (hc i))).lpTw r).lpDim = n) :
    UpperTheory.GalStar.Tor ((sumList (List.ofFn fun i => WD.lpOfChar h (c i) (hc i))).lpTw r) := by
  intro s t
  show WD.charPol _ _ = _
  rw [hd, GlobalTheory.GalCut.AwayMono.charPol_lpTw, comm_one, GlobalTheory.LocLanglands.CuspExt.charPol_sumList, List.map_ofFn]
  have h1 : ∀ i, (WD.lpOfChar h (c i) (hc i)).charPol ⁅s, t⁆ = X - 1 := fun i => by
    rw [WD.lpOfChar, WD.charPol_mk, WDRep.ofChars_ρ, charpoly_diagEnd]
    simp only [comm_one]
    simp
  simp [h1, List.prod_ofFn]

end WD

open UpperTheory.V.Z.Win.MonodromyFreeReciprocity in
theorem UpperTheory.V.Z.Win.plainRec :
    UpperTheory.V.Z.Win.PlainRec := by
  intro E Ω _ _ _ _ _ _ A w h rc ⟨a, ha, L⟩ n π t hw σ hσ
  haveI : CharZero (w.adicCompletion E) := charZero_of_injective_algebraMap (algebraMap E _).injective
  have hq : GroundTheory.LocalField.residueCard (w.adicCompletion E) = Ideal.absNorm w.asIdeal :=
    GroundTheory.Global.residueCard_adicCompletion
  have hP : GlobalTheory.RoadU.PlainTor (IrrGL.of σ.ρ σ.smooth σ.irred) := by
    rcases Nat.eq_zero_or_pos n with rfl | hn
    · obtain ⟨s, hs, h1⟩ := hw.1 σ hσ
      refine ⟨s, hs, h1, fun y hy z _ => ?_⟩
      have h2 := hs.sum_fst
      have h3 := Multiset.le_sum_of_mem (Multiset.mem_map_of_mem Sigma.fst hy)
      rw [h2, h1 y hy] at h3
      exact absurd (show 1 ≤ 0 from h3) (by omega)
    · exact (plainIff E n hn π w).2 ⟨hw.1, fun s hs => winUnl E n π t w hw s hs⟩ σ hσ
  obtain ⟨ξ, hξ, hs, hu⟩ := GlobalTheory.RoadU.plainTor_exists_chars _ _ hP
  have hs' : HasSupport σ.ρ (GlobalTheory.RoadU.chars ξ hξ) := hs.of_equiv (IrrGL.equivOf σ.ρ σ.smooth σ.irred).symm
  have hl := winUnl E n π t w hw _ ⟨σ, hσ, hs'⟩
  obtain ⟨e⟩ := unlFull (w.adicCompletion E) n σ.V σ.ρ ξ hξ hs' (hq ▸ hl)
  have hL := (isL_chars σ.ρ σ.irred ξ hξ hu e).of_equiv (IrrGL.equivOf σ.ρ σ.smooth σ.irred)
  have hr : rc (IrrGL.of σ.ρ σ.smooth σ.irred) =
      sumList (List.ofFn fun i => WD.lpOfChar h ((ξ i).comp a) (ha.smooth (hξ i))) := by
    rw [L.langlands _ _ hL, segSum, List.map_ofFn]
    congr 1
    refine List.ofFn_inj.mpr (funext fun i => ?_)
    simp only [Function.comp_apply, Segment.single, Segment.first_ofFirst, Segment.len_ofFirst, Nat.cast_one, sub_self,
      zero_div, WD.lpTw_zero, Test.lpChar_lpSp_one, L.one]
  have hA : rc (algNorm σ) = (sumList (List.ofFn fun i => WD.lpOfChar h ((ξ i).comp a) (ha.smooth (hξ i)))).lpTw
      ((1 - (n : ℝ)) / 2) := by
    rw [algNorm, L.nu, hr]
  refine ⟨hP, ?_, ?_⟩
  · rw [hA]
    exact tor_sum _ _ _ (by rw [← hA, L.dim]; rfl)
  · rw [hA, WD.monomial_twist_iff]
    exact monodromy_free_sum_list _ fun z hz => by
      obtain ⟨i, rfl⟩ := List.mem_ofFn.1 hz
      exact monodromy_free_of_character _ _

section Loc

variable {k : Type} [Field k] [ValuativeRel k] [TopologicalSpace k] [IsNonarchimedeanLocalField k]

variable (k) in

def unif : kˣ := Classical.epsilon fun p : kˣ => ValuativeRel.valuation k (p : k) < 1 ∧
  ∀ x : k, ValuativeRel.valuation k x < 1 → ValuativeRel.valuation k x ≤ ValuativeRel.valuation k (p : k)

def chars (s : Multiset (IrrGL k)) : Set (kˣ →* ℂˣ) := {ξ | ∃ hξ, IrrGL.ofChar ξ hξ ∈ s}

def pol (s : Multiset (IrrGL k)) (χ : kˣ →* ℂˣ) : ℂ[X] :=
  (s.map fun y => if h : ∃ ξ : kˣ →* ℂˣ, (∃ hξ, y = IrrGL.ofChar ξ hξ) ∧ IsUnram (ξ * χ) then
    1 - C (((h.choose * χ) (unif k) : ℂˣ) : ℂ) * X else 1).prod

def Unl (q : ℕ) (s : Multiset (IrrGL k)) : Prop :=
  ∀ ξ ∈ chars s, ∀ ξ' ∈ chars s, IsUnram (ξ * ξ'⁻¹) → (((ξ * ξ'⁻¹) (unif k) : ℂˣ) : ℂ) ≠ q

end Loc

variable {K : Type} [Field K] [NumberField K] [IsTotallyComplex K] {n : ℕ}

def WinAt (π : CuspSub K n) (t : ℝ) (v : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 K)) : Prop :=
  TorAt π v ∧ ∀ s, SuppAt π v s → ∀ ξ ∈ chars s,
    (Ideal.absNorm v.asIdeal : ℝ) ^ (t - 1 / 2) < ‖((ξ (unif (v.adicCompletion K)) : ℂˣ) : ℂ)‖ ∧
      ‖((ξ (unif (v.adicCompletion K)) : ℂˣ) : ℂ)‖ < (Ideal.absNorm v.asIdeal : ℝ) ^ (t + 1 / 2)

def AllWin (π : CuspSub K n) : Prop := ∃ t, ∀ v, WinAt π t v

theorem AllWin.tor {π : CuspSub K n} (h : AllWin π) : AllTor π := fun v => (h.choose_spec v).1

def PolFacts : Prop :=

variable {k : Type} [Field k] [ValuativeRel k] [TopologicalSpace k] [IsNonarchimedeanLocalField k]
private theorem exists_unr {ξ : kˣ →* ℂˣ} (h : IsUnram ξ) : ∃ a : ℂˣ, ξ = GlobalTheory.SmGL.unrChar k a := by
  obtain ⟨x, hx⟩ := ValuativeRel.valuation_surjective (K := k) (ValuativeRel.uniformizer k)
  have h0 : x ≠ 0 := fun e => ValuativeRel.uniformizer_ne_zero (R := k) (by rw [← hx, e, map_zero])
  exact ⟨_, eq_unrChar h (ϖ := Units.mk0 x h0) hx⟩
private theorem full_chars [CharZero k] {n : ℕ} {V : Type} [AddCommGroup V] [Module ℂ V] (ρ : Representation ℂ (GL (Fin n) k) V)
    (ξ : Fin n → kˣ →* ℂˣ) (hξ : ∀ i, IsSmoothChar (ξ i)) (hs : HasSupport ρ (GlobalTheory.RoadU.chars ξ hξ))
    (hu : GlobalTheory.RoadU.Unlinked (GlobalTheory.RoadU.chars ξ hξ)) : Nonempty (ρ.Equiv (prinSeries ξ)) ∧
    IsL ((List.ofFn fun i => Segment.single (IrrGL.ofChar (ξ i) (hξ i)) (IrrGL.isCusp_ofChar _ _) : List (Segment k)) :
      Multiset (Segment k)) ρ := by
  obtain ⟨hρ, hq⟩ := (UpperTheory.Gal.LocD.ofSupport_holds k n V ρ ξ hξ).1 hs
  let y : Fin n → IrrGL k := fun i => IrrGL.ofChar (ξ i) (hξ i)
  have hy : ∀ i, (y i).IsCusp := fun i => IrrGL.isCusp_ofChar _ _
  have hd : ∑ i, (y i).1 = n := by simp [y, IrrGL.ofChar]
  have hmem : ∀ i, y i ∈ GlobalTheory.RoadU.chars ξ hξ := fun i => Multiset.mem_map_of_mem _ (Finset.mem_univ i)
  have hnl : ∀ i j, ¬ (Segment.single (y i) (hy i)).Linked (Segment.single (y j) (hy j)) := fun i j =>
    GlobalTheory.CompanionTheory.BZPair.not_linked_single (hy i) (hy j) (hu _ (hmem j) _ (hmem i)) (hu _ (hmem i) _ (hmem j))
  have hP : (prodIrr y hd).IsIrreducible :=
    GlobalTheory.RgGu.Holds.lUnlinkedIrr_holds k n n (fun i => Segment.single (y i) (hy i)) y hd hnl
      (fun i => (single_spec (hy i)).2.1)
  obtain ⟨p⟩ := (Placing.parInd_jac_placing k _ _ _ (Equiv.sigmaUnique (Fin n) fun _ => Fin 1)
    (stdBlocks (fun i => (y i).1) hd) _).1 (piTensor fun i => detRep (Fin 1) (ξ i))
  let q := multiProdEquiv (stdBlocks (fun i => (y i).1) hd) fun i => equivRep (IrrRep.det (Fin 1) (ξ i) (hξ i))
  have hM : (multiProd (Equiv.sigmaUnique (Fin n) fun _ => Fin 1) fun i => detRep (Fin 1) (ξ i)).IsIrreducible :=
    GlobalTheory.Parabolic.isIrreducible_of_equiv (p.trans q).symm hP
  haveI := nontrivial_of_irreducible hM
  haveI := GlobalTheory.TypeTwo.PowIrrProof.nontrivial_of_isSubquotient
    (GlobalTheory.PermPrin.isSubquotient_multiProd_prinSeries (Equiv.sigmaUnique (Fin n) fun _ => Fin 1) ξ)
  obtain ⟨e1⟩ := GlobalTheory.TypeTwo.PowIrrProof.equiv_of_isSubquotient (GlobalTheory.PrinProd.isSubquotient_prinSeries_multiProd k n ξ) hM
  obtain ⟨e⟩ := hq.nonempty_equiv hρ (GlobalTheory.Parabolic.isIrreducible_of_equiv e1 hM)
  have φ : ρ.Equiv (prodIrr y hd) := (e.symm.trans e1.symm).trans (p.trans q)
  exact ⟨⟨e.symm⟩, n, y, hd, ⟨fun i => Segment.single (y i) (hy i), ⟨Fin.univ_val_map _, fun i j _ hp => hnl i j hp.1⟩,
    fun i => (single_spec (hy i)).2.1⟩, (isCosocle_self hρ).of_equiv (.refl _) φ⟩
end Loc
section WD
variable {E Ω : Type} [Field E] [NumberField E] [Field Ω] [Algebra E Ω] [IsAlgClosure E Ω]
  {A : ValuationSubring Ω} {w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E)} {h : IsPlaceOver A w}
private theorem eq_one_of_unr {n : ℕ} (c : Fin n → Weil h →* ℂˣ) (hc : ∀ i, GlobalTheory.WeilDeligne.IsSmooth (c i))
    (hu : (sumList (List.ofFn fun i => WD.lpOfChar h (c i) (hc i))).unr) (i : Fin n) (t : Ine (F := E) A) :
    c i (ineIncl h t) = 1 := by
  have h1 : ∀ i g, (WD.lpOfChar h (c i) (hc i)).charPol g = X - C (c i g : ℂ) := fun i g => by
    rw [WD.lpOfChar, WD.charPol_mk, WDRep.ofChars_ρ, charpoly_diagEnd]
    simp
  have h2 : ((sumList (List.ofFn fun i => WD.lpOfChar h (c i) (hc i))).charPol (ineIncl h t)).IsRoot (c i (ineIncl h t) : ℂ) := by
    rw [GlobalTheory.LocLanglands.CuspExt.charPol_sumList, List.map_ofFn, List.prod_ofFn]
    refine IsRoot.dvd ?_ (Finset.dvd_prod_of_mem _ (Finset.mem_univ i))
    simp [h1]
  generalize sumList (List.ofFn fun i => WD.lpOfChar h (c i) (hc i)) = x at hu h2
  obtain ⟨m, b, rfl⟩ := x.exists_rep
  rw [WD.unr_mk] at hu
  rw [WD.charPol_mk, hu.2 t, ← Module.End.hasEigenvalue_iff_isRoot_charpoly] at h2
  obtain ⟨v, hv⟩ := h2.exists_hasEigenvector
  have h3 := hv.apply_eq_smul
  refine Units.ext (smul_left_injective ℂ hv.2 ?_)
  simpa using h3.symm
end WD
theorem sphT_alt : _root_.UpperTheory.V.Z.Desc.W.SphTAlt := by
  intro _ F Ω _ _ _ _ _ l _ ι n r _ w hl A h hI rc ⟨a, ha, L⟩ σ hP hx
  haveI : CharZero (w.adicCompletion F) := charZero_of_injective_algebraMap (algebraMap F _).injective
  have e0 := hx.wdOf_eq hl
  obtain ⟨t, ht, φ, hφ, N, b, hN, -, -⟩ := id hx
  have hxu : (rc (algNorm σ)).unr := e0 ▸ (hN.unr_wdOf_iff hl ht ι).2 hI
  obtain ⟨ξ, hξ, hs, hu⟩ := GlobalTheory.RoadU.plainTor_exists_chars _ _ hP
  have hs' : HasSupport σ.ρ (GlobalTheory.RoadU.chars ξ hξ) := hs.of_equiv (IrrGL.equivOf σ.ρ σ.smooth σ.irred).symm
  obtain ⟨⟨e⟩, hL0⟩ := full_chars (n := n) σ.ρ ξ hξ hs' hu
  have hL := hL0.of_equiv (IrrGL.equivOf σ.ρ σ.smooth σ.irred)
  have hr : rc (IrrGL.of σ.ρ σ.smooth σ.irred) =
      sumList (List.ofFn fun i => WD.lpOfChar h ((ξ i).comp a) (ha.smooth (hξ i))) := by
    rw [L.langlands _ _ hL, segSum, List.map_ofFn]
    congr 1
    refine List.ofFn_inj.mpr (funext fun i => ?_)
    simp only [Function.comp_apply, Segment.single, Segment.first_ofFirst, Segment.len_ofFirst, Nat.cast_one, sub_self,
      zero_div, WD.lpTw_zero, Test.lpChar_lpSp_one, L.one]
  rw [algNorm, L.nu, hr, WD.unr_tw] at hxu
  have hun : ∀ i, IsUnram (ξ i) := fun i u hu1 => by
    obtain ⟨g, rfl⟩ := ha.surj u
    have h0 : deg g = 0 := by
      have h1 := ha.ord_eq g
      rw [ord_eq_zero_iff.2 hu1] at h1
      omega
    exact eq_one_of_unr _ _ hxu i ⟨g, deg_eq_zero_iff.1 h0⟩
  choose α hα using fun i => exists_unr (hun i)
  obtain rfl : ξ = fun i => GlobalTheory.SmGL.unrChar _ (α i) := funext hα
  obtain ⟨v, hv, hv0⟩ := (Submodule.ne_bot_iff _).1
    (GlobalTheory.PrinSat.hasSatGL_of_equiv e (GlobalTheory.PrinSat.hasSatGL_prinSeries w α)).1
  exact ⟨v, hv0, GlobalTheory.LGrp.mem_fixed.1 hv⟩
end UpperTheory.V.Z.Desc.W
end

namespace UpperTheory.V.Z.Win

variable {F Ω : Type} [Field F] [NumberField F] [Field Ω] [Algebra F Ω] {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {n : ℕ}

def IsAutCsdA [IsCMField F] (ι : Qbar l ≃+* ℂ) (r : G F Ω →* GL (Fin n) (Qbar l)) : Prop :=
  AutCsd0 ι r ∧ (Continuous r → WitA ι r)

omit [NumberField F] in

def IsAutQA (ι : Qbar l ≃+* ℂ) (r : G F Ω →* GL (Fin n) (Qbar l)) : Prop :=
  Continuous r → ∀ (K : Type) [Field K] [NumberField K] [IsCMField K] [Algebra F K] [Algebra K Ω] [IsScalarTower F K Ω],
    Module.finrank F K = 2 → IsIrr (r.comp (AlgEquiv.restrictScalarsHom F : G K Ω →* G F Ω)) →
      (∃ π : CuspSub K n, (CG.mk π).IsRegAlg ∧ AttC ι (r.comp (AlgEquiv.restrictScalarsHom F : G K Ω →* G F Ω)) π) ∧
        WitA ι (r.comp (AlgEquiv.restrictScalarsHom F : G K Ω →* G F Ω))

example : type_of% @IsAutCsd := @IsAutCsdA
example : type_of% @IsAutQ := @IsAutQA
example : type_of% @Wit := @WitA
example : type_of% @WitQ := @WitAQ

end UpperTheory.V.Z.Win


[HPT_SRC_GUARD] Unl1, PotUnl, WitA and retained RowT
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/ModularityArgument/AutomorphyLifting/WeakOfAtt.lean#L1-L53

open NumberField IsDedekindDomain Formalism.L2.Host Formalism.ConjugateSelfDual ReciprocityTheory.Gal Formalism.BasicNotions Formalism.MainNotions.N Polynomial GlobalTheory.CuspRep GlobalTheory.WeilDeligne
open GlobalTheory.PRep GlobalTheory.SmGL GlobalTheory.SplitWeil GlobalTheory.LocGL GlobalTheory.LocLanglands GlobalTheory.GalOfCusp UpperTheory.Zero.LLC UpperTheory.V.Af UpperTheory.V.Z.Gam

attribute [local instance] GlobalTheory.LocalData.complRel GlobalTheory.LocalData.isLocalField

namespace UpperTheory.V.Z.Win

variable {F Ω : Type} [Field F] [NumberField F] [Field Ω] [Algebra F Ω] {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {n : ℕ}

def Unl1 (r : G F Ω →* GL (Fin n) (Qbar l)) (v : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F)) : Prop :=
  ∀ A : ValuationSubring Ω, IsPlaceOver A v → ∃ κ : Fin n → ↥(Dec (F := F) A) →* (Qbar l)ˣ, IsFlagOn r (Dec (F := F) A) κ ∧
    ∀ i j, κ i ≠ κ j * (cycBar F Ω l).comp (Dec (F := F) A).subtype

def PotUnl (r : G F Ω →* GL (Fin n) (Qbar l)) (v : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F)) : Prop :=
  ∀ A : ValuationSubring Ω, IsPlaceOver A v → ∃ κ : Fin n → ↥(Dec (F := F) A) →* (Qbar l)ˣ, IsFlagOn r (Dec (F := F) A) κ ∧
    ∀ i j (m : ℕ), 1 ≤ m → κ i ^ m ≠ (κ j * (cycBar F Ω l).comp (Dec (F := F) A).subtype) ^ m

def WitA [IsCMField F] (ι : Qbar l ≃+* ℂ) (r : G F Ω →* GL (Fin n) (Qbar l)) : Prop :=
  (∀ v : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F), ((l : ℕ) : 𝓞 F) ∈ v.asIdeal → Unl1 r v → PotUnl r v) ∧
    ∀ π : CuspSub F n, (CG.mk π).IsRegAlg → AttC ι r π → ∃ t : ℝ, ∀ v : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F),
      (((l : ℕ) : 𝓞 F) ∉ v.asIdeal ∨ PotUnl r v) → WinAt π t v

def IneAb (r : G F Ω →* GL (Fin n) (Qbar l)) : Prop :=
  ∀ v : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F), ((l : ℕ) : 𝓞 F) ∉ v.asIdeal → ∀ A : ValuationSubring Ω, IsPlaceOver A v →
    ∃ κ : Fin n → ↥(Dec (F := F) A) →* (Qbar l)ˣ, (∀ i, Continuous (κ i)) ∧
      ∀ g : ↥(Dec (F := F) A), (g : G F Ω) ∈ Ine (F := F) A →
        (r g : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) (Qbar l)).charpoly = ∏ i, (X - C (κ i g : Qbar l))

def Flagged (r : G F Ω →* GL (Fin n) (Qbar l)) : Prop :=
  ∀ v : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F), ((l : ℕ) : 𝓞 F) ∈ v.asIdeal → ∀ A : ValuationSubring Ω, IsPlaceOver A v →
    ∃ κ : Fin n → ↥(Dec (F := F) A) →* (Qbar l)ˣ, IsFlagOn r (Dec (F := F) A) κ

def Keeps (r : G F Ω →* GL (Fin n) (Qbar l)) (L : Type) [Field L] [NumberField L] [Algebra F L] [Algebra L Ω]
    [IsScalarTower F L Ω] : Prop :=
  ∀ w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 L), ((l : ℕ) : 𝓞 L) ∈ w.asIdeal → Unl1 r (w.under (𝓞 F)) →
    Unl1 (r.comp (AlgEquiv.restrictScalarsHom F : G L Ω →* G F Ω)) w

def RowT : Prop := UpperTheory.GalStar.Row0b fun h rc => IsRecT h rc

def PlainRec : Prop :=
  ∀ (E Ω : Type) [Field E] [NumberField E] [IsTotallyComplex E] [Field Ω] [Algebra E Ω] [IsAlgClosure E Ω] (A : ValuationSubring Ω)
    (w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E)) (h : IsPlaceOver A w) (rc : IrrGL (w.adicCompletion E) → WD h), IsRecT h rc →
    ∀ (a : ℕ) (π : CuspSub E a) (t : ℝ), WinAt π t w → ∀ σ, π.IsLocComp w σ →
      GlobalTheory.RoadU.PlainTor (IrrGL.of σ.ρ σ.smooth σ.irred) ∧ UpperTheory.GalStar.Tor (rc (algNorm σ)) ∧ (rc (algNorm σ)).mono0

import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.ModularityArgument.CuspidalDescent.SphericalTwists.UnramifiedTwists.SameOf

private theorem satLink_of (P : _root_.UpperTheory.V.Af.Hypotheses') : SatLink := by
  intro E _ _ _ a π
  refine ⟨fun w σ hσ B => (_root_.UpperTheory.V.Af.satake_local_of_printed_results P E a w σ).2 _ π.finPart ((_root_.UpperTheory.V.Af.local_comp_complex_of_printed_results P E a π w).2 σ hσ) B, ?_⟩
  exact ExLib.eventually_sat (_root_.UpperTheory.V.Af.satake_local_of_printed_results P) π.finPart π.isSmooth_finPart fun w =>
    let ⟨⟨σ, hσ⟩, hi⟩ := _root_.UpperTheory.V.Af.local_comp_complex_of_printed_results P E a π w
    ⟨σ, hi σ hσ⟩
private theorem ev_notMem {K : Type} [Field K] [NumberField K] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] :
    ∀ᶠ v : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 K) in Filter.cofinite, ((l : ℕ) : 𝓞 K) ∉ v.asIdeal := by
  have h0 : Ideal.span {((l : ℕ) : 𝓞 K)} ≠ 0 := by
    rw [Ne, Ideal.zero_eq_bot, Ideal.span_singleton_eq_bot]
    exact_mod_cast (Fact.out : l.Prime).ne_zero
  exact (Ideal.finite_factors h0).subset fun v hv => Ideal.dvd_span_singleton.mpr (not_not.1 hv)
private theorem algSat_inj {L : Type} [Field L] [NumberField L] (w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 L)) (n : ℕ) {B B' : Multiset ℂˣ}
    (h : ((algSat n (normV w) B).map fun b => X - C b).prod = ((algSat n (normV w) B').map fun b => X - C b).prod) :
    B = B' := by
  have h3 := congrArg Polynomial.roots h
  rw [roots_multiset_prod_X_sub_C, roots_multiset_prod_X_sub_C] at h3
  have hq : (0 : ℝ) < (normV w : ℕ) := Nat.cast_pos.2 (lt_trans one_pos (one_lt_normV w))
  have h4 := Multiset.map_injective
    (mul_right_injective₀ (Complex.ofReal_ne_zero.2 (Real.rpow_pos_of_pos hq (((n : ℝ) - 1) / 2)).ne')) (a₁ := B.map Units.val)
    (a₂ := B'.map Units.val) (by simpa [algSat, Multiset.map_map] using h3)
  exact Multiset.map_injective Units.val_injective h4
private theorem eq_of_split {L Ω : Type} [Field L] [NumberField L] [Field Ω] [Algebra L Ω] [IsAlgClosure L Ω] {l : ℕ}
    [Fact l.Prime] {n : ℕ} [IsCMField L]
    (hE : ∀ w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 L), Algebra.IsUnramifiedAt (𝓞 (maximalRealSubfield L)) w.asIdeal)
    (r : G L Ω →* GL (Fin n) (Qbar l)) (hr : Continuous r) (f : G L Ω → (Qbar l)[X])
    (hf : ∀ j, Continuous fun g => (f g).coeff j)
    (hw : ∀ᶠ w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 L) in Filter.cofinite, IsSplitAt w → ∀ A : ValuationSubring Ω, IsPlaceOver A w →
      ∀ σ : G L Ω, IsFrob A (normV w) σ → (r σ⁻¹ : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) (Qbar l)).charpoly = f σ) (g : G L Ω) :
    (r g⁻¹ : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) (Qbar l)).charpoly = f g := by
  have contCoeff : ∀ i : ℕ, Continuous fun M : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) (Qbar l) => M.charpoly.coeff i := fun i => by
    have h : (fun M : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) (Qbar l) => M.charpoly.coeff i) =
        fun M => MvPolynomial.eval (fun ij : Fin n × Fin n => M ij.1 ij.2) ((Matrix.charpoly.univ (Qbar l) (Fin n)).coeff i) := by
      funext M
      exact (Matrix.charpoly.univ_coeff_eval₂Hom (Fin n) (RingHom.id _) (fun ij : Fin n × Fin n => M ij.1 ij.2) i).symm
    rw [h]
    exact (MvPolynomial.continuous_eval _).comp (continuous_pi fun ij => (continuous_apply ij.2).comp (continuous_apply ij.1))
  have hD := GlobalTheory.Published.ChebotarevDegreeOne.dense (F := L) (Ω := Ω) GlobalTheory.Published.chebotarevDegreeOne'
    (Filter.eventually_cofinite.1 hw)
  ext i
  have h1 : Continuous fun g : G L Ω => (r g⁻¹ : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) (Qbar l)).charpoly.coeff i :=
    (contCoeff i).comp (Units.continuous_val.comp (hr.comp continuous_inv))
  refine congrFun (Continuous.ext_on hD h1 (hf i) ?_) g
  rintro σ ⟨v, hv, hd, A, hA, hσ⟩
  have h := not_not.1 hv (UpperTheory.isSplitAt_of_unramified_of_inertiaDeg_eq_one L v (hE v) hd) A hA σ hσ
  simp only [h]
private theorem spehInf_one' (m : Multiset ℂ) : spehInf 1 m = m := by
  simp [spehInf]
private theorem satTot_one {E : Type} [Field E] [NumberField E] (w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E)) (S : Fin 1 → Multiset ℂˣ) :
    satTot w (fun _ => 1) S = S 0 := by
  simp [satTot, GlobalTheory.WeilDeligne.qPow_zero]
theorem UpperTheory.V.Z.Desc.W.same_of_statement :
    _root_.UpperTheory.V.Z.Desc.W.SameOfAlt := by
  intro Pr hBc hTw hSum hSp hOne E Ω _ _ _ _ _ _ hE l _ ι n hn π r hc hatt U hU hsat
  have hLink := satLink_of Pr
  obtain ⟨c, a, d, hsum, P, ρ, m, σ, hP, hS, hsplit⟩ := hBc E hE n hn U hU
  have hint : ∀ i (k : Fin (d i)) τ, (m i τ).Nodup ∧ ∀ z ∈ m i τ, ∃ j : ℤ, z + (ex n (d i) k : ℂ) / 2 = j := fun i k τ => by
    obtain ⟨lam, hl⟩ := GlobalTheory.T.exists_algebraicWeight_of_automorphic_definiteUnitary E n hn U hU τ
    exact (UpperTheory.speh_parameter_strings n c d (m · τ) lam (hP.weight τ lam hl)).1 i k
  obtain ⟨f, hfc, hf⟩ := hSum E Ω l ι n c a d P m (fun i => (hP.pos i).1) hP.csd hP.inf hint
  have hev : ∀ᶠ w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E) in Filter.cofinite, ((l : ℕ) : 𝓞 E) ∉ w.asIdeal ∧
      ∃ S : Fin c → Multiset ℂˣ, ∀ i, (CG.mk (P i)).HasSat w (S i) := by
    filter_upwards [ev_notMem (K := E) l, Filter.eventually_all.2 fun i => (hLink E _ (P i)).2] with w h1 h2
    exact ⟨h1, Classical.skolem.1 h2⟩
  have hall := eq_of_split hE r hc f hfc (by
    filter_upwards [hsat, hatt.2, hev] with w h1 ⟨b, hb, hF⟩ ⟨hw, S, hS'⟩ hs A hA s hs'
    obtain ⟨τ, b', h2, h3, h4⟩ := h1 hs
    obtain rfl := hb.unique h4
    obtain rfl := hSp Pr.litN E n c a d hsum w (ρ · w) (σ · w) hP.pos (fun i => (hS i w).2.2) S
      (fun i => ((hLink E _ (P i)).1 w _ (hP.loc i w) _).1 (hS' i)) τ
      (UpperTheory.building_component_uniqueness E n U hU w hs _ _ _ _ h2 (hsplit w hs)) b h3
    exact Polynomial.map_injective ι.toRingHom ι.injective (((hF A hA).2 s hs').trans (hf w hw S hS' A hA s hs').symm))
  have hπ : ∀ᶠ w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E) in Filter.cofinite, ∃ S : Fin c → Multiset ℂˣ,
      (∀ i, (CG.mk (P i)).HasSat w (S i)) ∧ (CG.mk π).HasSat w (satTot w d S) := by
    filter_upwards [hatt.2, hev] with w ⟨b, hb, hF⟩ ⟨hw, S, hS'⟩
    obtain ⟨A, hA⟩ : ∃ A : ValuationSubring Ω, IsPlaceOver A w := exists_isPlaceOver Ω w
    obtain ⟨s, hs⟩ := exists_isFrob hA
    have h1 := (hF A hA).2 s hs
    rw [hall s, hf w hw S hS' A hA s hs] at h1
    exact ⟨S, hS', algSat_inj w n h1 ▸ hb⟩
  obtain ⟨rfl, hd⟩ := hOne Pr.litN.aut.js.1 hTw E n c hn a d P π hP.pos hπ
  obtain rfl : d = fun _ => 1 := funext hd
  have hn' : a 0 = n := by simpa using hsum
  obtain rfl : a = fun _ => n := funext fun i => by rw [Subsingleton.elim i 0, hn']
  obtain rfl : π = P 0 := CuspSub.ext (strongMultOneC E n π (P 0) (by
    filter_upwards [hπ] with w ⟨S, h1, h2⟩
    exact ⟨S 0, satTot_one w S ▸ h2, h1 0⟩))
  refine ⟨fun w hw τ hτ => ?_, fun φ => ?_⟩
  · refine Z.Desc.irrComp E n U hsum (P 0) w (ρ 0 w) (σ 0 w) (hP.loc 0 w) (hS 0 w).2.2 ?_ τ hτ
    have e : (σ · w) = fun _ => σ 0 w := funext fun i => by rw [Subsingleton.elim i 0]
    have key : ∀ s s' : Fin 1 → SmIrr (GL (Fin (1 * n)) (w.adicCompletion E)), s = s' →
        IsCompAlong U (locU n w) ((cLoc w).comp (locU n w).subtype) (locProd (N := fun _ : Fin 1 => 1 * n) hsum w s) →
        IsCompAlong U (locU n w) ((cLoc w).comp (locU n w).subtype) (locProd (N := fun _ : Fin 1 => 1 * n) hsum w s') := by
      rintro s _ rfl h
      exact h
    exact key _ _ e (hsplit w hw)
  · obtain ⟨lam, hl⟩ := GlobalTheory.T.exists_algebraicWeight_of_automorphic_definiteUnitary E n hn U hU φ
    have e := hP.weight φ lam hl
    rw [Fin.sum_univ_one, spehInf_one'] at e
    exact ⟨lam, hl, e ▸ hP.inf 0 φ⟩
end


[HPT_SRC_JS] Retained JacquetShalikaRigidity exact isobaric family scope
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AbelianVarietyTheory/PublishedTheorems/GaloisCohomology₂/StrongMultiplicityOne.lean#L189-L197
def JacquetShalikaRigidity : Prop :=
  ∀ (E : Type) [Field E] [NumberField E] [IsTotallyComplex E] (ι κ : Type) [Fintype ι] [Fintype κ]
    (m : ι → ℕ) (m' : κ → ℕ) (π : ∀ i, CG E (m i)) (π' : ∀ j, CG E (m' j)),
    (∀ i, 1 ≤ m i) → (∀ j, 1 ≤ m' j) →
    (∀ᶠ w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E) in Filter.cofinite, ∃ (B : ι → Multiset ℂˣ) (B' : κ → Multiset ℂˣ),
      (∀ i, (π i).HasSat w (B i)) ∧ (∀ j, (π' j).HasSat w (B' j)) ∧ ∑ i, B i = ∑ j, B' j) →
    ∃ s : ι ≃ κ, ∀ i, (⟨m i, π i⟩ : Σ a, CG E a) = ⟨m' (s i), π' (s i)⟩
