private theorem locU_congr {L : Type} [Field L] [NumberField L] [IsCMField L] {n : ℕ} {w u : Plc L}
    (h : u.under (𝓞 (Lp L)) = w.under (𝓞 (Lp L))) {x : UA L n} (hx : x ∈ locU n w) : x ∈ locU n u := by
  unfold locU at hx ⊢
  rw [h]
  exact hx

private theorem local_retraction_component_cases {L : Type} [Field L] [NumberField L] [IsCMField L] {n : ℕ} (w u : Plc L) (x : UA L n) :
    cLoc u (GlobalTheory.DefU.LocRet.ret0 w n x) = cLoc u x ∨ cLoc u (GlobalTheory.DefU.LocRet.ret0 w n x) = 1 := by
  by_cases h : u.under (𝓞 (Lp L)) = w.under (𝓞 (Lp L))
  · exact Or.inl (GlobalTheory.DefU.LocRet.localProj_unitaryAdelicLocalRetractionEnd_of_under_eq h x)
  · exact Or.inr (GlobalTheory.DefU.LocRet.localProj_unitaryAdelicLocalRetractionEnd_of_under_ne h x)

private theorem local_retraction_mem_comap {L : Type} [Field L] [NumberField L] [IsCMField L] {n : ℕ} (w u : Plc L) (x : UA L n)
    {H : Subgroup (GL (Fin n) (u.adicCompletion L))} (hx : cLoc u x ∈ H) : cLoc u (GlobalTheory.DefU.LocRet.ret0 w n x) ∈ H := by
  rcases local_retraction_component_cases w u x with h | h <;> rw [h]
  · exact hx
  · exact one_mem _

private theorem local_retraction_eq_one {L : Type} [Field L] [NumberField L] [IsCMField L] {n : ℕ} (w u : Plc L) (x : UA L n)
    (hx : cLoc u x = 1) : cLoc u (GlobalTheory.DefU.LocRet.ret0 w n x) = 1 :=
  (local_retraction_component_cases w u x).elim (fun h => h.trans hx) id

private theorem ne_zero_of_irr {G H W M : Type} [Group G] [Group H] [AddCommGroup W] [Module ℂ W] [AddCommGroup M] [Module ℂ M]
    (p : G →* H) (hp : Function.Surjective p) (ρ : Representation ℂ H W) (hρ : ρ.IsIrreducible) (R : G → M →ₗ[ℂ] M)
    (f : W →ₗ[ℂ] M) (hf : f ≠ 0) (he : ∀ x v, f (ρ (p x) v) = R x (f v)) {v : W} (hv : v ≠ 0) : f v ≠ 0 := by
  intro h0
  let S : Subrepresentation ρ := ⟨LinearMap.ker f, fun g y hy => by
    obtain ⟨x, rfl⟩ := hp g
    rw [LinearMap.mem_ker] at hy ⊢
    rw [he, hy, map_zero]⟩
  rcases hρ.eq_bot_or_eq_top S with h | h
  · have h1 : LinearMap.ker f = ⊥ := congrArg Subrepresentation.toSubmodule h
    exact hv (by simpa [h1] using (LinearMap.mem_ker.2 h0))
  · have h1 : LinearMap.ker f = ⊤ := congrArg Subrepresentation.toSubmodule h
    exact hf (LinearMap.ker_eq_top.1 h1)

theorem UpperTheory.exists_nonzero_form_fixed_by_level_of_unramified :
    ∀ (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (L Ω : Type) [Field L] [NumberField L] [IsCMField L] [Field Ω] [Algebra L Ω] [IsAlgClosure L Ω] (s : HidaSetting l L Ω) (n : ℕ), 1 ≤ n → ∀ (V : s.Lev) (Q : QData V) (σ : Submodule ℂ (UA L n → ℂ)), IsAutRepU σ → (∀ w : Plc L, w.under (𝓞 (Lp L)) ∉ V.S' → IsUnramU σ w) → (∃ τ : SmIrr (GLw n (s.w s.v0)), IsCompAlong σ (locU n (s.w s.v0)) ((cLoc (s.w s.v0)).comp (locU n (s.w s.v0)).subtype) τ.ρ ∧ fixd τ.ρ (Kc _ n 1) ≠ ⊥) → ∃ φ ∈ σ, φ ≠ 0 ∧ ∀ k ∈ GlobalTheory.UnitaryBC.finU L n, (∀ u : Plc L, u.under (𝓞 (Lp L)) ∉ V.S' → cLoc u k ∈ KO (u.adicCompletion L) (Fin n)) → cLoc (s.w s.v0) k ∈ Kc _ n 1 → (∀ v : V.frame.Vl, cLoc (s.w v.1) k = 1) → (∀ u : s.St, cLoc (s.w u) k = 1) → (∀ q : Q.Q, cLoc (Q.wq q) k = 1) → RU L n k φ = φ := by
  intro l _ L Ω _ _ _ _ _ _ s n hn V Q σ hσ hun hτ
  obtain ⟨hund, hspl⟩ := UpperTheory.under_chosen_place_and_isSplitAt_of_decomp_le l L Ω s
  have hsplit : ∀ w : Plc L, w.under (𝓞 (Lp L)) ∈ V.S' → IsSplitAt w := fun w hw => hspl w (V.split _ hw)
  let K : Subgroup (UA L n) :=
    { carrier := {k | k ∈ GlobalTheory.UnitaryBC.finU L n ∧
        (∀ u : Plc L, u.under (𝓞 (Lp L)) ∉ V.S' → cLoc u k ∈ KO (u.adicCompletion L) (Fin n)) ∧
        cLoc (s.w s.v0) k ∈ Kc _ n 1 ∧ (∀ v : V.frame.Vl, cLoc (s.w v.1) k = 1) ∧ (∀ u : s.St, cLoc (s.w u) k = 1) ∧
        (∀ q : Q.Q, cLoc (Q.wq q) k = 1)}
      one_mem' := ⟨one_mem _, fun u _ => by rw [map_one]; exact one_mem _, by rw [map_one]; exact one_mem _,
        fun _ => map_one _, fun _ => map_one _, fun _ => map_one _⟩
      mul_mem' := fun {a b} ha hb => ⟨mul_mem ha.1 hb.1,
        fun u hu => by rw [map_mul]; exact mul_mem (ha.2.1 u hu) (hb.2.1 u hu),
        by rw [map_mul]; exact mul_mem ha.2.2.1 hb.2.2.1,
        fun v => by rw [map_mul, ha.2.2.2.1 v, hb.2.2.2.1 v, one_mul],
        fun v => by rw [map_mul, ha.2.2.2.2.1 v, hb.2.2.2.2.1 v, one_mul],
        fun v => by rw [map_mul, ha.2.2.2.2.2 v, hb.2.2.2.2.2 v, one_mul]⟩
      inv_mem' := fun {a} ha => ⟨inv_mem ha.1,
        fun u hu => by rw [map_inv]; exact inv_mem (ha.2.1 u hu),
        by rw [map_inv]; exact inv_mem ha.2.2.1,
        fun v => by rw [map_inv, ha.2.2.2.1 v, inv_one],
        fun v => by rw [map_inv, ha.2.2.2.2.1 v, inv_one],
        fun v => by rw [map_inv, ha.2.2.2.2.2 v, inv_one]⟩ }
  have hKO : ∀ u : Plc L, KO (u.adicCompletion L) (Fin n) = GlobalTheory.LFun.KGL u n :=
    fun u => GlobalTheory.SmGL.SphCompletion.KO_completion u n
  have hT : ((fun w : Plc L => w.under (𝓞 (Lp L))) ⁻¹' V.S').Finite := by
    refine V.fin.preimage' fun v _ => ?_
    have := v.isMaximal
    refine ((IsDedekindDomain.primesOver_finite v.asIdeal (𝓞 L)).preimage
      (f := fun w : Plc L => w.asIdeal) (fun a _ b _ h => HeightOneSpectrum.ext h)).subset ?_
    rintro w rfl
    exact ⟨w.isPrime, ⟨rfl⟩⟩
  have key := UpperTheory.exists_fixed_vector_of_local_fixed_vectors L n σ hσ K (fun k hk => hk.1)
    (fun w k hk => ⟨(Subgroup.mem_inf.1 (GlobalTheory.DefU.LocRet.unitaryAdelicLocalRetractionEnd_mem_localPoints k)).1,
      fun u hu => local_retraction_mem_comap w u k (hk.2.1 u hu), local_retraction_mem_comap w _ k hk.2.2.1,
      fun v => local_retraction_eq_one w _ k (hk.2.2.2.1 v), fun v => local_retraction_eq_one w _ k (hk.2.2.2.2.1 v),
      fun v => local_retraction_eq_one w _ k (hk.2.2.2.2.2 v)⟩) _ hT
    (fun k hk u hu => hKO u ▸ hk.2.1 u hu) ?_
  · obtain ⟨φ, h1, h2, h3⟩ := key
    exact ⟨φ, h1, h2, fun k a b c d e f => h3 k ⟨a, b, c, d, e, f⟩⟩
  intro w
  have htriv : ∀ u : Plc L, u.under (𝓞 (Lp L)) = w.under (𝓞 (Lp L)) → w.under (𝓞 (Lp L)) ∈ V.S' →
      (∀ k ∈ K, cLoc u k = 1) → ∃ φ ∈ σ, φ ≠ 0 ∧ ∀ k ∈ K, k ∈ locU n w → RU L n k φ = φ := by
    intro u hu hw h1
    obtain ⟨φ, hφ, h0⟩ := Submodule.exists_mem_ne_zero_of_ne_bot hσ.ne_bot
    refine ⟨φ, hφ, h0, fun k hk hl => ?_⟩
    have h2 : k = 1 := congrArg Subtype.val
      (GlobalTheory.SplitPts.eq_one_of_cLoc (hsplit u (hu ▸ hw)) ⟨k, locU_congr hu hl⟩ (h1 k hk))
    rw [h2, map_one]
    rfl
  by_cases hw : w.under (𝓞 (Lp L)) ∈ V.S'
  · have hw' := hw
    rw [Q.range] at hw'
    rcases hw' with (h | h | h) | ⟨q, h⟩
    · exact htriv (s.w _) (hund _) hw fun k hk => hk.2.2.2.1 ⟨_, h⟩
    · exact htriv (s.w _) (hund _) hw fun k hk => hk.2.2.2.2.1 ⟨_, h⟩
    · obtain ⟨τ, ⟨hirr, f, hf, hfσ, he⟩, hfix⟩ := hτ
      have hu : (s.w s.v0).under (𝓞 (Lp L)) = w.under (𝓞 (Lp L)) := (hund _).trans h.symm
      obtain ⟨⟨j, hj1, hj2, -⟩, -⟩ := UpperTheory.V.Hi.secU l L Ω s n hn (s.w s.v0) (hsplit _ (hu ▸ hw))
      obtain ⟨v, hv, hv0⟩ := Submodule.exists_mem_ne_zero_of_ne_bot hfix
      refine ⟨f v, hfσ v, ne_zero_of_irr _ (fun g => ⟨⟨j g, hj1 g⟩, hj2 g⟩) τ.ρ hirr
        (fun x => RU L n (x : locU n (s.w s.v0))) f hf he hv0, fun k hk hl => ?_⟩
      rw [← he ⟨k, locU_congr hu hl⟩ v]
      exact congrArg f (hv _ hk.2.2.1)
    · exact htriv (Q.wq q) h hw fun k hk => hk.2.2.2.2.2 q
  · obtain ⟨φ, h1, h2, h3⟩ := hun w hw
    exact ⟨φ, h1, h2, fun k hk hl => h3 k hl (hKO w ▸ hk.2.1 w hw)⟩


[HPT_SRC_FIXEDLOCAL] Original local-stable-subspace fixed-vector transfer
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/BuildingsResidueChars/ExistsFixedVectorInLocallyStableSubspace.lean#L1-L50
import Mathlib
import Development.AbelianVarietyTheory.Proofs.IwasawaDecomposition.ExistsAdmissibleLocalComponentDefiniteUnitary
import Development.AbelianVarietyTheory.Proofs.IwasawaDecomposition.IrreducibleRepIsotypicOfCentraliserFactor
import Development.AbelianVarietyTheory.Proofs.IwasawaDecomposition.ExistsContinuousRetractionOntoUnitaryLocalPoints
import Development.AbelianVarietyTheory.Proofs.InertiaEigenvalues.SatakeParametersUnramifiedUnitary
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.AutomorphicForms.TemperedLocalComponents.GlobFixed
open NumberField IsDedekindDomain GlobalTheory.CuspRep GlobalTheory.UnitaryBC Formalism.BasicNotions
open scoped TensorProduct

theorem UpperTheory.exists_fixed_vector_in_locally_stable_subspace :
    ∀ (E : Type) [Field E] [NumberField E] [IsCMField E] (n : ℕ) (σ : Submodule ℂ (UA E n → ℂ)), IsAutRepU σ → ∀ (w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 E)) (K : Subgroup (UA E n)), K ≤ locU n w → (∃ φ ∈ σ, φ ≠ 0 ∧ ∀ k ∈ K, RU E n k φ = φ) → ∀ W : Submodule ℂ (UA E n → ℂ), W ≤ σ → W ≠ ⊥ → (∀ x ∈ locU n w, ∀ φ ∈ W, RU E n x φ ∈ W) → ∃ φ ∈ W, φ ≠ 0 ∧ ∀ k ∈ K, RU E n k φ = φ := by
  intro E _ _ _ n π hπ w K hK hu W hWπ hW0 hW
  obtain ⟨φ, hφ, hφ0, hφK⟩ := hu
  let Ψ : GlobalTheory.DefU.AutSub E n := ⟨π, hπ⟩
  obtain ⟨τ, -, hirr, f, hf0, hfm, hf⟩ := GlobalTheory.DefU.LocAdm.exists_admissible_comp E n Ψ w
  let ρ : Representation ℂ (locU n w) Ψ.V := Ψ.rep.comp (locU n w).subtype
  let F : Representation.IntertwiningMap τ.ρ ρ :=
    ⟨f.codRestrict _ hfm, fun x => LinearMap.ext fun v => Subtype.ext (hf x v)⟩
  have hF0 : F ≠ 0 := fun h => hf0 (LinearMap.ext fun v => by
    have := congrArg Subtype.val (DFunLike.congr_fun h v)
    exact this)
  obtain ⟨r, -, -, hr⟩ := GlobalTheory.DefU.LocRet.exists_ret E n w
  have htop := (GlobalTheory.CuspRep.CompUniq.isotypic (UA E n) (locU n w) Ψ.V τ.V τ.V Ψ.rep (locU n w).subtype
    τ.ρ τ.ρ Ψ.isIrreducible_rep hirr hirr (fun g => ⟨r g, fun y => hr g y⟩) F hF0).1
  have hfi : ∀ (i : Representation.IntertwiningMap τ.ρ ρ) g u, i.toLinearMap (τ.ρ g u) = ρ g (i.toLinearMap u) :=
    fun i g u => Representation.IntertwiningMap.isIntertwining _ _ i g u
  let K' : Subgroup (locU n w) := K.comap (locU n w).subtype
  have hfix : ∃ v : τ.V, v ≠ 0 ∧ ∀ k ∈ K', τ.ρ k v = v := by
    by_contra hn
    have key := GlobalTheory.InertHeckeEigen.mem_of_mem_iSup ρ τ.ρ hirr
      (fun i : Representation.IntertwiningMap τ.ρ ρ => i.toLinearMap) hfi (K' : Set (locU n w)) ⊥
      (fun i v hv => by
        have hv' : v = 0 := by
          by_contra h
          exact hn ⟨v, h, hv⟩
        rw [hv', map_zero]
        exact Submodule.zero_mem _)
      (fun z hz _ _ => by rw [(Submodule.mem_bot ℂ).1 hz, map_zero]) ⟨φ, hφ⟩
      (fun k hk => Subtype.ext (hφK k.1 hk)) (by rw [htop]; exact Submodule.mem_top)
    exact hφ0 (congrArg Subtype.val ((Submodule.mem_bot ℂ).1 key))
  obtain ⟨v, hvW, hv0, hv⟩ := UpperTheory.V.Af.GlobalFixedVectors.sub_fixed τ.ρ hirr ρ
    (by
      rw [eq_top_iff, ← htop]
      exact iSup_le fun i => le_iSup_of_le ⟨i.toLinearMap, hfi i⟩ le_rfl)
    (W.comap π.subtype) (fun g v hv => hW g.1 g.2 v.1 hv)
    (fun e => hW0 ((Submodule.eq_bot_iff W).2 fun x hx => by
      have h : (⟨x, hWπ hx⟩ : π) ∈ W.comap π.subtype := hx
      rw [e] at h
      exact congrArg Subtype.val ((Submodule.mem_bot ℂ).1 h))) K' hfix
  exact ⟨v.1, hvW, fun e => hv0 (Subtype.ext e), fun k hk => congrArg Subtype.val (hv ⟨k, hK hk⟩ hk)⟩


[HPT_SRC_EMBEDDING_EXHAUSTION] Original actual infinity-embedding exhaustion used in nonzero tensor dual construction
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/BuildingsResidueChars/PlaceEmbeddingsBijectiveInfinitePlaces.lean#L68-L119
theorem UpperTheory.placeEmbeddings_bijective_infinitePlaces :
    ∀ (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (L Ω : Type) [Field L] [NumberField L] [IsCMField L] [Field Ω] [Algebra L Ω] [IsAlgClosure L Ω] (s : HidaSetting l L Ω) (V : s.Lev) (ι : Qbar l ≃+* ℂ), Finite (Emb l (fun v : V.frame.Vl => s.w v.1)) ∧ Function.Bijective (fun x : Emb l (fun v : V.frame.Vl => s.w v.1) => InfinitePlace.mk (ι.toRingHom.comp x.res)) := by
  intro l _ L Ω _ _ _ _ _ _ s V ι
  obtain ⟨hun, hsp⟩ := UpperTheory.under_chosen_place_and_isSplitAt_of_decomp_le l L Ω s
  have hinj : Function.Injective
      (fun x : Emb l (fun v : V.frame.Vl => s.w v.1) => InfinitePlace.mk (ι.toRingHom.comp x.res)) := by
    rintro ⟨v, j⟩ ⟨v', j'⟩ h
    rcases InfinitePlace.mk_eq_iff.1 h with h | h
    · have h1 : ∀ x : L, j.1 (algebraMap L _ x) = j'.1 (algebraMap L _ x) := fun x =>
        ι.injective (RingHom.congr_fun h x)
      have h2 : s.w v.1 = s.w v'.1 := HeightOneSpectrum.ext (Ideal.ext fun x => by
        rw [mem_iff j, mem_iff j', h1])
      have h3 : v = v' := Subtype.ext (by rw [← hun v.1, h2, hun])
      subst h3
      refine congrArg (Sigma.mk v) (Subtype.ext (DFunLike.coe_injective ?_))
      exact (HeightOneSpectrum.denseRange_algebraMap L (s.w v.1)).equalizer j.2 j'.2 (funext h1)
    · have h1 : ∀ x : L, j.1 (algebraMap L _ (IsCMField.complexConj L x)) = j'.1 (algebraMap L _ x) := fun x =>
        ι.injective ((IsCMField.complexEmbedding_complexConj L _ x).trans (RingHom.congr_fun h x))
      have h2 : ∀ x, IsCMField.ringOfIntegersComplexConj L x ∈ (s.w v.1).asIdeal ↔ x ∈ (s.w v'.1).asIdeal := fun x => by
        rw [mem_iff j, mem_iff j', ← h1]
        rfl
      have h3 : v = v' := Subtype.ext (by
        rw [← hun v.1, ← hun v'.1]
        refine HeightOneSpectrum.ext (Ideal.ext fun y => ?_)
        change algebraMap _ (𝓞 L) y ∈ (s.w v.1).asIdeal ↔ algebraMap _ (𝓞 L) y ∈ (s.w v'.1).asIdeal
        rw [← h2, (IsCMField.ringOfIntegersComplexConj_eq_self_iff L _).2 ⟨y, rfl⟩])
      subst h3
      refine (not_stable (hsp (s.w v.1) ?_) fun x hx => (h2 x).1 hx).elim
      rw [hun]
      exact s.split _ (Or.inl v.2)
  refine ⟨Finite.of_injective _ hinj, hinj, fun p => ?_⟩
  obtain ⟨φ, rfl⟩ : ∃ φ, InfinitePlace.mk φ = p := ⟨_, p.mk_embedding⟩
  let τ : L →+* Qbar l := ι.symm.toRingHom.comp φ
  have hl : ((l : ℕ) : 𝓞 (Lp L)) ∈ ((GlobalTheory.EvExtend.placeOf τ).under (𝓞 (Lp L))).asIdeal := by
    change algebraMap _ (𝓞 L) ((l : ℕ) : 𝓞 (Lp L)) ∈ (GlobalTheory.EvExtend.placeOf τ).asIdeal
    rw [map_natCast, GlobalTheory.EvExtend.mem_placeOf]
    simpa using GlobalTheory.EvExtend.norm_natCast_lt_one (l := l)
  rcases two (hun ((GlobalTheory.EvExtend.placeOf τ).under (𝓞 (Lp L)))).symm with h | h
  · obtain ⟨j, hj⟩ := ex_emb (w := s.w ((GlobalTheory.EvExtend.placeOf τ).under (𝓞 (Lp L)))) τ fun x => by
      rw [← h, GlobalTheory.EvExtend.mem_placeOf]
    refine ⟨⟨⟨_, hl⟩, j⟩, congrArg InfinitePlace.mk (RingHom.ext fun x => ?_)⟩
    exact (congrArg ι (RingHom.congr_fun hj x)).trans (ι.apply_symm_apply _)
  · obtain ⟨j, hj⟩ := ex_emb (w := s.w ((GlobalTheory.EvExtend.placeOf τ).under (𝓞 (Lp L))))
      (τ.comp (IsCMField.complexConj L).toRingEquiv.toRingHom) fun x => by
        have h1 := h (IsCMField.ringOfIntegersComplexConj L x)
        rw [cc, GlobalTheory.EvExtend.mem_placeOf] at h1
        exact h1.symm
    refine ⟨⟨⟨_, hl⟩, j⟩, InfinitePlace.mk_eq_iff.2 (Or.inr (RingHom.ext fun x => ?_))⟩
    have h1 : ι (j.1 (algebraMap L _ x)) = φ (IsCMField.complexConj L x) :=
      (congrArg ι (RingHom.congr_fun hj x)).trans (ι.apply_symm_apply _)
    change starRingEnd ℂ (ι (j.1 (algebraMap L _ x))) = φ x
    rw [h1, IsCMField.complexEmbedding_complexConj, Complex.conj_conj]


[HPT_SRC_NONZERO] Actual nonempty_unitary_hecke_parts all-rank coefficient function construction
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/ModularityArgument/ThresholdArguments/DenseThresholdCases/AbsoluteIrreducibleRankFour.lean#L1-L273

private theorem neu_comm {L : Type} [Field L] [NumberField L] [IsCMField L] {n : ℕ} {w : Plc L} {j : GLw n w →* UA L n}
    (hj : ∀ x ∈ locU n w, x = j (cLoc w x)) {g y : UA L n} (hg : cLoc w g = 1) (hy : y ∈ locU n w) : Commute g y := by
  have h1 : (GlobalTheory.DefU.LocRet.ret w n g : UA L n) = 1 := by
    rw [hj _ (GlobalTheory.DefU.LocRet.ret w n g).2]
    have : cLoc w (GlobalTheory.DefU.LocRet.ret w n g : UA L n) = cLoc w g := GlobalTheory.DefU.LocRet.localProj_unitaryAdelicLocalRetractionEnd_of_under_eq rfl g
    rw [this, hg, map_one]
  have h2 := GlobalTheory.DefU.LocRet.commute_ret g ⟨y, hy⟩
  rwa [h1, inv_one, one_mul] at h2
private theorem ne_J {L : Type} [Field L] [NumberField L] [IsCMField L] {n : ℕ} {St : Type} [Fintype St] [DecidableEq St]
    {wt : St → Plc L} (hinj : Function.Injective fun u => (wt u).under (𝓞 (Lp L))) (j : ∀ u, GLw n (wt u) →* UA L n)
    (h1 : ∀ u g, j u g ∈ locU n (wt u)) (h2 : ∀ u g, cLoc (wt u) (j u g) = g) (h3 : ∀ u, ∀ x ∈ locU n (wt u), x = j u (cLoc (wt u) x)) :
    ∃ J : GV n wt →* UA L n, (∀ u g, J (Pi.mulSingle u g) = j u g) ∧ (∀ g, J g ∈ GlobalTheory.UnitaryBC.finU L n) ∧
      (∀ g u, cLoc (wt u) (J g) = g u) ∧
      (∀ g (v : Plc L), (∀ u, v.under (𝓞 (Lp L)) ≠ (wt u).under (𝓞 (Lp L))) → cLoc v (J g) = 1) ∧
      ∀ g k, (∀ u, cLoc (wt u) k = 1) → Commute k (J g) := by
  have h4 : ∀ u g (v : Plc L), v.under (𝓞 (Lp L)) ≠ (wt u).under (𝓞 (Lp L)) → cLoc v (j u g) = 1 := fun u g v hv =>
    GlobalTheory.DefU.LocRet.mem_locU_ne (h1 u g) hv
  have hc : Pairwise fun a b => ∀ x y, Commute (j a x) (j b y) := fun a b hab x y =>
    neu_comm (h3 b) (h4 a x _ fun e => hab (hinj e).symm) (h1 b y)
  let J := MonoidHom.noncommPiCoprod j hc
  have hJ : ∀ u g, J (Pi.mulSingle u g) = j u g := fun u g => MonoidHom.noncommPiCoprod_mulSingle j u g
  have key : ∀ P : Subgroup (UA L n), (∀ u g, j u g ∈ P) → ∀ g, J g ∈ P := fun P hP g => by
    have : J.range ≤ P := by
      rw [MonoidHom.noncommPiCoprod_range]
      exact iSup_le fun u => by rintro _ ⟨g, rfl⟩; exact hP u g
    exact this ⟨g, rfl⟩
  refine ⟨J, hJ, key _ fun u g => (h1 u g).1, fun g u => ?_, fun g v hv => key (cLoc v).ker (fun u g => h4 u g v (hv u)) g,
    fun g k hk => ?_⟩
  · have : (cLoc (wt u)).comp J = Pi.evalMonoidHom (fun u => GLw n (wt u)) u := MonoidHom.pi_ext fun i x => by
      rw [MonoidHom.comp_apply, hJ, Pi.evalMonoidHom_apply]
      by_cases h : u = i
      · subst h
        rw [h2, Pi.mulSingle_eq_same]
      · rw [Pi.mulSingle_eq_of_ne h]
        exact h4 i x _ fun e => h (hinj e)
    exact DFunLike.congr_fun this g
  · exact (Subgroup.mem_centralizer_singleton_iff.1
      (key (Subgroup.centralizer {k}) (fun u g => Subgroup.mem_centralizer_singleton_iff.2
        (neu_comm (h3 u) (hk u) (h1 u g)).symm.eq) g)).symm
private theorem ne_iso {L : Type} [Field L] [NumberField L] [IsCMField L] {n : ℕ} {w : Plc L} {j : GLw n w →* UA L n}
    (h1 : ∀ g, j g ∈ locU n w) (h2 : ∀ g, cLoc w (j g) = g) (h3 : ∀ x ∈ locU n w, x = j (cLoc w x))
    {σ : Submodule ℂ (UA L n → ℂ)} (hσ : IsAutRepU σ) (τ : SmIrr (GLw n w))
    (hτ : IsCompAlong σ (locU n w) ((cLoc w).comp (locU n w).subtype) τ.ρ)
    (M : Submodule ℂ (UA L n → ℂ)) (hM : M ≤ σ) (ρ : Representation ℂ (GLw n w) M) (hρ : ∀ g y, (ρ g y : UA L n → ℂ) = RU L n (j g) y) :
    IsoSum ρ ⊤ τ := by
  let π : GlobalTheory.DefU.AutSub L n := ⟨σ, hσ⟩
  obtain ⟨-, f, hf0, hfm, hf⟩ := hτ
  let F : Representation.IntertwiningMap τ.ρ (π.rep.comp j) :=
    ⟨f.codRestrict _ hfm, fun x => LinearMap.ext fun v => Subtype.ext (by
      have := hf ⟨j x, h1 x⟩ v
      rwa [show ((cLoc w).comp (locU n w).subtype) ⟨j x, h1 x⟩ = x from h2 x] at this)⟩
  have hF0 : F ≠ 0 := fun h => hf0 (LinearMap.ext fun v => congrArg Subtype.val (DFunLike.congr_fun h v))
  have htop := (GlobalTheory.CuspRep.CompUniq.isotypic (UA L n) (GLw n w) π.V τ.V τ.V π.rep j τ.ρ τ.ρ π.isIrreducible_rep τ.irred τ.irred
    (fun g => ⟨cLoc w g, fun y => neu_comm h3 (by rw [map_mul, map_inv, h2, inv_mul_cancel]) (h1 y)⟩) F hF0).1
  have h5 := UpperTheory.isotypic_of_jointly_injective_into_isotypic _ _ _ τ.ρ (π.rep.comp j) τ.irred (by
    refine top_le_iff.1 (htop.ge.trans (iSup_le fun f => ?_))
    exact le_iSup_of_le ⟨f.toLinearMap, fun g v => LinearMap.congr_fun (f.isIntertwining' g) v⟩ le_rfl)
    M ρ Unit (fun _ => Submodule.inclusion hM) (fun _ g y => Subtype.ext (hρ g y))
    (fun y hy => Submodule.inclusion_injective hM ((hy ()).trans (map_zero _).symm))
  refine h5.ge.trans (iSup_le fun f => ?_)
  exact le_iSup_of_le ⟨f.1, f.2, le_top⟩ le_rfl
private theorem ne_type {L Ω : Type} [Field L] [NumberField L] [IsCMField L] [Field Ω] [Algebra L Ω] [IsAlgClosure L Ω] {n m : ℕ}
    {St : Type} [Finite St] {wt : St → Plc L} {At : St → ValuationSubring Ω} (ht : ∀ u, IsPlaceOver (At u) (wt u))
    (hinj : Function.Injective fun u => (wt u).under (𝓞 (Lp L))) (j : ∀ u, GLw n (wt u) →* UA L n)
    (h1 : ∀ u g, j u g ∈ locU n (wt u)) (h2 : ∀ u g, cLoc (wt u) (j u g) = g) (h3 : ∀ u, ∀ x ∈ locU n (wt u), x = j u (cLoc (wt u) x))
    (τ₀ : KT n wt →* GL (Fin m) ℂ) (χ : ∀ u, Fin n → Ine (F := L) (At u) →* ℂˣ) (hT : _root_.UpperTheory.V.Hi.IsTypeAlt ht τ₀ χ)
    {σ : Submodule ℂ (UA L n → ℂ)} (hσ : IsAutRepU σ) (τ : ∀ u, SmIrr (GLw n (wt u)))
    (hτ : ∀ u, IsCompAlong σ (locU n (wt u)) ((cLoc (wt u)).comp (locU n (wt u)).subtype) (τ u).ρ)
    (hwd : ∀ u rc, _root_.UpperTheory.Zero.LLC.IsRecT (ht u) rc → IsTypeWD (ht u) (χ u) (rc (algNorm (τ u))))
    (K : Subgroup (UA L n)) (hK : ∀ k ∈ K, ∀ u, cLoc (wt u) k = 1) (h0 : σ ⊓ fixd (RU L n) K ≠ ⊥) :
    ∃ J : GV n wt →* UA L n, (∀ g, J g ∈ GlobalTheory.UnitaryBC.finU L n) ∧ (∀ g u, cLoc (wt u) (J g) = g u) ∧
      (∀ g (v : Plc L), (∀ u, v.under (𝓞 (Lp L)) ≠ (wt u).under (𝓞 (Lp L))) → cLoc v (J g) = 1) ∧
      ∃ F : Fin m → UA L n → ℂ, F ≠ 0 ∧ (∀ i, F i ∈ σ ⊓ fixd (RU L n) K) ∧
        ∀ (k : KT n wt) i, ∑ a, τ₀ k i a • RU L n (J k) (F a) = F i := by
  classical
  letI := Fintype.ofFinite St
  obtain ⟨J, hJ, j1, j2, j3, j4⟩ := ne_J hinj j h1 h2 h3
  let M := σ ⊓ fixd (RU L n) K
  have hst : ∀ g, ∀ φ ∈ M, ((RU L n).comp J) g φ ∈ M := fun g φ hφ => ⟨hσ.stable _ _ hφ.1, fun k hk => by
    change RU L n k (RU L n (J g) φ) = RU L n (J g) φ
    rw [GlobalTheory.DefU.RU_RU, (j4 g k (hK k hk)).eq, ← GlobalTheory.DefU.RU_RU, hφ.2 k hk]⟩
  haveI : Nontrivial M := Submodule.nontrivial_iff_ne_bot.2 h0
  let ρ : Representation ℂ (GV n wt) M := GlobalTheory.PRep.sub ((RU L n).comp J) M hst
  obtain ⟨F, hF0, hF⟩ := (hT.2 M ρ τ fun u => ne_iso (h1 u) (h2 u) (h3 u) hσ (τ u) (hτ u) M inf_le_left _ fun g y => by
    change RU L n (J (Pi.mulSingle u g)) y = _
    rw [hJ]).2 hwd
  refine ⟨J, j1, j2, j3, fun i => F i, fun e => hF0 (funext fun i => Subtype.ext (congrFun e i)), fun i => (F i).2, fun k i => ?_⟩
  have := congrArg M.subtype (hF k i)
  rw [map_sum] at this
  exact this
private theorem ne_teich_ex (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (O : Type) [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O]
    [IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal O) O] [Finite (ResidueField O)] [CharP (ResidueField O) l] :
    ∃ τ : ResidueField O →* O, ∀ x, residue O (τ x) = x := by
  haveI : CharP (O ⧸ maximalIdeal O) l := inferInstanceAs (CharP (ResidueField O) l)
  haveI : PerfectRing (ResidueField O) l := inferInstance
  let j : ResidueField O →+* Perfection (O ⧸ maximalIdeal O) l :=
    Perfection.lift l (ResidueField O) (O ⧸ maximalIdeal O) (RingHom.id (ResidueField O))
  have hj : ∀ x, Perfection.coeff (O ⧸ maximalIdeal O) l 0 (j x) = x := fun x => by
    have := (Perfection.lift l (ResidueField O) (O ⧸ maximalIdeal O)).symm_apply_apply (RingHom.id (ResidueField O))
    exact RingHom.congr_fun this x
  refine ⟨(Perfection.teichmuller l (maximalIdeal O)).comp j, fun x => ?_⟩
  exact (Perfection.mk_teichmuller (j x)).trans (hj x)
private theorem ne_isTeich {l : ℕ} [hl : Fact l.Prime] {F : W.GFrame l} (c : Coef l) {n : ℕ} (r : Resid F c.O n) :
    ∃ t, r.IsTeich t := by
  obtain ⟨hc, hfin, hlm, -⟩ := UpperTheory.V.abelian₂.dvr_finite l c.O
  haveI : Finite (ResidueField c.O) := hfin
  haveI : CharP (ResidueField c.O) l := by
    refine (CharP.charP_iff_prime_eq_zero hl.out).2 ?_
    rw [← map_natCast (residue c.O), residue_eq_zero_iff]
    exact hlm
  obtain ⟨τ, hτ⟩ := ne_teich_ex l c.O
  refine ⟨fun i => (Units.map τ).comp (r.chib i), fun i g => ⟨hτ _, ?_⟩⟩
  rw [MonoidHom.comp_apply, ← map_pow, ← Nat.card_units, pow_card_eq_one', map_one]
private theorem ne_notMem {l : ℕ} {L : Type} [Field L] [NumberField L] [IsCMField L] {u : Plc L}
    (h : ((l : ℕ) : 𝓞 (Lp L)) ∉ (u.under (𝓞 (Lp L))).asIdeal) : ((l : ℕ) : 𝓞 L) ∉ u.asIdeal := fun h' => h <| by
  change algebraMap (𝓞 (Lp L)) (𝓞 L) (l : 𝓞 (Lp L)) ∈ u.asIdeal
  rwa [map_natCast]
private theorem ne_cmp {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {L : Type} [Field L] [NumberField L] [IsCMField L] {n : ℕ} {w : Plc L}
    (h : ((l : ℕ) : 𝓞 (Lp L)) ∉ (w.under (𝓞 (Lp L))).asIdeal) (x : UA L n) :
    cmp n w ((away l L n).rangeRestrict x) = cLoc w x := by
  unfold CaseU.cmp
  rw [dif_pos (ne_notMem h)]
  rfl
section
variable {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {L Ω : Type} [Field L] [NumberField L] [IsCMField L] [Field Ω] [Algebra L Ω] [IsAlgClosure L Ω]
  {s : HidaSetting l L Ω} (n : ℕ) {V : s.Lev} (Q : QData V)
private noncomputable def neK : Subgroup (UA L n) :=
  GlobalTheory.UnitaryBC.finU L n ⊓
    (⨅ (u : Plc L) (_ : u.under (𝓞 (Lp L)) ∉ V.S'), (KO (u.adicCompletion L) (Fin n)).comap (cLoc u)) ⊓
    (Kc _ n 1).comap (cLoc (s.w s.v0)) ⊓ (⨅ v : V.frame.Vl, (cLoc (s.w v.1)).ker) ⊓ (⨅ u : s.St, (cLoc (s.w u)).ker) ⊓
    ⨅ q : Q.Q, (cLoc (Q.wq q)).ker
variable {n Q}
private theorem mem_neK {k : UA L n} : k ∈ neK n Q ↔ k ∈ GlobalTheory.UnitaryBC.finU L n ∧
    (∀ u : Plc L, u.under (𝓞 (Lp L)) ∉ V.S' → cLoc u k ∈ KO (u.adicCompletion L) (Fin n)) ∧ cLoc (s.w s.v0) k ∈ Kc _ n 1 ∧
    (∀ v : V.frame.Vl, cLoc (s.w v.1) k = 1) ∧ (∀ u : s.St, cLoc (s.w u) k = 1) ∧ ∀ q : Q.Q, cLoc (Q.wq q) k = 1 := by
  simp only [neK, Subgroup.mem_inf, Subgroup.mem_iInf, Subgroup.mem_comap, MonoidHom.mem_ker, and_assoc]
private theorem ne_dec (J : GV n (wt s) →* UA L n) (j1 : ∀ g, J g ∈ GlobalTheory.UnitaryBC.finU L n) (j2 : ∀ g u, cLoc (wt s u) (J g) = g u)
    (j3 : ∀ g (v : Plc L), (∀ u, v.under (𝓞 (Lp L)) ≠ (wt s u).under (𝓞 (Lp L))) → cLoc v (J g) = 1)
    (u : Ub n s) (hu : u ∈ UQ n Q) (x : UA L n) (hx : x ∈ GlobalTheory.UnitaryBC.finU L n) (h1 : (away l L n).rangeRestrict x = u)
    (h2 : ∀ v : V.frame.Vl, cLoc (s.w v.1) x = 1) : (J (toKT n s u))⁻¹ * x ∈ neK n Q := by
  have hw := (UpperTheory.under_chosen_place_and_isSplitAt_of_decomp_le l L Ω s).1
  have j4 : ∀ v : Plc L, v.under (𝓞 (Lp L)) ∉ s.St → cLoc v ((J (toKT n s u))⁻¹ * x) = cLoc v x := fun v hv => by
    rw [map_mul, map_inv, j3 _ v fun a e => hv (by rw [e, hw]; exact a.2), inv_one, one_mul]
  have hc : ∀ v : Plc L, ((l : ℕ) : 𝓞 (Lp L)) ∉ (v.under (𝓞 (Lp L))).asIdeal → cLoc v x = cmp n v (u : Ga l L n) := fun v hv => by
    rw [← h1, ne_cmp hv]
  obtain ⟨hu1, hu2⟩ := Subgroup.mem_inf.1 hu
  refine mem_neK.2 ⟨mul_mem (inv_mem (j1 _)) hx, fun v hv => ?_, ?_, fun v => ?_, fun a => ?_, fun q => ?_⟩
  · rw [j4 v fun h => hv (V.sub (Or.inr (Or.inl h))), hc v fun h => hv (V.sub (Or.inl h))]
    exact Subgroup.mem_iInf.1 (Subgroup.mem_iInf.1 hu1 v) hv
  · rw [j4 _ (by rw [hw]; exact s.v0_St)]
    by_cases h : ((l : ℕ) : 𝓞 (Lp L)) ∈ s.v0.asIdeal
    · rw [h2 ⟨s.v0, h⟩]
      exact one_mem _
    · rw [hc _ (by rwa [hw])]
      exact (Subgroup.mem_inf.1 u.2).2
  · rw [j4 _ (by rw [hw]; exact fun h => s.St_l _ h v.2), h2]
  · rw [map_mul, map_inv, j2, hc _ (by rw [hw]; exact s.St_l _ a.2)]
    exact inv_mul_cancel _
  · rw [j4 _ fun h => Q.notS q (Or.inr (Or.inl h)), hc _ fun h => Q.notS q (Or.inl h)]
    exact Subgroup.mem_iInf.1 hu2 q
private theorem ne_inf (ι : Qbar l ≃+* ℂ) (lam : Wt L l n) {x : UA L n} (hx : x ∈ GlobalTheory.UnitaryBC.finU L n) :
    infRep (V := V) ι lam x = 1 := by
  have h : ∀ τ, cEmb τ x = 1 := fun τ => by
    unfold cEmb
    rw [MonoidHom.comp_apply, MonoidHom.mem_ker.1 hx, map_one]
  unfold infRep
  rw [MonoidHom.comp_apply, ← map_one PiTensorProduct.mapMonoidHom]
  congr 1
  funext e
  change GlobalTheory.Branching.sqRep ℂ n _ (cEmb _ x) = 1
  rw [h, map_one]
private theorem ne_lin (ι : Qbar l ≃+* ℂ) {lam : Wt L l n} {d : ℕ}
    (e : (Fin d → ℂ) ≃ₗ[ℂ] ⨂[ℂ] e : Emb l (fun v : V.frame.Vl => s.w v.1), nablaSq ℂ n (lam e.res)) (ℓ) :
    ∃ T : (Ga l L n × GV n (fun v : V.frame.Vl => s.w v.1) → Fin d → ℂ) →ₗ[ℂ] (UA L n → ℂ), ∀ F, T F = toForm ι e F ℓ :=
  ⟨{ toFun := fun F => toForm ι e F ℓ
     map_add' := fun F F' => funext fun g => by simp [toForm]
     map_smul' := fun c F => funext fun g => by simp [toForm] }, fun _ => rfl⟩
end
theorem UpperTheory.V.Hi.nonempty_unitary_hecke_parts :
    _root_.UpperTheory.V.Af.HypothesesH' → ∀ (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (L Ω : Type) [Field L] [NumberField L] [IsCMField L] [Field Ω] [Algebra L Ω] [IsAlgClosure L Ω] (s : HidaSetting l L Ω) (n : ℕ), 1 ≤ n →
    ∀ (V : s.Lev) (Q : QData V) (c : Coef l), ∀ (r : Resid V.frame c.O n) (ι : Qbar l ≃+* ℂ), ∀ (m : ℕ) (τ₀ : KT n (wt s) →* GL (Fin m) c.O), _root_.UpperTheory.V.Hi.CaseU.TypeForAlt r ι τ₀ →
    ∀ (lam : Wt L l n) (d : ℕ) (e : (Fin d → ℂ) ≃ₗ[ℂ] ⨂[ℂ] e : Emb l (fun v : V.frame.Vl => s.w v.1), nablaSq ℂ n (lam e.res)) (ξ : GV n (fun v : V.frame.Vl => s.w v.1) →* GL (Fin d) ℂ), IsBasis ι e ξ →
    ∀ σ : Submodule ℂ (UA L n → ℂ), IsAutRepU σ → (∀ x : Emb l (fun v : V.frame.Vl => s.w v.1), HasWeightAt σ (ι.toRingHom.comp x.res) (lam x.res).dual) → (∀ w : Plc L, w.under (𝓞 (Lp L)) ∉ V.S' → IsUnramU σ w) → (∃ τ : SmIrr (GLw n (s.w s.v0)), IsCompAlong σ (locU n (s.w s.v0)) ((cLoc (s.w s.v0)).comp (locU n (s.w s.v0)).subtype) τ.ρ ∧ fixd τ.ρ (Kc _ n 1) ≠ ⊥) → (∀ t, r.IsTeich t → ∀ u : s.St, ∃ τ : SmIrr (GLw n (s.w u)), IsCompAlong σ (locU n (s.w u)) ((cLoc (s.w u)).comp (locU n (s.w u)).subtype) τ.ρ ∧ ∀ rc, _root_.UpperTheory.Zero.LLC.IsRecT (s.hw u) rc → IsTypeWD (s.hw u) (fun i => W.pushCh (ι.toRingHom.comp (algebraMap c.O (Qbar l))) ((t i).comp (V.pr.comp (Ine (F := L) (s.A u)).subtype))) (rc (algNorm τ))) → YOf Q ι τ₀ e ξ σ ≠ ⊥ := by
  intro _ l _ L Ω _ _ _ _ _ _ s n hn V Q c r ι m τ₀ hτ lam d e ξ he σ hσ hwt hun h0 hty
  obtain ⟨hw, hsp⟩ := UpperTheory.under_chosen_place_and_isSplitAt_of_decomp_le l L Ω s
  obtain ⟨t, ht⟩ := ne_isTeich c r
  choose τ hτc hτw using hty t ht
  choose j h1 h2 _ h3 using fun u : s.St => (secU l L Ω s n hn (wt s u) (hsp _ (by
    rw [hw]
    exact s.split _ (Or.inr (Or.inl u.2))))).1
  obtain ⟨φ, hφ, hφ0, hφK⟩ := UpperTheory.exists_nonzero_form_fixed_by_level_of_unramified l L Ω s n hn V Q σ hσ hun h0
  obtain ⟨J, j1, j2, j3, F₀, hF0, hF1, hF2⟩ := ne_type (fun u : s.St => s.hw u)
    (fun a b hab => Subtype.ext ((hw _).symm.trans (hab.trans (hw _)))) j h1 h2 h3 _ _ (hτ t ht) hσ τ hτc hτw (neK n Q)
    (fun k hk => (mem_neK.1 hk).2.2.2.2.1) ((Submodule.ne_bot_iff _).2 ⟨φ, ⟨hφ, fun k hk => by
      obtain ⟨a1, a2, a3, a4, a5, a6⟩ := mem_neK.1 hk
      exact hφK k a1 a2 a3 a4 a5 a6⟩, hφ0⟩)
  set τ₁ := (Matrix.GeneralLinearGroup.map (ι.toRingHom.comp (algebraMap c.O (Qbar l)))).comp τ₀ with hτ₁
  let N : Submodule ℂ (Fin m → UA L n → ℂ) :=
    { carrier := {F | (∀ i, F i ∈ σ ⊓ fixd (RU L n) (neK n Q)) ∧
        ∀ (k : KT n (wt s)) i, ∑ a, τ₁ k i a • RU L n (J k) (F a) = F i}
      add_mem' := fun {a b} ha hb => ⟨fun i => add_mem (ha.1 i) (hb.1 i), fun k i => by
        simp only [Pi.add_apply, map_add, smul_add, Finset.sum_add_distrib, ha.2, hb.2]⟩
      zero_mem' := ⟨fun i => zero_mem _, fun k i => by simp⟩
      smul_mem' := fun z a ha => ⟨fun i => Submodule.smul_mem _ z (ha.1 i), fun k i => by
        simp only [Pi.smul_apply, map_smul, smul_comm _ z, ← Finset.smul_sum, ha.2]⟩ }
  obtain ⟨hfin, hbij⟩ := UpperTheory.placeEmbeddings_bijective_infinitePlaces l L Ω s V ι
  have hcomm : ∀ x ∈ infU L n, ∀ k ∈ GlobalTheory.UnitaryBC.finU L n, ∀ ψ, RU L n k (RU L n x ψ) = RU L n x (RU L n k ψ) :=
    fun x hx k hk ψ => by rw [GlobalTheory.DefU.RU_RU, GlobalTheory.DefU.RU_RU, GlobalTheory.DefU.fin_mul_inf hk hx]
  obtain ⟨Φ, hΦ0, hΦN, hΦ⟩ := UpperTheory.exists_tensor_dual_embedding_of_hasWeightAt L n hn _ _ hbij (fun x => lam x.res) σ hσ hwt m N
    ((Submodule.ne_bot_iff _).2 ⟨F₀, ⟨hF1, hF2⟩, hF0⟩) (fun F hF k => (hF.1 k).1) fun x hx F hF =>
      ⟨fun i => ⟨hσ.stable _ _ (hF.1 i).1, fun k hk => by rw [hcomm x hx k (mem_neK.1 hk).1, (hF.1 i).2 k hk]⟩, fun k i => by
        beta_reduce
        rw [← hF.2 k i, map_sum]
        exact Finset.sum_congr rfl fun a _ => by rw [map_smul, hcomm x hx _ (j1 _)]⟩
  obtain ⟨f1, -, f3, f4, f5⟩ := formU l L Ω s n hn V Q c m τ₀ ι lam d e ξ he _ fun _ => rfl
  have f6 : ∀ i : Fin m, ∃ F : Ga l L n × GV n (fun v : V.frame.Vl => s.w v.1) → Fin d → ℂ, F ∈ (hset Q τ₀).X.AF ξ ∧
      ∀ ℓ, toForm ι e F ℓ = Φ ℓ i := fun i => by
    have := f5 ((LinearMap.proj i : (Fin m → UA L n → ℂ) →ₗ[ℂ] (UA L n → ℂ)) ∘ₗ Φ) (fun ℓ => hσ.aut _ ((hΦN ℓ).1 i).1)
      fun ℓ x hx => hΦ ℓ x hx i
    exact this
  choose F hFA hF using f6
  have key0 : ∀ u : Ub n s, u ∈ UQ n Q → ∀ i,
      ∑ a, τ₁ (toKT n s u) i a • (fun g => F a (g * ((u : Ga l L n), 1))) - F i = 0 := fun u hu i => by
    obtain ⟨x, hx⟩ := f4 ((u : Ga l L n), 1)
    have hx1 : (away l L n).rangeRestrict (x : UA L n) = (u : Ga l L n) := congrArg Prod.fst hx
    have hx2 : ∀ v : V.frame.Vl, cLoc (s.w v.1) (x : UA L n) = 1 := congrFun (congrArg Prod.snd hx)
    have hk := ne_dec J j1 j2 j3 u hu x x.2 hx1 hx2
    let A : Submodule ℂ (Ga l L n × GV n (fun v : V.frame.Vl => s.w v.1) → Fin d → ℂ) := (hset Q τ₀).X.AF ξ
    have hF' : ∀ a, (fun g => F a (g * ((u : Ga l L n), 1))) ∈ A := fun a γ g =>
      (congrArg (F a) (mul_assoc _ _ _)).trans (hFA a γ _)
    refine f1 _ (A.sub_mem (A.sum_mem fun a _ => A.smul_mem _ (hF' a)) (hFA i)) fun ℓ => ?_
    obtain ⟨T, hT⟩ := ne_lin ι e ℓ
    have e2 : ∀ a, T (fun g => F a (g * ((u : Ga l L n), 1))) = RU L n (J (toKT n s u)) (Φ ℓ a) := fun a => by
      have e3 := f3 (F a) ℓ x
      rw [ne_inf ι lam (inv_mem x.2), hF] at e3
      simp only [hx] at e3
      rw [hT, ← ((hΦN ℓ).1 a).2 _ hk, GlobalTheory.DefU.RU_RU, mul_inv_cancel_left]
      exact e3.symm
    refine (hT _).symm.trans ?_
    rw [map_sub, map_sum, hT, hF, sub_eq_zero]
    simp only [map_smul, e2]
    exact (hΦN ℓ).2 _ i
  have key : ∀ u : Ub n s, u ∈ UQ n Q → ∀ i g k,
      ∑ a, τ₁ (toKT n s u) i a * F a (g * ((u : Ga l L n), 1)) k = F i g k := fun u hu i g k => by
    have := congrFun (congrFun (sub_eq_zero.1 (key0 u hu i)) g) k
    rw [Finset.sum_apply, Finset.sum_apply] at this
    exact this
  refine (Submodule.ne_bot_iff _).2 ⟨F, ⟨⟨fun i _ => hFA i, fun u hu => funext fun i => funext fun g => funext fun k => ?_⟩,
    Submodule.subset_span fun i ℓ => ?_⟩, fun h => hΦ0 (LinearMap.ext fun ℓ => funext fun i => ?_)⟩
  · exact key u hu i g k
  · rw [hF]
    exact ((hΦN ℓ).1 i).1
  · obtain ⟨T, hT⟩ := ne_lin ι e ℓ
    have h' : F i = 0 := congrFun h i
    rw [← hF, ← hT, h', map_zero]
    rfl
end

private theorem ordA_lin {W ι : Type} [AddCommGroup W] [Module ℂ W] [FiniteDimensional ℂ W]
    (f : ι → Module.End ℂ W) (hc : ∀ i j, Commute (f i) (f j)) (l : W →ₗ[ℂ] ℂ) (μ : ι → ℂ)
    (hl : ∀ i v, l (f i v) = μ i * l v) {w : W} (hw : l w ≠ 0) : ∃ v, v ≠ 0 ∧ ∀ i, f i v = μ i • v := by
  have htop := Module.End.iSup_iInf_maxGenEigenspace_eq_top_of_iSup_maxGenEigenspace_eq_top_of_commute f
    (fun i j _ => hc i j) fun i => Module.End.iSup_maxGenEigenspace_eq_top (f i)
  obtain ⟨χ, u, hu, hlu⟩ : ∃ χ : ι → ℂ, ∃ u ∈ ⨅ i, (f i).maxGenEigenspace (χ i), l u ≠ 0 := by
    by_contra h
    push Not at h
    have : (⊤ : Submodule ℂ W) ≤ LinearMap.ker l := htop ▸ iSup_le fun χ u hu => h χ u hu
    exact hw (this trivial)
  have pw : ∀ i (x : ℂ) (m : ℕ) v, l (((f i - x • 1) ^ m) v) = (μ i - x) ^ m * l v := by
    intro i x m
    induction m with
    | zero => simp
    | succ m ih => intro v; rw [pow_succ, Module.End.mul_apply, ih]; simp [hl]; ring
  obtain rfl : μ = χ := funext fun i => by
    obtain ⟨m, hm⟩ := (Module.End.mem_maxGenEigenspace _ _ _).1 ((Submodule.mem_iInf _).1 hu i)
    have := pw i (χ i) m u
    rw [hm, map_zero, eq_comm, mul_eq_zero] at this
    exact sub_eq_zero.1 (eq_zero_of_pow_eq_zero (this.resolve_right hlu))
  let A : Set (Submodule ℂ W) :=
    {S | S ≠ ⊥ ∧ S ≤ (⨅ i, (f i).maxGenEigenspace (μ i)) ∧ ∀ i, ∀ v ∈ S, f i v ∈ S}
  obtain ⟨S, ⟨h0, h1, h2⟩, hmin⟩ := (wellFounded_lt (α := Submodule ℂ W)).has_min A
    ⟨_, (Submodule.ne_bot_iff _).2 ⟨u, hu, fun h => hlu (by rw [h, map_zero])⟩, le_rfl, fun i v hv =>
      (Submodule.mem_iInf _).2 fun j =>
        Module.End.mapsTo_maxGenEigenspace_of_comm (hc j i) (μ j) ((Submodule.mem_iInf _).1 hv j)⟩
  obtain ⟨v, hv, hv0⟩ := (Submodule.ne_bot_iff _).1 h0
  refine ⟨v, hv0, fun i => ?_⟩
  have ne : ∀ (m : ℕ), ∀ x ∈ S, x ≠ 0 → ((f i - μ i • 1) ^ m) x = 0 → S ⊓ (f i).eigenspace (μ i) ≠ ⊥ := by
    intro m
    induction m with
    | zero => intro x _ h h'; exact absurd h' h
    | succ m ih =>
      intro x hx h h'
      by_cases e : f i x - μ i • x = 0
      · exact (Submodule.ne_bot_iff _).2 ⟨x, ⟨hx, Module.End.mem_eigenspace_iff.2 (sub_eq_zero.1 e)⟩, h⟩
      · exact ih _ (sub_mem (h2 i x hx) (S.smul_mem _ hx)) e (by rw [pow_succ] at h'; exact h')
  obtain ⟨m, hm⟩ := (Module.End.mem_maxGenEigenspace _ _ _).1 ((Submodule.mem_iInf _).1 (h1 hv) i)
  have := hmin (S ⊓ (f i).eigenspace (μ i)) ⟨ne m v hv hv0 hm, inf_le_left.trans h1, fun j x hx =>
    ⟨h2 j x hx.1, Module.End.mem_eigenspace_iff.2 (by
      rw [← Module.End.mul_apply, (hc i j).eq, Module.End.mul_apply, Module.End.mem_eigenspace_iff.1 hx.2, map_smul])⟩⟩
  have e : S ⊓ (f i).eigenspace (μ i) = S := (inf_le_left.lt_or_eq).resolve_left this
  exact Module.End.mem_eigenspace_iff.1 (e.symm ▸ hv : v ∈ S ⊓ (f i).eigenspace (μ i)).2
private theorem ordA_abs {G V : Type} [Group G] (I : Iwa G) [AddCommGroup V] [Module ℂ V] (π : Representation ℂ G V) (c : ℕ)
    [FiniteDimensional ℂ (fixd π (I.I1 c))] (l : V →ₗ[ℂ] ℂ) (χ : G → ℂ) (hN : ∀ x ∈ I.Nf, ∀ v, l (π x v) = l v)
    (hT : ∀ t ∈ I.Tf, ∀ v, l (π t v) = χ t * l v) {w : V} (hw : w ∈ fixd π (I.I1 c)) (hl : l w ≠ 0) :
    ∃ v ∈ fixd π (I.I1 c), v ≠ 0 ∧ (∀ t ∈ I.T, π t v = χ t • v) ∧
      ∀ a ∈ I.P, I.uOp π a v = (((I.Nconj a).relIndex I.N : ℂ) * χ a) • v := by
  have mul : ∀ (g h : G) (w : V), π g (π h w) = π (g * h) w := fun g h w => by
    rw [map_mul π g h, Module.End.mul_apply]
  have kN : ∀ v : fixd π (I.I1 c), (v : V) ∈ fixd π I.N := fun v x hx =>
    v.2 x (Subgroup.mem_sup_left (Subgroup.mem_sup_left hx))
  have st : ∀ t ∈ I.T, ∀ v ∈ fixd π (I.I1 c), π t v ∈ fixd π (I.I1 c) := by
    intro t ht v hv g hg
    obtain ⟨x, hx, s, hs, y, hy, rfl⟩ := (iwMem G I c g).2.1.1 hg
    have e : x * s * y * t = t * ((t⁻¹ * x * t⁻¹⁻¹) * s * (t⁻¹ * y * t⁻¹⁻¹)) := by
      have := I.comm s (I.T_le (I.Tc_le c hs)) t (I.T_le ht)
      calc x * s * y * t = x * (s * t) * (t⁻¹ * y * t) := by group
        _ = _ := by rw [this]; group
    rw [mul, e, ← mul, hv _ ((iwMem G I c _).2.1.2
      ⟨_, I.T_N _ (inv_mem ht) x hx, s, hs, _, I.T_Nm c _ (inv_mem ht) y hy, rfl⟩)]
  have hJ : I.jacKer π ≤ LinearMap.ker l := Submodule.span_le.2 (by
    rintro _ ⟨x, hx, v, rfl⟩
    simp [hN x hx])
  let f : I.T ⊕ I.P → Module.End ℂ (fixd π (I.I1 c)) :=
    Sum.elim (fun t => (π t).restrict (st t t.2)) fun a => (I.uOp π a).restrict ((uOpLevel G I ℂ V π c).1 a a.2)
  have mix : ∀ t a, Commute (f (.inl t)) (f (.inr a)) := fun t a => LinearMap.ext fun v => Subtype.ext
    ((uOpMul G I ℂ V π a a a.2 a.2 v (kN v)).2.2.2.1 t t.2)
  obtain ⟨v, hv0, hv⟩ := ordA_lin f (by
      rintro (s | a) (t | b)
      · refine LinearMap.ext fun v => Subtype.ext ?_
        show π s (π t v) = π t (π s v)
        rw [mul, mul, I.comm s (I.T_le s.2) t (I.T_le t.2)]
      · exact mix _ _
      · exact (mix _ _).symm
      · refine LinearMap.ext fun v => Subtype.ext ?_
        show I.uOp π a (I.uOp π b v) = I.uOp π b (I.uOp π a v)
        rw [(uOpMul G I ℂ V π a b a.2 b.2 v (kN v)).2.1, (uOpMul G I ℂ V π b a b.2 a.2 v (kN v)).2.1,
          I.comm a (I.P_le a a.2) b (I.P_le b b.2)])
    (l.comp (Submodule.subtype _))
    (Sum.elim (fun t => χ t) fun a => ((I.Nconj a).relIndex I.N : ℂ) * χ a) (by
      rintro (t | a) v
      · exact hT t (I.T_le t.2) v
      · have := hJ (propCp G I ℂ V π a a.2 v (kN v))
        rw [LinearMap.mem_ker, map_sub, map_smul, hT a (I.P_le a a.2), sub_eq_zero, smul_eq_mul, ← mul_assoc] at this
        exact this) (w := ⟨w, hw⟩) hl
  exact ⟨v, v.2, fun h => hv0 (Subtype.ext h), fun t ht => congrArg Subtype.val (hv (.inl ⟨t, ht⟩)),
    fun a ha => congrArg Subtype.val (hv (.inr ⟨a, ha⟩))⟩
section SlopeTestRemainingChoices
variable {k : Type} [Field k] [ValuativeRel k] [TopologicalSpace k] [IsNonarchimedeanLocalField k] {n : ℕ}
private theorem exists_torus_eq_diagT_eq_prod_eT_and_mem_Uc_of_mem_Tc (t : (glIwa k n).T) : ∃ y : Fin n → Uo k, (t : GL (Fin n) k) = diagT k (Fin n) (fun i => y i) ∧
    t = (List.ofFn fun i => eT k n i (y i)).prod ∧ ∀ c, (t : GL (Fin n) k) ∈ (glIwa k n).Tc c → ∀ i, (y i : kˣ) ∈ Uc k (c + 1) := by
  obtain ⟨y, h1, h2⟩ := (glBridge k n ℂ (Representation.trivial ℂ _ ℂ)).2.2.2.2.2.2.2.2.2 t
  refine ⟨y, ?_, Subtype.ext ?_, h2⟩
  · rw [h1]
    show (List.ofFn fun i => diagT k (Fin n) (Pi.mulSingle i (y i : kˣ))).prod = _
    rw [show (fun i => diagT k (Fin n) (Pi.mulSingle i (y i : kˣ))) =
      diagT k (Fin n) ∘ fun i => Pi.mulSingle i ((fun i => (y i : kˣ)) i) from rfl,
      ← List.map_ofFn, ← map_list_prod, List.prod_ofFn, Finset.univ_prod_mulSingle]
  · rw [h1]
    exact ((map_list_prod (glData k n).T.subtype _).trans (by rw [List.map_ofFn]; rfl)).symm
private theorem ordA_val (ω : Fin n → kˣ →* ℂˣ) (j : ℕ) (hj : j ≤ n) :
    ((Nat.card 𝓀[k] ^ (j * (n - j)) : ℕ) : ℂ) * ∏ i : Fin n,
      ((absPow k ((modExp (fun _ : Fin n => Unit) i : ℂ) / 2) * (ω i * absPow k (((n : ℂ) - 1) / 2)))
        (unif k ^ (if (i : ℕ) < j then (1 : ℤ) else 0)) : ℂ) = psEig ω j := by
  have hq : nabs k (unif k) = (Nat.card 𝓀[k] : ℝ)⁻¹ := GlobalTheory.GLDoubling.nabs_unif GlAx.unif_spec
  have h0 : (Nat.card 𝓀[k] : ℂ) ≠ 0 := by
    have := nabs_pos' k (unif k)
    rw [hq] at this
    exact_mod_cast (inv_pos.1 this).ne'
  have h1 : ∀ i : Fin n, ((absPow k ((modExp (fun _ : Fin n => Unit) i : ℂ) / 2) * (ω i * absPow k (((n : ℂ) - 1) / 2)))
        (unif k ^ (if (i : ℕ) < j then (1 : ℤ) else 0)) : ℂ) =
      if (i : ℕ) < j then (ω i (unif k) : ℂ) * ((Nat.card 𝓀[k] : ℂ) ^ (n - 1 - i))⁻¹ else 1 := by
    intro i
    split_ifs
    · have e : (modExp (fun _ : Fin n => Unit) i : ℂ) / 2 + ((n : ℂ) - 1) / 2 = ((n - 1 - i : ℕ) : ℂ) := by
        have := i.2
        rw [modExp_fin _ (fun _ => by simp), Nat.cast_sub (by omega), Nat.cast_sub (by omega)]
        push_cast; ring
      rw [mul_left_comm, ← absPow_add, e, zpow_one, MonoidHom.mul_apply, Units.val_mul, val_absPow, hq,
        Complex.cpow_natCast]
      simp
    · simp
  have h2 : ∀ m, m ≤ n → ∑ i ∈ Finset.range m, (n - 1 - i) = m * (n - m) + ∑ i ∈ Finset.range m, i := by
    intro m
    induction m with
    | zero => simp
    | succ m ih =>
      intro hm
      rw [Finset.sum_range_succ, Finset.sum_range_succ, ih (by omega)]
      obtain ⟨d, rfl⟩ := Nat.exists_eq_add_of_le hm
      rw [show m + 1 + d - m = d + 1 by omega, show m + 1 + d - 1 - m = d by omega, Nat.add_sub_cancel_left]
      ring
  have h3 : ∀ g : ℕ → ℕ, ∑ i ∈ Finset.univ.filter (fun i : Fin n => (i : ℕ) < j), g i = ∑ i ∈ Finset.range j, g i := by
    intro g
    rw [Finset.sum_filter, Fin.sum_univ_eq_sum_range (fun i => if i < j then g i else 0), ← Finset.sum_filter]
    congr 1
    ext i
    simp only [Finset.mem_filter, Finset.mem_range]
    omega
  simp only [h1]
  rw [← Finset.prod_filter, psEig, Finset.prod_mul_distrib, Finset.prod_mul_distrib, Finset.prod_inv_distrib,
    Finset.prod_inv_distrib, Finset.prod_pow_eq_pow_sum, Finset.prod_pow_eq_pow_sum, h3 fun i => n - 1 - i, h3 fun i => i,
    h2 j hj, pow_add, Nat.cast_pow]
  field_simp
end SlopeTestRemainingChoices
theorem UpperTheory.V.Hi.exists_ne_zero_mem_eig_uOp_eq_psEig_smul_of_equiv_ps :
    _root_.GlobalTheory.Mem.LiteralAutomorphy.LiteratureRankNHypothesis → ∀ (k : Type) [Field k] [ValuativeRel k] [TopologicalSpace k] [IsNonarchimedeanLocalField k] (n : ℕ) (V : Type) [AddCommGroup V] [Module ℂ V] (π : Representation ℂ (GL (Fin n) k) V) (ω : Fin n → kˣ →* ℂˣ), Nonempty (π.Equiv (PS ω)) → ∀ c : ℕ, (∀ i, ∀ y ∈ Uc k (c + 1), ω i y = 1) → ∀ φ : (glIwa k n).T →* ℂˣ, IsInvOn φ ω → ∃ v ∈ (glIwa k n).eig π c φ, v ≠ 0 ∧ ∀ j ≤ n, (glIwa k n).uOp π (al k n j) v = psEig ω j • v := by
  intro _ k _ _ _ _ n V _ _ π ω ⟨e⟩ c hω φ hφ
  classical
  obtain ⟨-, -, hI, -, -, -, -, hidx, -⟩ := glBridge k n V π
  let μ : Fin n → kˣ →* ℂˣ := fun i => ω i * absPow k (((n : ℂ) - 1) / 2)
  let Ψ : Levi k (fun _ : Fin n => Unit) →* ℂˣ :=
    deltaHalf k _ * (torusChar μ).comp (leviTorus k (Fin n)).symm.toMonoidHom
  let χ : GL (Fin n) k → ℂ := fun g => if h : g ∈ parab k (points (Fin n)) then Ψ (leviProj k _ ⟨g, h⟩) else 0
  have he : ∀ g v, e.toLinearEquiv (π g v) = PS ω g (e.toLinearEquiv v) := fun g v => e.toIntertwiningMap.isIntertwining _ _ g v
  let l : V →ₗ[ℂ] ℂ :=
    { toFun := fun v => (e.toLinearEquiv v).1.1 1
      map_add' := fun a b => by rw [map_add]; rfl
      map_smul' := fun a b => by rw [map_smul]; rfl }
  let σ := (twist ((ofChar (torusChar μ)).comp (leviTorus k (Fin n)).symm.toMonoidHom) (deltaHalf k _)).comp
    (leviProj k (points (Fin n)))
  have hσ : ∀ p x, σ p x = χ p * x := fun p x => by
    simp only [χ, dif_pos p.2]
    show (deltaHalf k _ _ : ℂ) • ofChar _ _ x = _
    rw [ofChar_apply]
    simp [Ψ, mul_assoc]
  have hl : ∀ g ∈ parab k (points (Fin n)), ∀ v, l (π g v) = χ g * l v := by
    intro g hg v
    refine Eq.trans ?_ (hσ ⟨g, hg⟩ _)
    refine Eq.trans ?_ ((e.toLinearEquiv v).1.2 ⟨g, hg⟩ 1)
    show (e.toLinearEquiv (π g v)).1.1 1 = _
    rw [he, mul_one]
    exact congrArg _ (one_mul g)
  have hN : ∀ x ∈ unipB k (Fin n), χ x = 1 := by
    intro x hx
    have h := unipRad_le_parab k _ hx
    simp only [χ, dif_pos h]
    rw [(coe_mem_unipRad k ⟨x, h⟩).1 hx, map_one, Units.val_one]
  have hD : ∀ t, diagT k (Fin n) t ∈ parab k (points (Fin n)) := fun t => leviEmb_mem_parab k _ _
  have hT : ∀ t, χ (diagT k (Fin n) t) =
      ∏ i, ((absPow k ((modExp (fun _ : Fin n => Unit) i : ℂ) / 2) * μ i) (t i) : ℂ) := by
    intro t
    have hd : ∀ i, Matrix.GeneralLinearGroup.det (leviTorus k (Fin n) t i) = t i :=
      congrFun ((leviTorus k (Fin n)).left_inv t)
    simp only [χ, dif_pos (hD t)]
    rw [show leviProj k _ ⟨_, hD t⟩ = leviTorus k (Fin n) t from leviProj_leviEmb k _ _]
    simp [Ψ, deltaHalf_apply, hd, Finset.prod_mul_distrib]
  have hφ' : ∀ t : (glIwa k n).T, χ t = ((φ t)⁻¹ : ℂˣ) ∧ ((t : GL (Fin n) k) ∈ (glIwa k n).Tc c → χ t = 1) := by
    intro t
    obtain ⟨y, h1, h2, h3⟩ := exists_torus_eq_diagT_eq_prod_eT_and_mem_Uc_of_mem_Tc t
    have h4 : χ t = ∏ i, (ω i (y i) : ℂ) := by
      rw [h1, hT]
      refine Finset.prod_congr rfl fun i _ => ?_
      simp only [μ, MonoidHom.mul_apply, isUnram_absPow k _ _ (y i).2, one_mul, mul_one]
    refine ⟨?_, fun h => by rw [h4]; exact Finset.prod_eq_one fun i _ => by rw [hω i _ (h3 c h i), Units.val_one]⟩
    have h5 : φ t = ∏ i, (ω i (y i))⁻¹ := by
      refine (congrArg φ h2).trans ((map_list_prod φ _).trans ?_)
      refine (congrArg List.prod (List.map_ofFn ..)).trans (List.prod_ofFn.trans ?_)
      exact Finset.prod_congr rfl fun i _ => hφ i (y i)
    rw [h4, h5]
    simp
  have hmul : ∀ a ∈ parab k (points (Fin n)), ∀ b ∈ parab k (points (Fin n)), χ (a * b) = χ a * χ b := by
    intro a ha b hb
    simp only [χ, dif_pos ha, dif_pos hb, dif_pos (mul_mem ha hb)]
    rw [← Units.val_mul, ← map_mul, ← map_mul]
    rfl
  obtain ⟨f, hf1, hf⟩ := UpperTheory.V.Hi.indVec _ ((glIwa k n).I1 c) σ (hI c).1 1 (by
    rintro ⟨p, hp⟩ h
    obtain ⟨x, hx, t, ht, y, hy, rfl⟩ := (iwMem _ _ c p).2.1.1 h
    have hx' := unipRad_le_parab k _ hx.1
    have ht' : t ∈ parab k (points (Fin n)) := by obtain ⟨⟨s, -, rfl⟩, -⟩ := ht; exact hD s
    have hy' : y ∈ parab k (points (Fin n)) := (Subgroup.mul_mem_cancel_left _ (mul_mem hx' ht')).1 hp
    rw [hσ, mul_one]
    show χ (x * t * y) = 1
    rw [hmul _ (mul_mem hx' ht') _ hy', hmul _ hx' _ ht', hN x hx.1, (hφ' ⟨t, ht.1⟩).2 ht,
      hN y ((mem_unipB k).2 ⟨(mem_borel k).1 hy', (GlAx.mem_low.1 hy.1).2⟩), one_mul, one_mul])
  haveI : FiniteDimensional ℂ (GlobalTheory.PRep.fixed (PS ω) ((glIwa k n).I1 c)) :=
    (GlobalTheory.SmGL.ParIndAdm.isAdmissible_parInd _ _).2 _ (hI c).1 (hI c).2
  haveI : FiniteDimensional ℂ (fixd π ((glIwa k n).I1 c)) :=
    Module.Finite.of_injective (e.toLinearEquiv.toLinearMap.restrict (p := fixd π ((glIwa k n).I1 c))
      (q := GlobalTheory.PRep.fixed (PS ω) ((glIwa k n).I1 c)) fun v hv u => by
        show PS ω u (e.toLinearEquiv v) = _
        rw [← he, hv u u.2]; exact (one_smul ℂ _).symm) fun a b h => Subtype.ext (e.toLinearEquiv.injective (congrArg Subtype.val h))
  obtain ⟨v, hv, hv0, h1, h2⟩ := ordA_abs (glIwa k n) π c l χ
    (fun x hx v => by rw [hl x (unipRad_le_parab k _ hx), hN x hx, one_mul])
    (by rintro _ ⟨t, rfl⟩; exact hl _ (hD t)) (w := e.toLinearEquiv.symm f)
    (fun u hu => e.toLinearEquiv.injective (by rw [he, LinearEquiv.apply_symm_apply]; exact hf u hu))
    (by
      show (e.toLinearEquiv (e.toLinearEquiv.symm f)).1.1 1 ≠ 0
      rw [LinearEquiv.apply_symm_apply]
      exact hf1.trans_ne one_ne_zero)
  refine ⟨v, ⟨fun x hx => hv x (Subgroup.mem_sup_left (Subgroup.mem_sup_left hx)),
    fun x hx => hv x (Subgroup.mem_sup_right hx), fun t => by rw [h1 t t.2, (hφ' t).1]⟩, hv0, fun j hj => ?_⟩
  rw [h2 _ (al_mem k n j), hidx j hj, ← ordA_val ω j hj, al, upow, hT]
end

private theorem up_list {σ : Type} [DecidableEq σ] {G : σ → Type} [∀ v, Group (G v)] (g : ∀ v, G v) (w : σ) (s : List σ)
    (hs : s.Nodup) : (s.map fun v => Pi.mulSingle v (g v)).prod w = if w ∈ s then g w else 1 := by
  induction s with
  | nil => simp
  | cons v s ih =>
    rw [List.nodup_cons] at hs
    rw [List.map_cons, List.prod_cons, Pi.mul_apply, ih hs.2]
    by_cases h : w = v
    · subst h; simp [hs.1]
    · simp [h]

variable {L Ω : Type} [Field L] [NumberField L] [Field Ω] [Algebra L Ω] [IsAlgClosure L Ω] {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {n : ℕ}
  {c : Coef l} (H : HSet L Ω l n c) (ι : Qbar l ≃+* ℂ)
private theorem up_VV (d : ℕ) (v v' : H.V) (h : v ≠ v') (g g') (F) :
    H.atV d v g (H.atV d v' g' F) = H.atV d v' g' (H.atV d v g F) := by
  classical
  change (H.X.rpF ℂ d H.m (Pi.mulSingle v g) * H.X.rpF ℂ d H.m (Pi.mulSingle v' g')) F =
    (H.X.rpF ℂ d H.m (Pi.mulSingle v' g') * H.X.rpF ℂ d H.m (Pi.mulSingle v g)) F
  rw [← map_mul, ← map_mul, (Pi.mulSingle_commute (f := fun v : H.V => GLw n (H.Sl v)) h g g').eq]
private theorem up_PA (d : ℕ) (g u) (F) :
    H.X.rpF ℂ d H.m g (H.X.raF d (H.τC ι) u F) = H.X.raF d (H.τC ι) u (H.X.rpF ℂ d H.m g F) := by
  funext i x k
  change (H.X.ra (H.τC ι) u (fun y j => F j y k) (x * (1, g))) i =
    H.X.ra (H.τC ι) u (fun y j => F j (y * (1, g)) k) x i
  rw [Frame.ra_apply, Frame.ra_apply]
  simp [mul_assoc]
private theorem up_vec (hL : _root_.GlobalTheory.Mem.LiteralAutomorphy.LiteratureRankNHypothesis) (hH : H.Ax) {lam : Wt L l n} {d : ℕ} {ξ : GV n H.Sl →* GL (Fin d) ℂ}
    (Y : H.PartAltAlt ι lam ξ) (K : H.Lev) (hK : ∀ q, fixd (Y.σq q).ρ (K.1 q) ≠ ⊥)
    (ω : ∀ v, Fin n → ((H.Sl v).adicCompletion L)ˣ →* ℂˣ) (hω : ∀ v, Nonempty ((Y.σl v).ρ.Equiv (PS (ω v)))) (e : ℕ)
    (he : ∀ v i, ∀ y ∈ Uc _ (e + 1), ω v i y = 1)
    (φ : ∀ v, (glIwa ((H.Sl v).adicCompletion L) n).T →* ℂˣ) (hφ : ∀ v, IsInvOn (φ v) (ω v)) :
    ∃ F ∈ Y.Y, F ≠ 0 ∧ (∀ v, F ∈ Up.ordC (H.atV d v) e (φ v) (psEig (ω v))) ∧
      ∀ q, F ∈ fixd (H.atW ι d (H.wq q)) (K.1 q) := by
  refine Exists.imp (fun F h => ⟨h.1, h.2.1, fun v => h.2.2 (.inl v), fun q => h.2.2 (.inr q)⟩) (Up.meet (I := H.V ⊕ H.Q)
    (Sum.elim (fun v => Set.range (H.atV d v)) fun q => Set.range (H.atW ι d (H.wq q)))
    (Sum.elim (fun v => Up.ordC (H.atV d v) e (φ v) (psEig (ω v))) fun q => fixd (H.atW ι d (H.wq q)) (K.1 q))
    Y.Y Y.ne ?_ ?_ ?_)
  · rintro (v | q) _ ⟨g, rfl⟩
    · exact Up.isoStab (Y.isoL v) g
    · exact Up.isoStab (Y.isoQ q) g
  · rintro (v | q) (v' | q') hne _ ⟨g, rfl⟩
    · exact Up.ordMap _ _ _ (fun g' F => up_VV H d v' v (fun h => hne (by rw [h])) g g' F) _ _ _
    · exact Up.ordMap _ _ _ (fun g' F => (up_PA H ι d _ _ F).symm) _ _ _
    · exact Up.fixMap (H.atW ι d (H.wq q)) (H.atW ι d (H.wq q)) _ (fun g' F => up_PA H ι d _ _ F) _
    · refine Up.fixMap _ _ _ (fun g' F => ?_) _
      change (H.X.raF d (H.τC ι) _ * H.X.raF d (H.τC ι) _) F = (H.X.raF d (H.τC ι) _ * H.X.raF d (H.τC ι) _) F
      rw [← map_mul, ← map_mul]
      exact congrArg (fun u => H.X.raF d (H.τC ι) u F) (hH.Q_Q q q' (fun h => hne (by rw [h])) g' g).eq.symm
  · rintro (v | q) Z hZ hne hst
    · obtain ⟨p, hp1, hp2, hp3⟩ := Up.isoSub (Y.isoL v) Z hZ hne fun g => hst _ ⟨g, rfl⟩
      obtain ⟨u, hu1, hu2, hu3⟩ := _root_.UpperTheory.V.Hi.exists_ne_zero_mem_eig_uOp_eq_psEig_smul_of_equiv_ps hL _ n _ (Y.σl v).ρ (ω v) (hω v) e (he v) (φ v) (hφ v)
      refine (Submodule.ne_bot_iff _).2 ⟨p u, ⟨hp2 ⟨u, rfl⟩, ?_⟩, fun h => hu2 (hp3 (by rw [h, map_zero]))⟩
      exact Up.ordMap _ _ p hp1 _ _ _ u ((Up.mem_ordC _ _ _ _ _).2 ⟨hu1, hu3⟩)
    · obtain ⟨p, hp1, hp2, hp3⟩ := Up.isoSub (Y.isoQ q) Z hZ hne fun g => hst _ ⟨g, rfl⟩
      obtain ⟨u, hu1, hu2⟩ := (Submodule.ne_bot_iff _).1 (hK q)
      exact (Submodule.ne_bot_iff _).2 ⟨p u, ⟨hp2 ⟨u, rfl⟩, Up.fixMap _ _ p hp1 _ u hu1⟩,
        fun h => hu2 (hp3 (by rw [h, map_zero]))⟩
private theorem up_ord {A : Type} [CommRing A] (θ : H.X.I.T →* Aˣ) (x : A →+* Qbar l)
    {lam : Wt L l n} {d : ℕ} {ξ : GV n H.Sl →* GL (Fin d) ℂ} (K : H.Lev)
    (ω : ∀ v, Fin n → ((H.Sl v).adicCompletion L)ˣ →* ℂˣ) (e : ℕ)
    (hω : ∀ v i (u : Uo ((H.Sl v).adicCompletion L)), (ω v i u : ℂ) * ι (H.finPart θ x lam v i u) = 1)
    (hn : ∀ v (j : Fin n), ‖ι.symm ((H.lowVal ι lam v (j + 1))⁻¹ * psEig (ω v) (j + 1))‖ = 1)
    (φ : ∀ v, (glIwa ((H.Sl v).adicCompletion L) n).T →* ℂˣ) (hφ : ∀ v, IsInvOn (φ v) (ω v))
    (F : Fin H.m → H.X.Fn ℂ d) (hB : F ∈ H.Big ι ξ) (hV : ∀ v, F ∈ Up.ordC (H.atV d v) e (φ v) (psEig (ω v)))
    (hQ : ∀ q, F ∈ fixd (H.atW ι d (H.wq q)) (K.1 q)) :
    F ∈ H.OrdAt θ ι x lam ξ K e ∧ ∀ v (j : Fin n),
      H.opC ι lam d (.inr (v, j)) F = ((H.lowVal ι lam v (j + 1))⁻¹ * psEig (ω v) (j + 1)) • F := by
  classical
  simp only [Up.mem_ordC] at hV
  let I := fun v : H.V => glIwa ((H.Sl v).adicCompletion L) n
  have hN : F ∈ fixd (H.X.rpF ℂ d H.m) H.X.I.N := Up.fixPi _ (fun v => (I v).N) F fun v => (hV v).1.1
  have hNm : F ∈ fixd (H.X.rpF ℂ d H.m) (H.X.I.Nm e) := Up.fixPi _ (fun v => (I v).Nm e) F fun v => (hV v).1.2.1
  have hlev : F ∈ fixd (H.X.raF d (H.τC ι)) (H.lev K) := by
    intro u hu
    let St : Subgroup H.X.Ub :=
      (MulAction.stabilizer (Module.End ℂ (Fin H.m → H.X.Fn ℂ d))ˣ F).comap (H.X.raF d (H.τC ι)).toHomUnits
    have : H.lev K ≤ St := sup_le (fun u hu => hB.2 u hu) (iSup_le fun q => by
      rintro _ ⟨g, hg, rfl⟩
      exact hQ q g hg)
    exact this hu
  have hCl : F ∈ H.ClAt θ ι x lam ξ K e := by
    refine ⟨⟨⟨⟨hB, hlev⟩, hN⟩, hNm⟩, ?_⟩
    simp only [SetLike.mem_coe, Submodule.mem_iInf, Module.End.mem_eigenspace_iff]
    intro v i y
    have h1 := (hV v).1.2.2 (eT _ n i y)
    rw [hφ v i y, inv_inv, eq_inv_of_mul_eq_one_left (hω v i y)] at h1
    exact h1
  have hz : ∀ v, (I v).uOp (H.atV d v) (I v).z F = ((List.range n).map (psEig (ω v))).prod • F := by
    intro v
    have h1 := (glBridge ((H.Sl v).adicCompletion L) n _ (H.atV d v)).2.2.2.2.2.2.2.2.1
    have h2 := (Up.uOpList (I v) (H.atV d v) F (hV v).1.1 (al _ n) (psEig (ω v)) (List.range n)
      fun j hj => ⟨al_mem _ n j, (hV v).2 j (List.mem_range.1 hj).le⟩).2
    rwa [← h1] at h2
  have hZ := (Up.uOpList (piIwa I) (H.X.rpF ℂ d H.m) F hN (fun v => Pi.mulSingle v (I v).z)
    (fun v => ((List.range n).map (psEig (ω v))).prod) Finset.univ.toList
    fun v _ => ⟨Up.singleP I v _ (I v).z_mem, (Up.uOpPi I _ v _ (I v).z_mem F hN).trans (hz v)⟩).2
  have hzz : (Finset.univ.toList.map fun v => (Pi.mulSingle v (I v).z : GV n H.Sl)).prod = H.X.I.z := by
    funext w
    rw [up_list (fun v => (I v).z) w _ (Finset.nodup_toList _), if_pos (by simp)]
    rfl
  rw [hzz, Finset.prod_map_toList] at hZ
  have hl : ∀ v, ((List.range n).map (psEig (ω v))).prod = ∏ j ∈ Finset.range n, psEig (ω v) j := fun v => rfl
  simp only [hl] at hZ
  refine ⟨?_, fun v j => ?_⟩
  · refine Submodule.mem_iSup_of_mem
      ⟨∏ v, ∏ j ∈ Finset.range n, (H.lowVal ι lam v j)⁻¹ * psEig (ω v) j, ?_⟩ ⟨hCl, ?_⟩
    · rw [map_prod, norm_prod]
      refine Finset.prod_eq_one fun v _ => ?_
      rw [map_prod, norm_prod]
      refine Finset.prod_eq_one fun j hj => ?_
      rcases j with _ | j
      · simp [HSet.lowVal, psEig]
      · exact hn v ⟨j, by have := Finset.mem_range.1 hj; omega⟩
    · refine Module.End.eigenspace_le_maxGenEigenspace (Module.End.mem_eigenspace_iff.2 ?_)
      change (∏ v, ∏ j ∈ Finset.range n, H.lowVal ι lam v j)⁻¹ • (piIwa I).uOp (H.X.rpF ℂ d H.m) H.X.I.z F = _
      rw [hZ, smul_smul]
      simp only [Finset.prod_mul_distrib, Finset.prod_inv_distrib]
  · change (H.lowVal ι lam v (j + 1))⁻¹ • (piIwa I).uOp (H.X.rpF ℂ d H.m) (Pi.mulSingle v (al _ n (j + 1))) F = _
    rw [Up.uOpPi I _ v _ (al_mem _ n _) F hN, ← smul_smul]
    exact congrArg _ ((hV v).2 _ j.2)
end
theorem UpperTheory.V.Hi.coreUp_alt :
    _root_.GlobalTheory.Mem.LiteralAutomorphy.LiteratureRankNHypothesis → ∀ (L Ω : Type) [Field L] [NumberField L] [Field Ω] [Algebra L Ω] [IsAlgClosure L Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (n : ℕ) (c : Coef l) (H : HSet L Ω l n c), H.Ax →
    ∀ (A : Type) [CommRing A] [Algebra c.O A] [Alg.IsCO c.O A] (θ : H.X.I.T →* Aˣ), H.IsWt θ →
    ∀ (ι : Qbar l ≃+* ℂ), ∀ (x : A →ₐ[c.O] Qbar l) (lam : Wt L l n) (d : ℕ) (ξ : GV n H.Sl →* GL (Fin d) ℂ) (c0 : ℕ) (sp : H.X.Fn A H.m → Fin H.m → H.X.Fn ℂ d), H.IsSpec θ ι x.toRingHom lam ξ c0 sp →
    ∀ (Y : H.PartAltAlt ι lam ξ) (K : H.Lev), (∀ q, fixd (Y.σq q).ρ (K.1 q) ≠ ⊥) → ∀ ω : ∀ v, Fin n → ((H.Sl v).adicCompletion L)ˣ →* ℂˣ, (∀ v, Nonempty ((Y.σl v).ρ.Equiv (PS (ω v)))) → (∀ v i (u : Uo ((H.Sl v).adicCompletion L)), (ω v i u : ℂ) * ι (H.finPart θ x.toRingHom lam v i u) = 1) → ∀ a : H.Op → c.O, (∀ w (j : Fin n), ‖ι.symm (Y.a w (j + 1)) - algebraMap c.O (Qbar l) (a (.inl (w, j)))‖ < 1) → (∀ v (j : Fin n), ‖ι.symm ((H.lowVal ι lam v (j + 1))⁻¹ * psEig (ω v) (j + 1))‖ = 1 ∧ ‖ι.symm ((H.lowVal ι lam v (j + 1))⁻¹ * psEig (ω v) (j + 1)) - algebraMap c.O (Qbar l) (a (.inr (v, j)))‖ < 1) → ∀ [Fact (CommOn (H.MK θ a K) (H.op A))], H.MK θ a K ≠ ⊥ ∧ ∃ y : H.TK θ a K →ₐ[c.O] Qbar l, y.toRingHom.comp (algebraMap A _) = x.toRingHom ∧ (∀ w (j : Fin n), ι (y (H.gen θ a K (.inl (w, j)))) = Y.a w (j + 1)) ∧ ∀ v (j : Fin n), ι (y (H.gen θ a K (.inr (v, j)))) = (H.lowVal ι lam v (j + 1))⁻¹ * psEig (ω v) (j + 1) := by
  intro hL L Ω _ _ _ _ _ l _ n c H hH A _ _ _ θ hθ ι x lam d ξ c0 sp hsp Y K hK ω hω1 hω2 a ha1 ha2 hFact
  haveI : Finite (ResidueField c.O) := (UpperTheory.V.abelian₂.dvr_finite l c.O).2.1
  haveI : IsCNL A := IsCNL.ofCO c.O A
  obtain ⟨hC, hfin, hfree, -⟩ := hiComm L Ω l n c H hH A θ hθ K
  choose φ hφ using fun v => Up.exInv (ω v)
  obtain ⟨F, hF, h0, hV, hQ⟩ := up_vec H ι hL hH Y K hK ω hω1 c0 (fun v i y hy => by
    have h := hω2 v i ⟨y, Up.Uc_le _ hy⟩
    rw [hsp.triv v i _ hy, map_one, mul_one] at h
    exact Units.ext h) φ hφ
  obtain ⟨hO, hU⟩ := up_ord H ι θ x.toRingHom K ω c0 hω2 (fun v j => (ha2 v j).1) φ hφ F (Y.le hF) hV hQ
  rw [← hsp.span K c0 le_rfl] at hO
  have hx2 : ∀ b ∈ maximalIdeal A, ‖x.toRingHom b‖ < 1 := Up.normLt x
  have hx1 : ∀ b, ‖x.toRingHom b‖ ≤ 1 := by
    intro b
    obtain ⟨o, ho⟩ := Alg.IsCO.res_surj (O := c.O) b
    have h1 : ‖x (algebraMap c.O A o)‖ ≤ 1 := by
      rw [x.commutes]
      exact (Up.intIff _).1 ((Algebra.IsIntegral.isIntegral (R := ℤ_[l]) o).map c.O.val)
    have h2 : x.toRingHom b = x.toRingHom (b - algebraMap c.O A o) + x (algebraMap c.O A o) := by simp
    rw [h2]
    exact (IsUltrametricDist.norm_add_le_max _ _).trans (max_le (hx2 _ ho).le h1)
  haveI : Fact (CommOn (locPart (H.X.Ord (H.τA A) θ (H.lev K)) (H.op A) fun i => algebraMap c.O A (a i)) (H.op A)) := hFact
  obtain ⟨hne, y, hy1, hy2⟩ := Up.eigPt (H.X.Ord (H.τA A) θ (H.lev K)) (H.op A) hC ι x.toRingHom hx1 hx2 sp
    (hsp.add K) (hsp.smul K) (hsp.indep K) (H.opC ι lam d) (hsp.op K) F hO h0
    (Sum.elim (fun p => Y.a p.1 (p.2 + 1)) fun p => (H.lowVal ι lam p.1 (p.2 + 1))⁻¹ * psEig (ω p.1) (p.2 + 1))
    (by
      rintro (⟨w, j⟩ | ⟨v, j⟩)
      · exact Y.sph w _ F hF
      · exact hU v j)
    (fun i => algebraMap c.O A (a i))
    (by
      rintro (⟨w, j⟩ | ⟨v, j⟩)
      · exact (x.commutes _).symm ▸ ha1 w j
      · exact (x.commutes _).symm ▸ (ha2 v j).2)
  refine ⟨hne, ⟨y, fun o => ?_⟩, RingHom.ext hy1, fun w j => hy2 (.inl (w, j)),
    fun v j => hy2 (.inr (v, j))⟩
  refine Eq.trans (congrArg y ?_) ((hy1 _).trans (x.commutes o))
  exact Subtype.ext (LinearMap.ext fun f => (algebraMap_smul A o f).symm)
end


[HPT_SRC_UNIF] Exact finite-part, unit and residual ordinary-eigenvalue normalization
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/ModularityArgument/ThresholdArguments/DenseThresholdCases/UnifVal.lean#L1-L100

import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.HeckeAlgebras.UpOperatorEigenvalues.HeckeCoeff
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.ModularityArgument.ThresholdArguments.DenseThresholdCases.UnitaryHeckeData

section

namespace UpperTheory.V.Hi.Up
section Loc
variable {F Ω : Type} [Field F] [NumberField F] [Field Ω] [Algebra F Ω] [IsAlgClosure F Ω] {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {n : ℕ}
  {ι : Qbar l ≃+* ℂ} {w : Plc F} {A : ValuationSubring Ω} {h : IsPlaceOver A w} {a : Weil h →* (w.adicCompletion F)ˣ}
private theorem ord_val (hN : _root_.GlobalTheory.Mem.LiteralAutomorphy.LiteratureRankNHypothesis) (hw : ((l : ℕ) : 𝓞 F) ∈ w.asIdeal) (ha : IsLocalArtin h a) {lam : Wt F l n} {i : Fin n}
    {ω : (w.adicCompletion F)ˣ →* ℂˣ} {ψ : Dec (F := F) A →* (Qbar l)ˣ} (ho : OrdChar ι a ω (htW lam i) ψ) (s : Weil h) :
    (ψ (Subgroup.inclusion (Weil_le_Dec h) s) : Qbar l) * (cycBar F Ω l s : Qbar l) ^ (i : ℕ) =
      (algPow w (lowW lam i) (a s : w.adicCompletion F))⁻¹ * (ι.symm (ω (a s)) * (((normV w : ℕ) : Qbar l) ^ deg s) ^ (i : ℕ)) := by
  have h1 : (ψ (Subgroup.inclusion (Weil_le_Dec h) s) : Qbar l) = _ := ho.val s _ rfl (Set.mem_univ _)
  have h2 : algPow w (-htW lam i) (a s : w.adicCompletion F) * algPow w (fun _ => 1) (a s : w.adicCompletion F) ^ (i : ℕ) =
      (algPow w (lowW lam i) (a s : w.adicCompletion F))⁻¹ := by
    haveI := GlobalTheory.HeckeGal.finite_contEmb (l := l) w
    haveI := Fintype.ofFinite {j : w.adicCompletion F →+* Qbar l // Continuous j}
    unfold algPow
    simp only [finprod_eq_prod_of_fintype]
    rw [← Finset.prod_pow, ← Finset.prod_mul_distrib, ← Finset.prod_inv_distrib]
    refine Finset.prod_congr rfl fun j _ => ?_
    rw [zpow_one, ← zpow_natCast, ← zpow_add₀ ((map_ne_zero j.1).2 (a s).ne_zero), ← zpow_neg]
    simp [htW]
  rw [h1, UpperTheory.V.Loc.epsArt hN.aut.art h hw a ha s, ← h2]
  ring
end Loc
section Set
variable {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {L Ω : Type} [Field L] [NumberField L] [IsCMField L] [Field Ω] [Algebra L Ω] [IsAlgClosure L Ω]
  {s : HidaSetting l L Ω} {n : ℕ} {V : s.Lev} {Q : QData V} {c : Coef l} {r : Resid V.frame c.O n} {ι : Qbar l ≃+* ℂ} {B : UniversalOrdinaryDeterminantRing r}
  {Λ : LamObj r} (R : _root_.UpperTheory.V.Hi.CaseU.UnitaryHeckeData Q ι Λ) (x : B.Pt) {lam : Wt L l n}
theorem ordChar_unit_mul_finite_component_eq_one (hN : _root_.GlobalTheory.Mem.LiteralAutomorphy.LiteratureRankNHypothesis) (hA : (hset Q R.τ₀).Ax) (v : V.frame.Vl) (i : Fin n)
    {ω : ((s.w v.1).adicCompletion L)ˣ →* ℂˣ} (ho : OrdChar ι (artOf (s.hw v.1)) ω (htW lam i) (x.psi v i))
    (u : Uo ((s.w v.1).adicCompletion L)) :
    (ω u : ℂ) * ι ((hset Q R.τ₀).finPart R.θ (x.comp (Λ.toPair B.u)).toRingHom lam v i u) = 1 := by
  have ha : IsLocalArtin (s.hw v.1) (artOf (s.hw v.1)) := hA.art_spec v
  obtain ⟨t, ht⟩ := ha.surj u
  have hd : deg t = 0 := by
    have := ha.ord_eq t
    rw [ht, ord_eq_zero u.2] at this
    omega
  have h1 := congrArg (fun z : Λ.Λˣ => x (Λ.toPair B.u z))
    (R.theta v i t (Subgroup.inclusion (Weil_le_Dec _) t) (deg_eq_zero_iff.1 hd) u rfl ht)
  have h2 := ord_val (w := s.w v.1) hN (hA.Sl_over v) ha ho t
  have h3 := congrArg (fun z : B.Bˣ => x z) (DFunLike.congr_fun (W.LamObj.toPair_spec r Λ B.u v i)
    ⟨Subgroup.inclusion (Weil_le_Dec _) t, deg_eq_zero_iff.1 hd⟩)
  have h4 : x (Λ.toPair B.u ((hset Q R.τ₀).epsV Λ.Λ v (Subgroup.inclusion (Weil_le_Dec _) t) : Λ.Λˣ)) = cycBar L Ω l t :=
    (x.comp (Λ.toPair B.u)).commutes _
  simp only [Units.val_mul, map_mul, Units.val_pow_eq_pow_val, map_pow, Units.val_one, map_one] at h1
  erw [h4] at h1
  rw [hd, ht, zpow_zero, one_pow, mul_one] at h2
  have h5 := (congrArg (· * _) (h2.symm.trans (congrArg (· * _) h3.symm))).trans h1
  rw [← ι.apply_symm_apply (ω u : ℂ), ← map_mul, ← map_one ι, ← h5]
  congr 1
  change _ * (x (Λ.toPair B.u _) / algPow (s.w v.1) _ _) = _
  rw [div_eq_inv_mul]
  ring
theorem norm_lowVal_inv_mul_psEig_eq_one_and_norm_sub_lt_one (hN : _root_.GlobalTheory.Mem.LiteralAutomorphy.LiteratureRankNHypothesis) (hA : (hset Q R.τ₀).Ax) (v : V.frame.Vl)
    {ω : Fin n → ((s.w v.1).adicCompletion L)ˣ →* ℂˣ} (ho : ∀ i, OrdChar ι (artOf (s.hw v.1)) (ω i) (htW lam i) (x.psi v i))
    (j : Fin n) : ‖ι.symm (((hset Q R.τ₀).lowVal ι lam v (j + 1))⁻¹ * psEig ω (j + 1))‖ = 1 ∧
      ‖ι.symm (((hset Q R.τ₀).lowVal ι lam v (j + 1))⁻¹ * psEig ω (j + 1)) - algebraMap c.O (Qbar l) (R.a (.inr (v, j)))‖ < 1 := by
  have ha : IsLocalArtin (s.hw v.1) (artOf (s.hw v.1)) := hA.art_spec v
  obtain ⟨t, ht⟩ := ha.surj (unif _)
  have hd : deg t = -1 := by
    have := ha.ord_eq t
    rw [ht, ord_eq_one GlAx.unif_spec] at this
    omega
  let g : Dec (F := L) (s.A v.1) := Subgroup.inclusion (Weil_le_Dec _) t
  let b : B.Bˣ := ∏ i with i ≤ j, B.u.ψ v i g * (hset Q R.τ₀).epsV B.B v g ^ (i : ℕ)
  have h1 : ι.symm (((hset Q R.τ₀).lowVal ι lam v (j + 1))⁻¹ * psEig ω (j + 1)) = x b := by
    have h4 : x ((hset Q R.τ₀).epsV B.B v g : B.Bˣ) = cycBar L Ω l t := x.commutes _
    have e : (Finset.univ.filter fun i : Fin n => (i : ℕ) < j + 1) = Finset.univ.filter fun i : Fin n => i ≤ j :=
      Finset.filter_congr fun i _ => Nat.lt_succ_iff
    simp only [b, HSet.lowVal, psEig, e, Units.coe_prod, map_prod, map_mul, map_inv₀, Units.val_mul, Units.val_pow_eq_pow_val,
      map_pow, h4, RingEquiv.symm_apply_apply, map_natCast]
    rw [← Finset.prod_inv_distrib, ← Finset.prod_mul_distrib]
    refine Finset.prod_congr rfl fun i _ => ?_
    have h2 := ord_val (w := s.w v.1) hN (hA.Sl_over v) ha (ho i) t
    rw [hd, ht, ← resCard_eq_normV, zpow_neg_one, inv_pow] at h2
    exact h2.symm
  rw [h1]
  refine ⟨pt_unit x b, pt_res x _ _ ?_⟩
  rw [← R.res.unif v j t g rfl ht]
  have h4 : Alg.res c.O B.B ((hset Q R.τ₀).epsV B.B v g : B.Bˣ) = ((hset Q R.τ₀).epsV (ResidueField c.O) v g : (ResidueField c.O)ˣ) :=
    (Alg.res c.O B.B).commutes _
  simp only [b, Units.coe_prod, map_prod, map_mul, Units.val_mul, Units.val_pow_eq_pow_val, map_pow, h4]
  exact Finset.prod_congr rfl fun i _ => congrArg (· * _) ((B.u.psi v i).red g)
end Set
end UpperTheory.V.Hi.Up
end


[HPT_SRC_EIGPOINT] Original residual-block eigenpoint supplier
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/HeckeAlgebras/UpOperatorEigenvalues/EigChar.lean#L1-L163
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.HeckeAlgebras.HeckeCommRing

theorem eigChar {A M ι K E T : Type} [CommRing A] [AddCommGroup M] [Module A M] [Field K] [AddCommGroup E] [Module K E]
    (Y : Submodule A M) [Module.Finite A Y] [Module.Free A Y] (op : ι → Module.End A M)
    [CommRing T] [Algebra A T] (j : T →ₐ[A] Module.End A Y) (t : ι → T) (ht : ∀ i, Restr (j (t i)) (op i))
    (hgen : Algebra.adjoin A (Set.range t) = ⊤)
    (φ : A →+* K) (sp : M → E) (hadd : ∀ f ∈ Y, ∀ f' ∈ Y, sp (f + f') = sp f + sp f')
    (hsmul : ∀ f ∈ Y, ∀ b : A, sp (b • f) = φ b • sp f)
    (hind : ∀ (κ : Type) (b : Module.Basis κ A Y), LinearIndependent K fun i => sp (b i))
    (opC : ι → Module.End K E) (hop : ∀ i, ∀ f ∈ Y, sp (op i f) = opC i (sp f))
    (F : E) (hF : F ∈ Submodule.span K (sp '' Y)) (h0 : F ≠ 0) (μ : ι → K) (hμ : ∀ i, opC i F = μ i • F) :
    ∃ χ : T →+* K, (∀ b, χ (algebraMap A T b) = φ b) ∧ ∀ i, χ (t i) = μ i := by
  classical
  haveI : Nontrivial A := φ.domain_nontrivial
  let κ := Module.Free.ChooseBasisIndex A Y
  let b : Module.Basis κ A Y := Module.Free.chooseBasis A Y
  let s : Y →ₛₗ[φ] E :=
    { toFun := fun y => sp y
      map_add' := fun y y' => hadd _ y.2 _ y'.2
      map_smul' := fun c y => hsmul _ y.2 c }
  let L : (κ → K) →ₗ[K] E := Fintype.linearCombination K fun i => sp (b i)
  have hL : Function.Injective L := (hind κ b).fintypeLinearCombination_injective
  have hs : ∀ y : Y, sp y = L fun i => φ (b.repr y i) := by
    intro y
    have : s y = s (∑ i, b.repr y i • b i) := by rw [b.sum_repr]
    rw [map_sum] at this
    simp only [map_smulₛₗ] at this
    exact this
  obtain ⟨w, rfl⟩ : F ∈ LinearMap.range L := by
    refine (Submodule.span_le.2 ?_) hF
    rintro _ ⟨y, hy, rfl⟩
    exact ⟨_, (hs ⟨y, hy⟩).symm⟩
  let ρ : T →+* Matrix κ κ K :=
    φ.mapMatrix.comp ((LinearMap.toMatrixAlgEquiv b).toRingEquiv.toRingHom.comp j.toRingHom)
  have hρ : ∀ u i k, ρ u i k = φ (b.repr (j u (b k)) i) := fun u i k =>
    congrArg φ (LinearMap.toMatrixAlgEquiv_apply b (j u) i k)
  have hρt : ∀ i, ρ (t i) *ᵥ w = μ i • w := by
    intro i
    refine hL ?_
    rw [map_smul, ← hμ]
    have : L ∘ₗ Matrix.toLin' (ρ (t i)) = opC i ∘ₗ L := by
      refine LinearMap.pi_ext' fun k => LinearMap.ext_ring ?_
      simp only [LinearMap.comp_apply, LinearMap.single_apply, Matrix.toLin'_apply, Matrix.mulVec_single_one]
      have h1 : L (Pi.single k 1) = sp (b k) := by simp [L]
      rw [h1, ← hop i _ (b k).2, ← ht i, hs]
      congr 1
      funext i'
      exact hρ _ _ _
    exact LinearMap.congr_fun this w
  have hρa : ∀ a, ρ (algebraMap A T a) *ᵥ w = φ a • w := by
    intro a
    have : ρ (algebraMap A T a) = φ a • (1 : Matrix κ κ K) := by
      ext i k
      rw [hρ, AlgHom.commutes, Module.algebraMap_end_apply, map_smul, b.repr_self]
      by_cases h : i = k
      · subst h; simp
      · simp [h, Finsupp.single_apply, Ne.symm h]
    rw [this, smul_mulVec, one_mulVec]
  have hall : ∀ u : T, ∃ c : K, ρ u *ᵥ w = c • w := by
    intro u
    have hu : u ∈ Algebra.adjoin A (Set.range t) := hgen ▸ Algebra.mem_top
    induction hu using Algebra.adjoin_induction with
    | mem x hx => obtain ⟨i, rfl⟩ := hx; exact ⟨_, hρt i⟩
    | algebraMap r => exact ⟨_, hρa r⟩
    | add x y _ _ hx hy =>
      obtain ⟨c, hc⟩ := hx
      obtain ⟨d, hd⟩ := hy
      exact ⟨c + d, by rw [map_add, add_mulVec, hc, hd, add_smul]⟩
    | mul x y _ _ hx hy =>
      obtain ⟨c, hc⟩ := hx
      obtain ⟨d, hd⟩ := hy
      exact ⟨c * d, by rw [map_mul, ← mulVec_mulVec, hd, mulVec_smul, hc, smul_smul, mul_comm]⟩
  choose χ hχ using hall
  have hw : w ≠ 0 := fun h => h0 (by rw [h, map_zero])
  have hu : ∀ u c, ρ u *ᵥ w = c • w → χ u = c := fun u c h => smul_left_injective K hw ((hχ u).symm.trans h)
  refine ⟨{ toFun := χ
            map_one' := hu _ _ (by simp)
            map_mul' := fun x y => hu _ _ (by rw [map_mul, ← mulVec_mulVec, hχ, mulVec_smul, hχ, smul_smul, mul_comm])
            map_zero' := hu _ _ (by simp)
            map_add' := fun x y => hu _ _ (by rw [map_add, add_mulVec, hχ, hχ, add_smul]) },
    fun a => hu _ _ (hρa a), fun i => hu _ _ (hρt i)⟩

theorem intIff {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] (x : PadicAlgCl l) : IsIntegral ℤ_[l] x ↔ ‖x‖ ≤ 1 := by
  have hi : IsIntegral ℚ_[l] x := Algebra.IsIntegral.isIntegral x
  have h0 : ‖x‖ = spectralValue (minpoly ℚ_[l] x) := rfl
  rw [h0, spectralValue_le_one_iff (minpoly.monic hi)]
  constructor
  · intro h n
    rw [minpoly.isIntegrallyClosed_eq_field_fractions' ℚ_[l] h, Polynomial.coeff_map]
    exact PadicInt.norm_le_one _
  · intro h
    have hl' : minpoly ℚ_[l] x ∈ Polynomial.lifts (algebraMap ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l]) :=
      (Polynomial.lifts_iff_coeff_lifts _).mpr fun n => ⟨⟨_, h n⟩, rfl⟩
    obtain ⟨q, hq, -, hqm⟩ := Polynomial.lifts_and_degree_eq_and_monic hl' (minpoly.monic hi)
    exact ⟨q, hqm, by rw [← Polynomial.aeval_def, ← Polynomial.aeval_map_algebraMap ℚ_[l], hq, minpoly.aeval]⟩

open Polynomial in
theorem normLt {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {O R : Type} [CommRing O] [Algebra ℤ_[l] O]
    [Algebra O (PadicAlgCl l)] [IsScalarTower ℤ_[l] O (PadicAlgCl l)] [CommRing R] [IsLocalRing R] [Algebra O R]
    (y : R →ₐ[O] PadicAlgCl l) (a : R) (ha : a ∈ maximalIdeal R) : ‖y a‖ < 1 :=
  _root_.UpperTheory.Reformulated.B.NormLtOne.norm_lt_one y a ha

theorem eigPt {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {A M ι E : Type} [CommRing A] [IsCNL A] [AddCommGroup M] [Module A M]
    [AddCommGroup E] [Module ℂ E]
    (Y : Submodule A M) [Module.Finite A Y] [Module.Free A Y] (op : ι → Module.End A M) (hC : CommOn Y op)
    (e : PadicAlgCl l ≃+* ℂ) (x : A →+* PadicAlgCl l) (hx1 : ∀ b, ‖x b‖ ≤ 1) (hx2 : ∀ b ∈ maximalIdeal A, ‖x b‖ < 1)
    (sp : M → E) (hadd : ∀ f ∈ Y, ∀ f' ∈ Y, sp (f + f') = sp f + sp f')
    (hsmul : ∀ f ∈ Y, ∀ b : A, sp (b • f) = e (x b) • sp f)
    (hind : ∀ (κ : Type) (b : Module.Basis κ A Y), LinearIndependent ℂ fun i => sp (b i))
    (opC : ι → Module.End ℂ E) (hop : ∀ i, ∀ f ∈ Y, sp (op i f) = opC i (sp f))
    (F : E) (hF : F ∈ Submodule.span ℂ (sp '' Y)) (h0 : F ≠ 0) (μ : ι → ℂ) (hμ : ∀ i, opC i F = μ i • F)
    (a : ι → A) (ha : ∀ i, ‖e.symm (μ i) - x (a i)‖ < 1) [Fact (CommOn (locPart Y op a) op)] :
    locPart Y op a ≠ ⊥ ∧ ∃ y : heckeAlg (locPart Y op a) op →+* PadicAlgCl l,
      (∀ b, y (algebraMap A _ b) = x b) ∧ ∀ i, e (y (heckeGen _ op i)) = μ i := by
  obtain ⟨T, _, _, j, t, hj, hr, ht, hgen, -⟩ := UpperTheory.V.AlgebraTools.AnnihilatorQuotientFinite.heckeModel A M ι Y op hC
  obtain ⟨χ, h1, h2⟩ := eigChar Y op j t ht hgen (e.toRingHom.comp x) sp hadd hsmul hind opC hop F hF h0 μ hμ
  let χ' : T →+* PadicAlgCl l := e.symm.toRingHom.comp χ
  have h1' : ∀ b, χ' (algebraMap A T b) = x b := fun b => by
    change e.symm (χ _) = _
    rw [h1]; exact e.symm_apply_apply _
  have h2' : ∀ i, χ' (t i) = e.symm (μ i) := fun i => congrArg e.symm (h2 i)
  have hmem : ∀ u, χ' u ∈ Valued.integer (PadicAlgCl l) := by
    intro u
    have hu : u ∈ Algebra.adjoin A (Set.range t) := hgen ▸ Algebra.mem_top
    induction hu using Algebra.adjoin_induction with
    | mem x' hx =>
      obtain ⟨i, rfl⟩ := hx
      have : χ' (t i) = (e.symm (μ i) - x (a i)) + x (a i) := by rw [h2']; ring
      rw [this]
      exact add_mem (Valued.integer.mem_iff.2 (ha i).le) (Valued.integer.mem_iff.2 (hx1 _))
    | algebraMap r => rw [h1']; exact Valued.integer.mem_iff.2 (hx1 _)
    | add x y _ _ hx hy => rw [map_add]; exact add_mem hx hy
    | mul x y _ _ hx hy => rw [map_mul]; exact mul_mem hx hy
  haveI : IsLocalRing (Valued.integer (PadicAlgCl l)) :=
    inferInstanceAs (IsLocalRing (Valued.v (R := PadicAlgCl l)).valuationSubring)
  have hm : ∀ z : Valued.integer (PadicAlgCl l), ‖(z : PadicAlgCl l)‖ < 1 → z ∈ maximalIdeal _ := fun z hz hu =>
    hz.ne (Valued.integer.isUnit_iff_norm_eq_one.1 hu)
  obtain ⟨r, -, hr2, hr3⟩ := UpperTheory.V.AlgebraTools.AnnihilatorQuotientFinite.heckeRestr A M ι Y op a hC T (heckeAlg (locPart Y op a) op) j
    (Subalgebra.val _) hj hr Subtype.val_injective (Subalgebra.range_val _)
  obtain ⟨hne, x', hx'⟩ := hr3 (Valued.integer (PadicAlgCl l)) (χ'.codRestrict _ hmem)
    (fun c hc => hm _ (by
      change ‖χ' _‖ < 1
      rw [h1']; exact hx2 c hc))
    (fun i s hs => hm _ (by
      have : s = t i := hj (LinearMap.ext fun y => Subtype.ext ((hs y).trans (ht i y).symm))
      change ‖χ' _ - χ' _‖ < 1
      rw [this, h1', h2']; exact ha i))
  have hx'' : ∀ u, (x' (r u) : PadicAlgCl l) = χ' u := fun u => congrArg Subtype.val (RingHom.congr_fun hx' u)
  refine ⟨hne, (Subring.subtype _).comp x', fun b => ?_, fun i => ?_⟩
  · rw [← r.commutes, ← h1']; exact hx'' _
  · have : r (t i) = heckeGen _ op i :=
      Subtype.ext (LinearMap.ext fun y => Subtype.ext ((hr2 (t i) y).trans (ht i _)))
    rw [← this]
    change e (x' (r (t i)) : PadicAlgCl l) = _
    rw [hx'', h2']; exact e.apply_symm_apply _

end UpperTheory.V.Hi.Up

variable {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {L Ω : Type} [Field L] [NumberField L] [IsCMField L] [Field Ω] [Algebra L Ω] [IsAlgClosure L Ω]
  {s : HidaSetting l L Ω} {n : ℕ} {V : s.Lev} {Q : QData V} {c : Coef l} {r : Resid V.frame c.O n} {ι : Qbar l ≃+* ℂ} {B : UniversalOrdinaryDeterminantRing r}
private theorem split_w {v : Plc (Lp L)} (hv : v ∈ s.S) : IsSplitAt (s.w v) :=
  UpperTheory.isSplitAt_of_decomposition_le_restriction_range L Ω (s.A v) v (s.hA v) (s.split v hv) _ (under_w v)
private theorem types (hn : 1 ≤ n) (x : B.Pt) {σ : Submodule ℂ (UA L n → ℂ)} {ρ : G L Ω →* GL (Fin n) (Qbar l)} (hσ : IsAutRepU σ)
    (hs : Formalism.MainNotions.N.IsSSRep ρ) (hx : x.IsRep ρ) (hSt : _root_.UpperTheory.V.Z.Desc.W.StrongEquivalenceHida s ι σ ρ)
    (t : Fin n → V.frame.G₀ →* c.Oˣ) (ht : r.IsTeich t) (u : s.St) :
    ∃ τ : SmIrr (GLw n (s.w u)), IsCompAlong σ (locU n (s.w u)) ((cLoc (s.w u)).comp (locU n (s.w u)).subtype) τ.ρ ∧
      ∀ rc, _root_.UpperTheory.Zero.LLC.IsRecT (s.hw u) rc → IsTypeWD (s.hw u) (fun i => W.pushCh (ι.toRingHom.comp (algebraMap c.O (Qbar l)))
        ((t i).comp (V.pr.comp (Ine (F := L) (s.A u)).subtype))) (rc (algNorm τ)) := by
  have hw : IsSplitAt (s.w u) := split_w (Or.inr (Or.inl u.2))
  have hu : ((l : ℕ) : 𝓞 L) ∉ (s.w u).asIdeal := fun h => s.St_l u u.2 (by
    rw [← under_w (s := s) u]
    change algebraMap _ (𝓞 L) _ ∈ (s.w u).asIdeal
    rwa [map_natCast])
  obtain ⟨τ, hτ⟩ := comp_ex hσ hw
  obtain ⟨P, hP⟩ := diag_pt x V.pr ρ hx hn hs t ht u (Ine (F := L) (s.A u)) fun g hg => ⟨g, hg, rfl⟩
  suffices hop : ∀ i, IsOpen ((W.pushCh (algebraMap c.O (Qbar l)) ((t i).comp (V.pr.comp (Ine (F := L) (s.A u)).subtype))).ker :
      Set (Ine (F := L) (s.A u))) from ⟨τ, hτ, fun rc hrc => wd_type hu _ hop P hP (hSt u τ hu hτ rc hrc)⟩
  intro i
  obtain ⟨-, hfin, -⟩ := UpperTheory.V.abelian₂.dvr_finite l c.O
  refine Subgroup.isOpen_mono (H₁ := (r.chib i).ker.comap (V.pr.comp (Ine (F := L) (s.A u)).subtype)) (fun g hg => ?_)
    ((r.chib_open i).preimage ((V.pr_cont W.continuous_resP).comp continuous_subtype_val))
  have h1 : residue c.O (t i (V.pr g)) = 1 := (ht i _).1.trans (congrArg Units.val hg)
  have h2 : ((t i (V.pr g) : c.O)) ^ (Nat.card (ResidueField c.O) - 1) = 1 :=
    (Units.val_pow_eq_pow_val _ _).symm.trans (congrArg Units.val (ht i _).2)
  have h3 := geom_sum_mul ((t i (V.pr g) : c.O)) (Nat.card (ResidueField c.O) - 1)
  rw [h2, sub_self, mul_eq_zero] at h3
  have h4 : t i (V.pr g) = 1 := Units.ext (sub_eq_zero.1 (h3.resolve_left fun h5 => by
    have h6 := congrArg (residue c.O) h5
    simp only [map_sum, map_pow, h1, one_pow, Finset.sum_const, Finset.card_range, nsmul_one, map_zero] at h6
    rw [Nat.cast_pred Nat.card_pos] at h6
    letI := Fintype.ofFinite (ResidueField c.O)
    simp [Nat.card_eq_fintype_card] at h6))
  exact (congrArg (Units.map (algebraMap c.O (Qbar l) : c.O →* Qbar l)) h4).trans (map_one _)
private theorem chi_map {A A' H : Type} [CommRing A] [CommRing A'] [Group H] (D : W.GDet n A H) (f : A →+* A') (g : H) :
    (D.map f).chi (MonoidAlgebra.single g 1) = (D.chi (MonoidAlgebra.single g 1)).map f := by
  rw [← (AlgebraTools.Det.mapApi D f).1, MonoidAlgebra.mapRingHom_single, map_one]
