theorem UpperTheory.V.Z.Desc.W.upU_w_alt :
    _root_.UpperTheory.V.Af.HypothesesH' → ∀ (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (L Ω : Type) [Field L] [NumberField L] [IsCMField L] [Field Ω] [Algebra L Ω] [IsAlgClosure L Ω] (s : HidaSetting l L Ω) (n : ℕ), 1 ≤ n →
    ∀ (V : s.Lev) (Q : QData V) (c : Coef l), ∀ (r : Resid V.frame c.O n) (ι : Qbar l ≃+* ℂ), ∀ (B : UniversalOrdinaryDeterminantRing r) (Λ : LamObj r) (R : _root_.UpperTheory.V.Hi.CaseU.UnitaryHeckeData Q ι Λ) (K : (hset Q R.τ₀).Lev), V.S' = s.S → ∀ J : Ideal B.B, R.IsJ B K J → ∀ (x : B.Pt) (h : s.Wt n) (lam : Wt L l n), x.Arith h → WtOf h lam → ∀ (σ : Submodule ℂ (UA L n → ℂ)) (ρ : G L Ω →* GL (Fin n) (Qbar l)), IsAutRepU σ → Continuous ρ → Formalism.MainNotions.N.IsSSRep ρ → x.IsRep ρ → SphEq ι σ ρ → (∀ y : Emb l (fun v : V.frame.Vl => s.w v.1), HasWeightAt σ (ι.toRingHom.comp y.res) (lam y.res).dual) → (∀ w : Plc L, w.under (𝓞 (Lp L)) ∉ V.S' → IsUnramU σ w) → (∃ τ : SmIrr (GLw n (s.w s.v0)), IsCompAlong σ (locU n (s.w s.v0)) ((cLoc (s.w s.v0)).comp (locU n (s.w s.v0)).subtype) τ.ρ ∧ fixd τ.ρ (Kc _ n 1) ≠ ⊥) → (∀ v : V.frame.Vl, ∃ (τ : SmIrr (GLw n (s.w v.1))) (ω : Fin n → ((s.w v.1).adicCompletion L)ˣ →* ℂˣ), IsCompAlong σ (locU n (s.w v.1)) ((cLoc (s.w v.1)).comp (locU n (s.w v.1)).subtype) τ.ρ ∧ Nonempty (τ.ρ.Equiv (PS ω)) ∧ ∀ i, OrdChar ι (artOf (s.hw v.1)) (ω i) (htW lam i) (x.psi v i)) → _root_.UpperTheory.V.Z.Desc.W.StrongEquivalenceHida s ι σ ρ → J ≤ RingHom.ker x := by
  intro hP l _ L Ω _ _ _ _ _ _ s n hn V Q c r ι B Λ R K hS J hJ x h lam hx hlam σ ρ hσ hρ hss hrep hloc hwt hunr h0 hord hSt
  have hN : _root_.GlobalTheory.Mem.LiteralAutomorphy.LiteratureRankNHypothesis := hP.litN
  have hA := _root_.UpperTheory.V.Hi.axU_s hN l L Ω s n hn V Q c R.m R.τ₀
  have har := ((_root_.UpperTheory.V.Hi.arith_good_iff_hset_of_theta_incr_weight hN l L Ω s n hn V Q c R.m R.τ₀ r B Λ R.θ R.theta h hx.1).2 lam hlam x).1.1 hx
  obtain ⟨d, ξ, c0, sp, hsp⟩ := spec L Ω l n c _ hA Λ.Λ R.θ R.isWt ι _ lam har
  obtain ⟨e, he⟩ := hsp.alg
  obtain ⟨Y, -, -, hY, hYl⟩ := _root_.UpperTheory.V.Z.Desc.P.partU_alt hP l L Ω s n hn V Q c r ι R.m R.τ₀ R.type lam d e ξ he σ hσ
    (_root_.UpperTheory.V.Hi.nonempty_unitary_hecke_parts hP l L Ω s n hn V Q c r ι R.m R.τ₀ R.type lam d e ξ he σ hσ hwt hunr h0 (types hn x hσ hss hrep hSt))
  choose τ ω hτ hω ho using hord
  have hfr : ∀ w ∈ (hset Q R.τ₀).Wp, ∀ (A : ValuationSubring Ω) (g : G L Ω), IsPlaceOver A w → IsFrob A (normV w) g →
      (ρ g⁻¹ : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) (Qbar l)).charpoly = (Y.r (V.pr g⁻¹) : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) (Qbar l)).charpoly := by
    intro w hw A g hAw hg
    obtain ⟨π, hπ⟩ := comp_ex hσ hw.2
    have hl := hA.Wp_away w hw
    obtain ⟨z, hz, h0⟩ := (Submodule.ne_bot_iff _).1 (GlobalTheory.SphBC.isSpherical_of_isCompAlong_split hσ hw.2 π.ρ hπ (hunr w hw.1))
    obtain ⟨b, hb⟩ := (_root_.UpperTheory.V.Af.satake_local_of_printed_results hP.toClassicalTheorems L n w π).1 ⟨z, h0, GlobalTheory.LGrp.mem_fixed.1 hz⟩
    have h1 := (hloc w hw.2 hl π b hπ hb A hAw).2 g hg
    have h2 := (_root_.UpperTheory.V.Z.Desc.W.satFrobT_alt hP.toClassicalTheorems L Ω l ι n _ (Y.cont.comp (V.pr_cont W.continuous_resP)) w hl π b hb
      (_root_.UpperTheory.V.Z.Desc.W.lgAway' (hYl w hw.2 π hπ) hl) A hAw).2 g hg
    exact Polynomial.map_injective ι.toRingHom ι.injective (h1.trans h2.symm)
  have hD : B.u.D.map (x : B.B →+* Qbar l) = W.GDet.ofRep (W.mat Y.r) := by
    refine AlgebraTools.Det.extDense _ _ _ hA.dense (fun i _ => ?_)
      (fun i _ => AlgebraTools.Det.repCont _ (Units.continuous_val.comp Y.cont) i) ?_
    · simp only [(AlgebraTools.Det.mapApi B.u.D (x : B.B →+* Qbar l)).2.2.1]
      exact pt_cont x (B.u.cont i)
    · rintro _ ⟨w, hw, A, g, hAw, hg, rfl⟩
      exact (chi_map _ _ _).trans ((chi_pt x V.pr ρ hrep _).trans
        ((hfr w hw A g hAw hg).trans ((AlgebraTools.Det.repApi (W.mat Y.r)).2.1 _).symm))
  have hsph : ∀ (w : (hset Q R.τ₀).Wp) (j : Fin n),
      ‖ι.symm (Y.a w (j + 1)) - algebraMap c.O (Qbar l) (R.a (.inl (w, j)))‖ < 1 := by
    intro w j
    have hAw := isPlaceOver_placeOf Ω w.1
    have hg := deg_eq_one_iff.1 (deg_frob (h := hAw))
    have hq : IsUnit ((normV w.1 : ℕ) : B.B) := by
      have := ((cycZ L Ω l (frob hAw)).isUnit.map (algebraMap ℤ_[l] c.O)).map (algebraMap c.O B.B)
      rwa [cycZ_of_isFrob (hA.Wp_away _ w.2) hAw hg, map_natCast, map_natCast] at this
    have hq' := pt_unit x hq.unit
    rw [IsUnit.unit_spec, map_natCast] at hq'
    have := hecke_cong (algebraMap c.O (Qbar l)) ι hq' _ (Y.a w)
      (fun j => if h : 0 < j ∧ j - 1 < n then R.a (.inl (w, ⟨j - 1, h.2⟩)) else 1)
      ((congrArg _ (hfr _ w.2 _ _ hAw hg)).trans (Y.frob w _ _ hAw hg))
      (chi_cong x V.pr ρ hrep _ _ (by
        refine (R.res.frob w _ _ hAw hg).trans ?_
        rw [hecke_map]
        congr 1
        funext j
        split_ifs <;> simp)) (j + 1) j.2
    rwa [dif_pos ⟨Nat.succ_pos _, j.2⟩] at this
  have hfin := fun v i => _root_.UpperTheory.V.Hi.Up.ordChar_unit_mul_finite_component_eq_one R x hN hA v i (ho v i)
  have up : ∀ [Fact (CommOn (R.M K) ((hset Q R.τ₀).op Λ.Λ))], _ :=
    _root_.UpperTheory.V.Hi.coreUp_alt hN L Ω l n c _ hA Λ.Λ R.θ R.isWt ι _ lam d ξ c0 sp hsp Y K
      (fun q => absurd (hS ▸ Q.range ▸ Or.inr ⟨q, rfl⟩) (Q.notS q)) ω
      (fun v => (UpperTheory.building_component_uniqueness L n σ hσ _ (split_w (Or.inl v.2)) _ _ _ _ (hY v) (hτ v)).elim fun a => (hω v).elim fun b =>
        ⟨a.trans b⟩) hfin R.a hsph (fun v => _root_.UpperTheory.V.Hi.Up.norm_lowVal_inv_mul_psEig_eq_one_and_norm_sub_lt_one R x hN hA v (ho v))
  rcases hJ with ⟨hb, -⟩ | ⟨hc, hco, f, -, hmap, -, -, rfl⟩
  · haveI := Fact.mk ((hiComm L Ω l n c _ hA Λ.Λ R.θ R.isWt K).2.2.2 R.a).1
    exact absurd hb up.1
  obtain ⟨-, y, hy, hy1, hy2⟩ := up
  obtain ⟨F, hF, hF0⟩ := (Submodule.ne_bot_iff _).1 Y.ne
  obtain ⟨k1, k2⟩ := _root_.UpperTheory.V.Hi.core_eigen_of_weight_char_hecke_level hN L Ω l n c _ hA Λ.Λ R.θ R.isWt ι R.a K _ _ hmap y lam d ξ Y F hF hF0 fun w j => by
    rw [hy1]
    exact Y.sph w _ F hF
  obtain ⟨hcp, hfn, hlm, -⟩ := UpperTheory.V.abelian₂.dvr_finite l c.O
  haveI : CharP (ResidueField c.O) l := by
    refine (CharP.charP_iff_prime_eq_zero Fact.out).2 ?_
    rw [← map_natCast (residue c.O), residue_eq_zero_iff]
    exact hlm
  have key : x = y.comp f := by
    refine pt_ext _ x _ (UpperTheory.V.CA.exists_adjoin_coeff_character_sub_mem_maximalIdeal_pow r B) ?_
    rintro _ (⟨i, g, -, -, rfl⟩ | ⟨v, i, g, rfl⟩)
    · have e1 := (AlgebraTools.Det.mapApi B.u.D (x : B.B →+* Qbar l)).2.2.1 i g
      have e2 := (AlgebraTools.Det.mapApi (B.u.D.map f.toRingHom) y.toRingHom).2.2.1 i g
      rw [(AlgebraTools.Det.mapApi B.u.D f.toRingHom).2.2.1 i g] at e2
      rw [hD] at e1
      exact e1.symm.trans ((congrArg (fun D : W.GDet n (Qbar l) V.frame.G₀ => D.lam i (MonoidAlgebra.single g 1)) k1).symm.trans e2)
    · exact congrArg Units.val (DFunLike.congr_fun (ord_uniq (ho v i)
        (k2 v (ω v) (fun i => (ho v i).smooth) (by rw [hy]; exact hfin v) (hy2 v) i)) g)
  intro b hb
  rw [RingHom.mem_ker] at hb ⊢
  rw [key, AlgHom.comp_apply, hb, map_zero]
end


[HPT_SRC_HECKE] TypeForAlt, actual UnitaryPointProperties and IsJ
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/ModularityArgument/ThresholdArguments/DenseThresholdCases/UnitaryHeckeData.lean#L27-L74
      ∀ u rc, _root_.UpperTheory.Zero.LLC.IsRecT (ht u) rc → IsTypeWD (ht u) (χ u) (rc (algNorm (σ u))))
end Type_
variable {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {L Ω : Type} [Field L] [NumberField L] [IsCMField L] [Field Ω] [Algebra L Ω] [IsAlgClosure L Ω]
namespace CaseU
variable (l L) (n : ℕ)
variable {l L}
variable (s : HidaSetting l L Ω)
variable {s} (V : s.Lev)
variable {V} (Q : QData V)
variable {n} {c : Coef l} {m : ℕ}
variable (r : Resid V.frame c.O n) (ι : Qbar l ≃+* ℂ)
def TypeForAlt (τ₀ : KT n (wt s) →* GL (Fin m) c.O) : Prop :=
  ∀ t, r.IsTeich t → _root_.UpperTheory.V.Hi.IsTypeAlt (fun u : s.St => s.hw u)
    ((Matrix.GeneralLinearGroup.map (ι.toRingHom.comp (algebraMap c.O (Qbar l)))).comp τ₀)
    fun u i => W.pushCh (ι.toRingHom.comp (algebraMap c.O (Qbar l))) ((t i).comp (V.pr.comp (Ine (F := L) (s.A u)).subtype))
variable (τ₀ : KT n (wt s) →* GL (Fin m) c.O)
structure UnitaryPointProperties (lam : Wt L l n) (ρ : V.frame.G₀ →* GL (Fin n) (Qbar l)) : Prop where
  form : ∃ P, V.frame.IsForm c.O n ρ P
  type : ∀ t, r.IsTeich t → ∀ u : s.St, ∃ g : GL (Fin n) (Qbar l), ∀ x ∈ V.frame.Iu u,
    ((g * ρ x * g⁻¹ : GL (Fin n) (Qbar l)) : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) (Qbar l)) =
      Matrix.diagonal fun i => algebraMap c.O (Qbar l) (t i x)
  cusp : Af.IsIrr ρ → ∃ π : CuspSub L n, (CG.mk π).IsRegAlg ∧ GlobalTheory.ContraPair.DualIsConj π ∧ Af.AttC ι (ρ.comp V.pr) π ∧ HasWt ι s π lam
  aut : ∃ σ : Submodule ℂ (UA L n → ℂ), IsAutRepU σ ∧ (∀ w, IsSplitAt w → ∀ τ : SmIrr (GLw n w),
      IsCompAlong σ (locU n w) ((cLoc w).comp (locU n w).subtype) τ.ρ → _root_.UpperTheory.V.Z.Win.LocGlobT ι (ρ.comp V.pr) w τ) ∧
    (∀ y : Emb l (fun v : V.frame.Vl => s.w v.1), HasWeightAt σ (ι.toRingHom.comp y.res) (lam y.res).dual) ∧
    (∀ w : Plc L, w.under (𝓞 (Lp L)) ∉ V.S' → IsUnramU σ w) ∧
    (∀ w, IsSplitAt w → ∃ τ : SmIrr (GLw n w), IsCompAlong σ (locU n w) ((cLoc w).comp (locU n w).subtype) τ.ρ) ∧
    ∃ τ : SmIrr (GLw n (s.w s.v0)), IsCompAlong σ (locU n (s.w s.v0)) ((cLoc (s.w s.v0)).comp (locU n (s.w s.v0)).subtype) τ.ρ ∧
      fixd τ.ρ (Kc _ n 1) ≠ ⊥
variable {lam : Wt L l n} {d : ℕ}
  (e : (Fin d → ℂ) ≃ₗ[ℂ] ⨂[ℂ] e : Emb l (fun v : V.frame.Vl => s.w v.1), nablaSq ℂ n (lam e.res))
variable {r} (B : UniversalOrdinaryDeterminantRing r) (Λ : LamObj r)
structure UnitaryHeckeData : Type where
  m : ℕ
  τ₀ : KT n (wt s) →* GL (Fin m) c.O
  type : _root_.UpperTheory.V.Hi.CaseU.TypeForAlt r ι τ₀
  θ : (hset Q τ₀).X.I.T →* Λ.Λˣ
  theta : IsTheta Q τ₀ Λ θ
  isWt : (hset Q τ₀).IsWt θ
  a : (hset Q τ₀).Op → c.O
  res : IsRes Q r τ₀ a
variable {Q ι Λ} (R : _root_.UpperTheory.V.Hi.CaseU.UnitaryHeckeData Q ι Λ) (K : (hset Q R.τ₀).Lev)
abbrev UnitaryHeckeData.M := (hset Q R.τ₀).MK R.θ R.a K
def UnitaryHeckeData.IsJ (J : Ideal B.B) : Prop := (hset Q R.τ₀).IsJ R.θ R.a K B.u.D B.u.ψ (Λ.toPair B.u) J
end CaseU
end UpperTheory.V.Hi
end
end


[HPT_SRC_NORMALIZATION] Original PS, psEig, htW and local ordinary definitions
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/HeckeAlgebras/Distinct.lean#L1-L129
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.ModularityArgument.SemisimpleLevels.CsdSsOfFull
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.HeckeAlgebras.OrdinaryHeckeCharacters.IndexNeZero
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.HeckeAlgebras.OrdinaryHeckeCharacters.DivMono
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.AutomorphicForms.LocIsotypic
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.WeilGroupCharacters.UniversalOrdinaryDeterminantRing
import Development.AbelianVarietyTheory.PublishedTheorems.PublishedFoundations.FiniteIntertwining

noncomputable section

open NumberField IsDedekindDomain Formalism.ConjugateSelfDual ReciprocityTheory.Gal Formalism.BasicNotions Formalism.BasicNotions.Loc Formalism.MainNotions.N Polynomial ValuativeRel
open GlobalTheory.CuspRep GlobalTheory.WeilDeligne GlobalTheory.Pst GlobalTheory.LocLanglands GlobalTheory.LocGL GlobalTheory.GalOfCusp GlobalTheory.SmGL GlobalTheory.SplitWeil

attribute [local instance] GlobalTheory.LocalData.complRel GlobalTheory.LocalData.isLocalField

namespace UpperTheory.V.Hi

section Lin

variable (k : Type) [Field k] [ValuativeRel k] [TopologicalSpace k] [IsNonarchimedeanLocalField k] (n : ℕ)

def glIwa : Iwa (GL (Fin n) k) := (glData k n).toIwa (glAx k n)

def piIwa {σ : Type} [Finite σ] {G : σ → Type} [∀ v, Group (G v)] (I : ∀ v, Iwa (G v)) : Iwa (∀ v, G v) :=
  (piData I).toIwa (piAx σ G I)

variable {k n}

abbrev PS (ω : Fin n → kˣ →* ℂˣ) := prinSeries fun i => ω i * absPow k (((n : ℂ) - 1) / 2)

def psEig (ω : Fin n → kˣ →* ℂˣ) (j : ℕ) : ℂ :=
  ∏ i ∈ Finset.univ.filter fun i : Fin n => (i : ℕ) < j, (ω i (unif k) : ℂ) * ((Nat.card 𝓀[k] : ℂ) ^ (i : ℕ))⁻¹

def IsInvOn (φ : (glIwa k n).T →* ℂˣ) (ω : Fin n → kˣ →* ℂˣ) : Prop := ∀ i (y : Uo k), φ (eT k n i y) = (ω i y)⁻¹

def Distinct {C : Type} [Monoid C] (φ : (glIwa k n).T →* C) : Prop := ∀ i j, i ≠ j → ∃ y, φ (eT k n i y) ≠ φ (eT k n j y)

def invPart {V : Type} [AddCommGroup V] [Module ℂ V] (π : Representation ℂ (GL (Fin n) k) V) (c : ℕ)
    (φ : (glIwa k n).T →* ℂˣ) : Submodule ℂ V :=
  ⨅ j : ℕ, ((glIwa k n).eig π c φ).map ((glIwa k n).uOp π (glIwa k n).z ^ j)

def sphOp {A V : Type} [CommRing A] [AddCommGroup V] [Module A V] (π : Representation A (GL (Fin n) k) V) (j : ℕ) :
    Module.End A V := dc π (KO k (Fin n)) (KO k (Fin n)) (al k n j)

end Lin

def heckePol {A : Type} [CommRing A] (n q : ℕ) (a : ℕ → A) : A[X] :=
  ∑ j ∈ Finset.range (n + 1), C ((-1) ^ j * (q : A) ^ (j * (j - 1) / 2) * a j) * X ^ (n - j)

section Gal

variable {L Ω : Type} [Field L] [NumberField L] [Field Ω] [Algebra L Ω] [IsAlgClosure L Ω] {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {n : ℕ}

def algPow (w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 L)) (h : (L →+* Qbar l) → ℤ) (y : w.adicCompletion L) : Qbar l :=
  ∏ᶠ j : {j : w.adicCompletion L →+* Qbar l // Continuous j}, j.1 y ^ h (j.1.comp (algebraMap L (w.adicCompletion L)))

variable (L l n) in

abbrev Wt : Type := (L →+* Qbar l) → DomWeight n

omit [NumberField L] in

def lowW (lam : Wt L l n) (j : Fin n) (τ : L →+* Qbar l) : ℤ := (lam τ).w j.rev

omit [NumberField L] in

def htW (lam : Wt L l n) (j : Fin n) (τ : L →+* Qbar l) : ℤ := lowW lam j τ + (j : ℕ)

variable (ι : Qbar l ≃+* ℂ) {A : ValuationSubring Ω} {w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 L)} (h : IsPlaceOver A w)

def DRat (r : Dec (F := L) A →* GL (Fin n) (Qbar l)) (HT : (L →+* Qbar l) → Multiset ℤ) (P : Weil h → ℂ[X]) : Prop :=
  ∀ (O : Type) [CommRing O] [Algebra ℤ_[l] O] [TopologicalSpace O] (Bw : LocBridge l L Ω (Dec (F := L) A) O)
    (hj : GlobalTheory.Pst.Induces Bw), IsdRAt (Loc.PP l) Bw r ∧
      (∀ (τ : L →+* Qbar l) (LL' : FinExt l) (τ' : ↥Bw.Kw.1 →ₐ[ℚ_[l]] ↥LL'.1), IsInduced Bw τ τ' →
        IsJumpsAt (Loc.PP l) Bw r τ (HT τ)) ∧
      ∀ s, (wdAt (Loc.PP l) ι Bw h hj r).charPol s = P s

def AtL (r : Dec (F := L) A →* GL (Fin n) (Qbar l)) (lam : Wt L l n) (σ : SmIrr (GL (Fin n) (w.adicCompletion L))) : Prop :=
  ∀ ω : Fin n → (w.adicCompletion L)ˣ →* ℂˣ, (∀ i, IsSmoothChar (ω i)) →
    (∀ i j, i ≠ j → ∃ y : Uo (w.adicCompletion L), ω i y ≠ ω j y) → Nonempty (σ.ρ.Equiv (PS ω)) →
  ∀ rc, IsRec h rc → Lev.AbW (rc (algNorm σ)) → DRat ι h r (fun τ => Finset.univ.val.map fun j => htW lam j τ) fun s => (rc (algNorm σ)).charPol s

omit [NumberField L] [IsAlgClosure L Ω] in
variable (A) in

def PotCrys (r : Dec (F := L) A →* GL (Fin n) (Qbar l)) : Prop :=
  ∀ (O : Type) [CommRing O] [Algebra ℤ_[l] O] [TopologicalSpace O] (Bw : LocBridge l L Ω (Dec (F := L) A) O),
    GlobalTheory.Pst.Induces Bw → IsPotCrysAt (Loc.PP l) Bw r

variable {h} in

def OnArt {C : Type} [Monoid C] (a : Weil h →* (w.adicCompletion L)ˣ) (ψ : Dec (F := L) A →* C)
    (S : Set (w.adicCompletion L)ˣ) (f : (w.adicCompletion L)ˣ → C) : Prop :=
  ∀ (s : Weil h) (g : Dec (F := L) A), (g : G L Ω) = (s : G L Ω) → a s ∈ S → ψ g = f (a s)

variable {h} in

structure OrdChar (a : Weil h →* (w.adicCompletion L)ˣ) (ω : (w.adicCompletion L)ˣ →* ℂˣ) (hh : (L →+* Qbar l) → ℤ)
    (ψ : Dec (F := L) A →* (Qbar l)ˣ) : Prop where
  smooth : IsSmoothChar ω
  cont : Continuous ψ
  val : OnArt a ((Units.coeHom _).comp ψ) Set.univ fun x => ι.symm (ω x) * algPow w (-hh) (x : w.adicCompletion L)

def IsTypeWD (χ : Fin n → Ine (F := L) A →* ℂˣ) (x : WD h) : Prop :=
  ∃ b : WDRep h (Fin n → ℂ), WD.mk b = x ∧ b.N = 0 ∧
    ∀ s : ↥(Ine (F := L) A), b.ρ (ineIncl h s) = Matrix.toLin' (Matrix.diagonal fun i => (χ i s : ℂ))

end Gal

def OrdFlag : Prop :=
  ∀ (L Ω : Type) [Field L] [NumberField L] [Field Ω] [Algebra L Ω] [IsAlgClosure L Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime], l ≠ 2 →
    ∀ (ι : Qbar l ≃+* ℂ) (n : ℕ) (w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 L)), ((l : ℕ) : 𝓞 L) ∈ w.asIdeal →
    ∀ (A : ValuationSubring Ω) (h : IsPlaceOver A w) (a : Weil h →* (w.adicCompletion L)ˣ), IsLocalArtin h a →
    ∀ (r : Dec (F := L) A →* GL (Fin n) (Qbar l)), Continuous r →
    ∀ (hh : Fin n → (L →+* Qbar l) → ℤ), (∀ τ, StrictMono fun j => hh j τ) →
    ∀ (ω : Fin n → (w.adicCompletion L)ˣ →* ℂˣ) (ψ : Fin n → Dec (F := L) A →* (Qbar l)ˣ),
      (∀ j, OrdChar ι a (ω j) (hh j) (ψ j)) → (∀ i j, i ≠ j → ∃ y : Uo (w.adicCompletion L), ω i y ≠ ω j y) →
      DRat ι h r (fun τ => Finset.univ.val.map fun j => hh j τ) (fun s => ∏ j, (X - C (ω j (a s) : ℂ))) →
      W.HasFlag r ψ ∧ PotCrys A r

def FlagWD : Prop :=
  ∀ (L Ω : Type) [Field L] [NumberField L] [Field Ω] [Algebra L Ω] [IsAlgClosure L Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime], l ≠ 2 →
    ∀ (ι : Qbar l ≃+* ℂ) (n : ℕ) (w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 L)), ((l : ℕ) : 𝓞 L) ∈ w.asIdeal →
    ∀ (A : ValuationSubring Ω) (h : IsPlaceOver A w) (a : Weil h →* (w.adicCompletion L)ˣ), IsLocalArtin h a →
    ∀ (r : Dec (F := L) A →* GL (Fin n) (Qbar l)), Continuous r →
    ∀ (hh : Fin n → (L →+* Qbar l) → ℤ) (ω : Fin n → (w.adicCompletion L)ˣ →* ℂˣ)
      (ψ : Fin n → Dec (F := L) A →* (Qbar l)ˣ), (∀ j, OrdChar ι a (ω j) (hh j) (ψ j)) → W.HasFlag r ψ →
      ∀ HT P, DRat ι h r HT P → DRat ι h r (fun τ => Finset.univ.val.map fun j => hh j τ) fun s => ∏ j, (X - C (ω j (a s) : ℂ))

end UpperTheory.V.Hi


[HPT_SRC_LITERATURE] Original local and global foundations retained by HypothesesHprime
